// v401_incremental_cnn.cpp
// v401 / B-159: exact local CNN activation reuse; direct parent v311.
// Experiment v311; direct parent v105_nn_lns.cpp at 886464d2374ad81e937fde84fc25cba771307e2e.
// Reuse exactly the parent's 26,866 policy/value parameters, without training.
// Output-channel SIMD kernels preserve per-output accumulation order. Multiple
// symmetry-generated completed plans compete AFTER short LNS refinement.
// V311_SINGLE_SEED is the preregistered speed-only ablation, not the default.
// All preparation, inference, transformations and LNS share the parent's clock.
// v104の固定165採取時点のNNで完成解を1本作り、v214のLNSと短縮処理へ渡す。
// 学習条件と重みの由来、時間を含む比較条件は notes/experiments/v105.md に記録する。
#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;
#if defined(__GNUC__) && !defined(__clang__)
#pragma GCC push_options
#pragma GCC optimize("O3,unroll-loops")
#endif
namespace nn {
struct Input {
    int N = 0, K = 0;
    vector<string> C;

    void read(istream& in = cin) {
        in >> N >> K;
        C.resize(N);
        for (auto& row : C) in >> row;
    }
};

struct Point {
    int i, j;
};

// 入力から一度だけ作る地形・巣の情報。塔の変化する状態は持たない。
struct Board {
    // d = 0, 1, 2, 3 は U, D, L, R に対応する。
    static constexpr array<int, 4> di{-1, 1, 0, 0};
    static constexpr array<int, 4> dj{0, 0, -1, 1};
    static constexpr uint16_t unreachable_distance = numeric_limits<uint16_t>::max();

    int cell_count = 0;
    // 床だけに行優先で連番を振る。壁と盤外の未使用領域は -1。
    array<array<int16_t, 20>, 20> cell_id;
    vector<Point> coordinates;
    vector<int> nest;             // nest[p]: 巣の色。巣がなければ -1。
    vector<int16_t> nest_pos;      // nest_pos[c]: 色 c の巣の床マスID。

    // ray[p][d][l - 1]: 途中もすべて床なら移動先ID、それ以外は -1。
    // 途中の塔は飛び越えられるため、初期スライムの位置には依存しない。
    // 4近傍の移動先は ray[p][d][0] で取得できる。
    vector<array<array<int16_t, 8>, 4>> ray;

    // floor_dist[p * cell_count + q]: 壁だけを考慮した4近傍最短距離。
    // 塔の高さ・着地容量・帰巣を含まず、実際の最短操作数は表さない。
    vector<uint16_t> floor_dist;

    explicit Board(const Input& input) {
        for (auto& row : cell_id) row.fill(-1);
        coordinates.reserve(input.N * input.N);
        for (int i = 0; i < input.N; ++i) {
            for (int j = 0; j < input.N; ++j) {
                if (input.C[i][j] == '#') continue;
                cell_id[i][j] = static_cast<int16_t>(cell_count++);
                coordinates.push_back({i, j});
            }
        }

        nest.assign(cell_count, -1);
        nest_pos.assign(input.K, -1);
        ray.resize(cell_count);
        for (int p = 0; p < cell_count; ++p) {
            const auto [i, j] = coordinates[p];
            const char ch = input.C[i][j];
            if ('A' <= ch && ch <= 'L') {
                const int c = ch - 'A';
                nest[p] = c;
                nest_pos[c] = static_cast<int16_t>(p);
            }
            for (int d = 0; d < 4; ++d) {
                ray[p][d].fill(-1);
                for (int l = 1; l <= 8; ++l) {
                    const int ni = i + di[d] * l, nj = j + dj[d] * l;
                    if (ni < 0 || ni >= input.N || nj < 0 || nj >= input.N) break;
                    const int q = cell_id[ni][nj];
                    if (q < 0) break;
                    ray[p][d][l - 1] = static_cast<int16_t>(q);
                }
            }
        }
        // 各床を始点にBFSする。全点対でも O(cell_count^2)、最大400^2要素。
        floor_dist.assign(size_t(cell_count) * cell_count, unreachable_distance);
        array<int16_t, 400> queue;
        for (int source = 0; source < cell_count; ++source) {
            // この距離行への読み書きはdistだけを使い、rayとqueueの領域とは重ならない。
            uint16_t* __restrict__ dist = floor_dist.data() + source * cell_count;
            int head = 0, tail = 0;
            dist[source] = 0;
            queue[tail++] = static_cast<int16_t>(source);
            while (head < tail) {
                const int p = queue[head++];
                for (int d = 0; d < 4; ++d) {
                    const int q = ray[p][d][0];
                    if (q < 0 || dist[q] != unreachable_distance) continue;
                    dist[q] = static_cast<uint16_t>(dist[p] + 1);
                    queue[tail++] = static_cast<int16_t>(q);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    int floor_distance(int p, int q) const {
        return floor_dist[p * cell_count + q];
    }
};

// 1塔を32 bitで保持する。下から順に4 bitずつ c + 1 を詰め、
// 未使用の上位部分を0にする。色0も非零になるため、高さを別に保存せず求められる。
// 色・添字・高さは呼び出し側が条件を満たす。top/popは非空、追加後は高さ8以下。
struct Tower {
    uint32_t bits = 0;
    static constexpr array<uint32_t, 9> mask{
        0u, 0xfu, 0xffu, 0xfffu, 0xffffu, 0xfffffu, 0xffffffu, 0xfffffffu, 0xffffffffu};

    bool empty() const { return bits == 0; }
    int height() const { return (bit_width(bits) + 3) / 4; }
    int at(int i) const { return int((bits >> (4 * i)) & 15u) - 1; }
    int top() const { return int(bits >> (4 * (height() - 1))) - 1; }
    int bottom() const { return int(bits & 15u) - 1; }

    // 高さが既知の処理では、h/gを渡すオーバーロードで再計算を省ける。
    void reverse() { reverse(height()); }
    void reverse(int h) {
        const uint32_t swapped = ((bits >> 4) & 0x0f0f0f0fu)
                               | ((bits & 0x0f0f0f0fu) << 4);
        // 4 bit単位の反転。64 bitに拡張し、空塔の32 bitシフトも定義済みにする。
        bits = uint32_t(uint64_t(byteswap(swapped)) >> (4 * (8 - h)));
    }

    // 上側n匹を順序を保って切り出す。0 <= n <= h。
    Tower take_top(int n) { return take_top(n, height()); }
    Tower take_top(int n, int h) {
        const int k = h - n;
        const Tower result{uint32_t(uint64_t(bits) >> (4 * k))};
        bits &= mask[k];
        return result;
    }

    // upperを順序を保って上へ追加する。空塔の追加も扱える。
    void append_top(Tower upper) { append_top(upper, height()); }
    void append_top(Tower upper, int g) {
        bits |= uint32_t(uint64_t(upper.bits) << (4 * g));
    }

    void push_top(int c) { bits |= uint32_t(c + 1) << (4 * height()); }
    void push_bottom(int c) { bits = (bits << 4) | uint32_t(c + 1); }
    void pop_top() { bits &= mask[height() - 1]; }
    void pop_bottom() { bits >>= 4; }

    // 巣の色cと一致する最上段の連続部分を除き、帰巣数を返す。巣なしはc=-1。
    int home(int c) { return home(c, height()); }
    int home(int c, int h) {
        if (c < 0) return 0;
        const uint32_t different = (bits ^ (0x11111111u * uint32_t(c + 1))) & mask[h];
        // 最上位の「巣と異なる段」より下はすべて残るため、一括で切り詰められる。
        const int remaining = (bit_width(different) + 3) / 4;
        bits &= mask[remaining];
        return h - remaining;
    }
};

// 1手を4 byteで保持する。pは床マスID、dは0=U, 1=D, 2=L, 3=R。
// 値域（p:0..399, k:0..7, d:0..3, l:1..8）は呼び出し側が保証する。
// 操作の合法性と盤面の更新は利用側で扱う。
struct Move {
    constexpr Move(int p, int k, int d, int l)
        : p_(uint16_t(p)), k_(uint8_t(k)), dl_(uint8_t((d << 3) | (l - 1))) {}

    constexpr int p() const { return p_; }
    constexpr int k() const { return k_; }
    constexpr int d() const { return dl_ >> 3; }
    constexpr int l() const { return (dl_ & 7) + 1; }

private:
    uint16_t p_;
    uint8_t k_;
    // 下位3 bitがl-1、続く2 bitがd。距離8も3 bitに収める。
    uint8_t dl_;
};

// 探索の各枝が塔を所有する。固定長の未使用部分も0なので盤面の完全一致に使える。
struct BoardState {
    array<uint32_t, 400> bits{};
    int E = 0;
    BoardState() = default;
    BoardState(const Input& input, const Board& board) {
        for (int p = 0; p < board.cell_count; ++p) {
            auto [i, j] = board.coordinates[p];
            const char ch = input.C[i][j];
            if ('a' <= ch && ch <= 'l') { bits[p] = uint32_t(ch - 'a' + 1); ++E; }
        }
    }
    int apply(const Board& board, Move move) {
        const int p = move.p(), k = move.k();
        const int q = board.ray[p][move.d()][move.l() - 1];
        Tower a{bits[p]}, b{bits[q]};
        const int h = a.height(), g = b.height(), n = h - k;
        Tower flying = a.take_top(n, h);
        flying.reverse(n); b.append_top(flying, g);
        const int returned = a.home(board.nest[p], k) + b.home(board.nest[q], g + n);
        bits[p] = a.bits; bits[q] = b.bits; E -= returned;
        return returned;
    }
};

int flat_cell(const Board& board, int p) {
    return board.coordinates[p].i * 20 + board.coordinates[p].j;
}

// 学習側は20×20座標で候補を保存し、C++探索は圧縮した床IDを用いる。
uint32_t action_code(const Board& board, Move move) {
    return uint32_t(flat_cell(board, move.p()) | (move.k() << 9)
                    | (move.d() << 12) | ((move.l() - 1) << 14));
}

vector<Move> legal_moves(const Board& board, const BoardState& state) {
    vector<Move> moves;
    moves.reserve(2048);
    for (int p = 0; p < board.cell_count; ++p) {
        const int h = Tower{state.bits[p]}.height();
        for (int k = 0; k < h; ++k) for (int d = 0; d < 4; ++d) {
            for (int l = 1; l <= k + 1; ++l) {
                const int q = board.ray[p][d][l - 1];
                if (q < 0) break;
                if (Tower{state.bits[q]}.height() + h - k <= 8) moves.emplace_back(p, k, d, l);
            }
        }
    }
    return moves;
}

array<float, 16> action_features(const Board& board, const BoardState& state, Move move) {
    const int p = move.p(), k = move.k(), q = board.ray[p][move.d()][move.l() - 1];
    Tower a{state.bits[p]}, b{state.bits[q]};
    const int h = a.height(), g = b.height(), n = h - k;
    array<float, 16> x{};
    x[0] = k / 8.f; x[1] = move.l() / 8.f; x[2] = n / 8.f; x[3] = g / 8.f;
    x[4 + move.d()] = 1.f;
    x[9] = a.at(k) == board.nest[q];
    x[10] = k > 0 && a.at(k - 1) == board.nest[p];
    float delta = 0.f; bool same = true;
    for (int t = k; t < h; ++t) {
        const int goal = board.nest_pos[a.at(t)];
        delta += board.floor_distance(p, goal) - board.floor_distance(q, goal);
        same &= a.at(t) == a.at(k);
    }
    x[11] = delta / (8.f * n); x[12] = (g + n) / 8.f;
    x[13] = board.nest[p] >= 0; x[14] = board.nest[q] >= 0; x[15] = same;
    Tower flying = a.take_top(n, h); flying.reverse(n); b.append_top(flying, g);
    x[8] = (a.home(board.nest[p], k) + b.home(board.nest[q], g + n)) / 8.f;
    return x;
}

// 床×40、以降の推論でもマス優先。壁・盤外の特徴は常に0。
array<array<float, 40>, 400> board_features(const Input& input, const Board& board, const BoardState& state) {
    array<array<float, 40>, 400> result{};
    for (int p = 0; p < board.cell_count; ++p) {
        const auto [i, j] = board.coordinates[p];
        auto& x = result[i * 20 + j];
        Tower tower{state.bits[p]};
        x[0] = 1.f; x[1] = board.nest[p] >= 0;
        x[2] = i / 20.f; x[3] = j / 20.f; x[4] = tower.height() / 8.f;
        x[5] = state.E / 256.f; x[6] = input.N / 20.f; x[7] = input.K / 12.f;
        for (int t = 0; t < tower.height(); ++t) {
            const int goal = board.nest_pos[tower.at(t)];
            const auto [gi, gj] = board.coordinates[goal];
            x[8 + t * 4] = 1.f;
            x[9 + t * 4] = (gi - i) / 20.f;
            x[10 + t * 4] = (gj - j) / 20.f;
            x[11 + t * 4] = board.floor_distance(p, goal) / 40.f;
        }
    }
    return result;
}

void read_snapshot(istream& in, const Board& board, BoardState& state) {
    state = BoardState{};
    for (int f = 0; f < 400; ++f) {
        uint32_t bits; if (!(in >> bits)) throw runtime_error("short snapshot");
        const int p = board.cell_id[f / 20][f % 20];
        if (p < 0) { if (bits) throw runtime_error("tower on wall"); continue; }
        state.bits[p] = bits; state.E += Tower{bits}.height();
    }
}

// The trained row-major tensors remain the authoritative decoded weights.
// A one-time transpose exposes independent output channels to SIMD, without
// changing the sequence of multiply-adds within any scalar output.
alignas(64) static array<array<float,NN_WIDTH>,40> fast_input;
alignas(64) static array<array<array<float,NN_WIDTH>,9>,NN_DEPTH> fast_depth;
alignas(64) static array<array<array<float,NN_WIDTH>,NN_WIDTH>,NN_DEPTH> fast_point;
alignas(64) static array<array<float,NN_HIDDEN>,NN_WIDTH> fast_source,fast_destination,fast_global;
alignas(64) static array<array<float,NN_HIDDEN>,16> fast_action;
void prepare_fast_weights() {
    for(int c=0;c<40;++c)for(int o=0;o<NN_WIDTH;++o)fast_input[c][o]=nn_input_w[o*40+c];
    for(int b=0;b<NN_DEPTH;++b){
        for(int k=0;k<9;++k)for(int c=0;c<NN_WIDTH;++c)fast_depth[b][k][c]=nn_depth_w[b][c*9+k];
        for(int c=0;c<NN_WIDTH;++c)for(int o=0;o<NN_WIDTH;++o)fast_point[b][c][o]=nn_point_w[b][o*NN_WIDTH+c];
    }
    for(int c=0;c<NN_WIDTH;++c)for(int o=0;o<NN_HIDDEN;++o){
        fast_source[c][o]=nn_actor_w[o*NN_ACTOR_INPUT+c];
        fast_destination[c][o]=nn_actor_w[o*NN_ACTOR_INPUT+NN_WIDTH+c];
        fast_global[c][o]=nn_actor_w[o*NN_ACTOR_INPUT+2*NN_WIDTH+c];
    }
    for(int c=0;c<16;++c)for(int o=0;o<NN_HIDDEN;++o)fast_action[c][o]=nn_actor_w[o*NN_ACTOR_INPUT+3*NN_WIDTH+c];
}
template<size_t Outputs,size_t Inputs>
inline void channel_matvec(const array<array<float,Outputs>,Inputs>& weights,
                           const float* __restrict__ x,float* __restrict__ out) {
    for(size_t c=0;c<Inputs;++c) {
        const float value=x[c];const float* __restrict__ row=weights[c].data();
        // No reduction is parallelized: each output still visits c in order.
        #pragma GCC ivdep
        for(size_t o=0;o<Outputs;++o)out[o]+=row[o]*value;
    }
}

void load_nn() {
    array<int,256> lookup; lookup.fill(-1);
    const string alphabet="ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/";
    for(int i=0;i<64;++i) lookup[uint8_t(alphabet[i])]=i;
    vector<uint8_t> bytes; bytes.reserve(sizeof(nn_packed)*3/4);
    unsigned buffer=0; int bits=0;
    for(char ch:nn_packed) {
        if(ch=='=' || ch==0) break;
        const int digit=lookup[uint8_t(ch)];
        if(digit<0) throw runtime_error("bad model encoding");
        buffer=(buffer<<6)|unsigned(digit); bits+=6;
        if(bits>=8) { bits-=8; bytes.push_back(uint8_t(buffer>>bits)); }
    }
    size_t pos=0;
    auto scalar=[&]() {
        if(pos+4>bytes.size()) throw runtime_error("short model scale");
        uint32_t value=0; for(int b=0;b<4;++b)value|=uint32_t(bytes[pos++])<<(8*b);
        return bit_cast<float>(value);
    };
    auto matrix=[&](float* out,int rows,int columns) {
        for(int row=0;row<rows;++row) {
            const float scale=scalar();
            for(int col=0;col<columns;++col) {
                if(pos==bytes.size())throw runtime_error("short model row");
                const int raw=bytes[pos++];
                *out++=float(raw<128?raw:raw-256)*scale;
            }
        }
    };
    auto bias=[&](float* out,int count){for(int i=0;i<count;++i)*out++=scalar();};
    matrix(nn_input_w.data(),48,40);
    bias(nn_input_b.data(),48);
    matrix(nn_depth_w[0].data(),48,9);
    bias(nn_depth_b[0].data(),48);
    matrix(nn_point_w[0].data(),48,48);
    bias(nn_point_b[0].data(),48);
    matrix(nn_depth_w[1].data(),48,9);
    bias(nn_depth_b[1].data(),48);
    matrix(nn_point_w[1].data(),48,48);
    bias(nn_point_b[1].data(),48);
    matrix(nn_depth_w[2].data(),48,9);
    bias(nn_depth_b[2].data(),48);
    matrix(nn_point_w[2].data(),48,48);
    bias(nn_point_b[2].data(),48);
    matrix(nn_depth_w[3].data(),48,9);
    bias(nn_depth_b[3].data(),48);
    matrix(nn_point_w[3].data(),48,48);
    bias(nn_point_b[3].data(),48);
    matrix(nn_actor_w.data(),64,160);
    bias(nn_actor_b.data(),64);
    matrix(nn_actor_out_w.data(),1,64);
    bias(nn_actor_out_b.data(),1);
    matrix(nn_critic_w.data(),64,48);
    bias(nn_critic_b.data(),64);
    matrix(nn_critic_out_w.data(),1,64);
    bias(nn_critic_out_b.data(),1);
    if(pos!=bytes.size() || pos!=30800)throw runtime_error("trailing model data");
    prepare_fast_weights();
}
// 重みはこの直前に生成する。Pythonと同じ演算順で可変容量CNNを直接計算する。
struct Encoded {
    array<array<float, NN_WIDTH>, 400> cells{};
    array<float, NN_WIDTH> global{};
    float remaining = 0.f;
};

// The cache belongs to one board/search. Global E is an input at every floor:
// any homecoming MUST invalidate every layer, not just the two moved towers.
struct EncodeCache {
    array<array<array<float,NN_WIDTH>,400>,NN_DEPTH+1> layers{};
    array<uint32_t,400> bits{};
    int E=-1;
    bool valid=false;
    void invalidate() { valid=false; }
};
#ifdef LOCAL
struct IncrementalStats {
    uint64_t calls=0,full=0,home=0,incremental=0;
    array<uint64_t,NN_DEPTH+1> cells{};
    uint64_t full_cells=0;
} incremental_stats;
#endif
unique_ptr<Encoded> encode(const Input& input,const Board& board,const BoardState& state,
                           EncodeCache* retained=nullptr) {
    // The diagnostic one-shot API uses a fresh cache; production owns one per search.
    unique_ptr<EncodeCache> fresh;
    if(!retained){fresh=make_unique<EncodeCache>();retained=fresh.get();}
    auto& cache=*retained;
    const bool full=!cache.valid || cache.E!=state.E;
    array<bool,400> changed{};
    for(int p=0;p<board.cell_count;++p)changed[p]=full || cache.bits[p]!=state.bits[p];
#ifdef LOCAL
    ++incremental_stats.calls;
    incremental_stats.full+=full;
    incremental_stats.home+=cache.valid && cache.E!=state.E;
    incremental_stats.incremental+=!full;
    incremental_stats.full_cells+=board.cell_count;
#endif
    auto result=make_unique<Encoded>();
    const auto features=board_features(input,board,state);
    auto& first=cache.layers[0];
    for(int p=0;p<board.cell_count;++p)if(changed[p]) {
        first[p]=nn_input_b;
        channel_matvec(fast_input,features[flat_cell(board,p)].data(),first[p].data());
        for(float& v:first[p])v=max(0.f,v);
#ifdef LOCAL
        ++incremental_stats.cells[0];
#endif
    }
    for(int block=0;block<NN_DEPTH;++block) {
        const auto& h=cache.layers[block];
        auto& output=cache.layers[block+1];
        array<bool,400> affected{};
        // Propagate the exact graph dependency including the residual/self edge.
        for(int p=0;p<board.cell_count;++p) {
            const auto [i,j]=board.coordinates[p];
            affected[p]=changed[p];
            for(int di=-1;di<=1;++di)for(int dj=-1;dj<=1;++dj) {
                const int ni=i+di,nj=j+dj;
                if(ni<0||ni>=input.N||nj<0||nj>=input.N)continue;
                const int q=board.cell_id[ni][nj];
                if(q>=0 && changed[q])affected[p]=true;
            }
        }
        array<array<float,NN_WIDTH>,400> depth{};
        for(int p=0;p<board.cell_count;++p)if(affected[p]) {
            const auto [i,j]=board.coordinates[p];depth[p]=nn_depth_b[block];
            for(int di=-1;di<=1;++di)for(int dj=-1;dj<=1;++dj) {
                const int ni=i+di,nj=j+dj;
                if(ni<0||ni>=input.N||nj<0||nj>=input.N)continue;
                const int q=board.cell_id[ni][nj];if(q<0)continue;
                const auto& weights=fast_depth[block][(di+1)*3+dj+1];
                for(int c=0;c<NN_WIDTH;++c)depth[p][c]+=weights[c]*h[q][c];
            }
            for(float& v:depth[p])v=max(0.f,v);
        }
        for(int p=0;p<board.cell_count;++p)if(affected[p]) {
            auto residual=nn_point_b[block];
            channel_matvec(fast_point[block],depth[p].data(),residual.data());
            for(int o=0;o<NN_WIDTH;++o)output[p][o]=max(0.f,h[p][o]+residual[o]);
#ifdef LOCAL
            ++incremental_stats.cells[block+1];
#endif
        }
        changed=affected;
    }
    cache.bits=state.bits;cache.E=state.E;cache.valid=true;
    result->cells=cache.layers[NN_DEPTH];
    const auto& h=result->cells;
    // Never subtract/add changed cells into the global sum: preserve parent order.
    for(int p=0;p<board.cell_count;++p)
        for(int c=0;c<NN_WIDTH;++c)result->global[c]+=h[p][c];
    for(float& v:result->global)v/=board.cell_count;
    float raw=nn_critic_out_b[0];
    for(int o=0;o<NN_HIDDEN;++o) {
        float v=nn_critic_b[o];
        for(int c=0;c<NN_WIDTH;++c)v+=nn_critic_w[o*NN_WIDTH+c]*result->global[c];
        raw+=nn_critic_out_w[o]*max(0.f,v);
    }
    result->remaining=(raw>20.f?raw:log1p(exp(raw)))*100.f;
    return result;
}

vector<float> policy(const Board& board,const BoardState& state,const Encoded& encoded,
                     const vector<Move>& moves) {
    array<array<float,NN_HIDDEN>,400> source{},dest{};
    auto global=nn_actor_b;
    channel_matvec(fast_global,encoded.global.data(),global.data());
    array<bool,400> used_source{},used_dest{};
    for(Move m:moves){used_source[m.p()]=true;used_dest[board.ray[m.p()][m.d()][m.l()-1]]=true;}
    // Empty/non-candidate cells cannot contribute to a returned logit.
    for(int p=0;p<board.cell_count;++p) {
        if(used_source[p])channel_matvec(fast_source,encoded.cells[p].data(),source[p].data());
        if(used_dest[p])channel_matvec(fast_destination,encoded.cells[p].data(),dest[p].data());
    }
    vector<float> logits;logits.reserve(moves.size());
    for(Move m:moves) {
        const int p=m.p(),q=board.ray[p][m.d()][m.l()-1];
        const auto features=action_features(board,state,m);
        array<float,NN_HIDDEN> action{};
        channel_matvec(fast_action,features.data(),action.data());
        float logit=nn_actor_out_b[0];
        for(int o=0;o<NN_HIDDEN;++o) {
            const float v=source[p][o]+dest[q][o]+global[o]+action[o];
            logit+=nn_actor_out_w[o]*max(0.f,v);
        }
        logits.push_back(logit);
    }
    return logits;
}
struct StateHash {
    size_t operator()(const array<uint32_t,400>& state) const {
        uint64_t h=0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
        for(uint32_t v:state)h^=uint64_t(v)+0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL+(h<<6)+(h>>2);
        return size_t(h);
    }
};

struct SearchResult {
    vector<Move> path;
    int E=0, depth=0, inferences=0, duplicates=0;
    bool deadline=false, exhausted=false;
};

template<class Timer>
SearchResult search(const Input& input,const Board& board,const BoardState& initial,Timer& timer) {
    SearchResult result; result.E=initial.E;
    BoardState state=initial; vector<Move> path;
    auto encode_cache=make_unique<EncodeCache>();
    int64_t best_score=int64_t(initial.E)*100000;
    uint64_t rng=90003;
    for(const auto& row:input.C)for(char ch:row)rng=(rng^uint8_t(ch))*1099511628211ULL;
    if(!rng)rng=90003;
    auto uniform=[&](){rng^=rng<<13;rng^=rng>>7;rng^=rng<<17;return double(rng>>11)*0x1p-53;};
    unordered_set<array<uint32_t,400>,StateHash> visited;
#ifndef V090_STOCHASTIC
    visited.reserve(4096); visited.insert(state.bits);
#endif
    for(int depth=0;depth<2048 && state.E>0;++depth) {
        if(!timer.step()){result.deadline=true;break;}
        auto moves=legal_moves(board,state);
        if(moves.empty()){result.exhausted=true;break;}
        const auto encoded=encode(input,board,state,encode_cache.get()); ++result.inferences;
        const auto logits=policy(board,state,*encoded,moves);
        if(!timer.step()){result.deadline=true;break;}
        int chosen=-1; BoardState next;
#ifdef V090_STOCHASTIC
        const float maximum=*max_element(logits.begin(),logits.end());
        double total=0;
        for(float value:logits)total+=exp(double(value-maximum));
        const double pick=uniform()*total; double cumulative=0;
        for(size_t i=0;i<moves.size();++i){
            cumulative+=exp(double(logits[i]-maximum));
            if(pick<cumulative || i+1==moves.size()){chosen=int(i);break;}
        }
        next=state; next.apply(board,moves[chosen]);
#else
        (void)uniform;
        vector<int> order(moves.size());iota(order.begin(),order.end(),0);
        stable_sort(order.begin(),order.end(),[&](int a,int b){return logits[a]>logits[b];});
        for(int index:order){
            next=state;next.apply(board,moves[index]);
            if(visited.contains(next.bits)){++result.duplicates;continue;}
            chosen=index;visited.insert(next.bits);break;
        }
        if(chosen<0){result.exhausted=true;break;}
#endif
        path.push_back(moves[chosen]);state=next;result.depth=int(path.size());
        const int64_t score=int64_t(state.E)*100000+path.size();
        if(score<best_score){best_score=score;result.E=state.E;result.path=path;}
    }
    return result;
}

#ifdef V090_DIAGNOSTIC
void dump_inference(const Input& input,const Board& board,const BoardState& state) {
    const auto started=chrono::steady_clock::now();
    const auto features=board_features(input,board,state);const auto moves=legal_moves(board,state);
    const auto encoded=encode(input,board,state);const auto logits=policy(board,state,*encoded,moves);
    const double elapsed=chrono::duration<double>(chrono::steady_clock::now()-started).count();
    cout<<setprecision(9)<<"{\"inference_seconds\":"<<elapsed<<",\"value\":"<<encoded->remaining<<",\"x\":[";
    for(int f=0;f<400;++f)for(int c=0;c<40;++c){if(f||c)cout<<',';cout<<features[f][c];}
    cout<<"],\"codes\":[";
    for(size_t n=0;n<moves.size();++n){if(n)cout<<',';cout<<action_code(board,moves[n]);}
    cout<<"],\"features\":[";
    for(size_t n=0;n<moves.size();++n){const auto x=action_features(board,state,moves[n]);
        for(int c=0;c<16;++c){if(n||c)cout<<',';cout<<x[c];}}
    cout<<"],\"logits\":[";
    for(size_t n=0;n<logits.size();++n){if(n)cout<<',';cout<<logits[n];}
    cout<<"]}\n";
}
#endif

#ifdef LOCAL
constexpr double JUDGE_TIME_LIMIT_SEC = 1.90;
constexpr double LOCAL_TIME_RATIO = 1.90 / 1.930; // Match the fixed cloud v311 LOCAL budget only.
constexpr double PROGRAM_TIME_LIMIT_SEC = JUDGE_TIME_LIMIT_SEC * LOCAL_TIME_RATIO;
struct TraceStats {
    map<string, int64_t> counts;
    map<string, double> times_ms;
    void count_by(const string& key, int64_t delta) { counts[key] += delta; }
    void add_time_ms(const string& key, double ms) { times_ms[key] += ms; }
    void summary() const {
        for (const auto& [key, value] : counts)
            cerr << "[summary.count] " << key << '=' << value << '\n';
        const auto flags = cerr.flags();
        const auto precision = cerr.precision();
        cerr << fixed << setprecision(3);
        for (const auto& [key, value] : times_ms)
            cerr << "[summary.time_ms] " << key << '=' << value << '\n';
        cerr.flags(flags); cerr.precision(precision);
    }
};
template<class F>
decltype(auto) local_time_impl(TraceStats& trace, const string& key, F&& body) {
    const auto start = chrono::steady_clock::now();
    if constexpr (is_void_v<invoke_result_t<F>>) {
        invoke(forward<F>(body));
        trace.add_time_ms(key, chrono::duration<double, milli>(chrono::steady_clock::now() - start).count());
    } else {
        auto result = invoke(forward<F>(body));
        trace.add_time_ms(key, chrono::duration<double, milli>(chrono::steady_clock::now() - start).count());
        return result;
    }
}
#define LOCAL_NOTE(...) __VA_ARGS__
#define LOCAL_ONLY(...) do { __VA_ARGS__; } while (false)
#define LOCAL_TIME(trace, key, ...) local_time_impl((trace), (key), (__VA_ARGS__))
// 既存版と同じLOCAL時間配分で比較する。非LOCALは持ち込み版の式を保持する。
#define LOCAL_SECONDS(value) (PROGRAM_TIME_LIMIT_SEC * ((value) / JUDGE_TIME_LIMIT_SEC))
#else
constexpr double JUDGE_TIME_LIMIT_SEC = 1.90;
constexpr double LOCAL_TIME_RATIO = 1.0;
constexpr double PROGRAM_TIME_LIMIT_SEC = JUDGE_TIME_LIMIT_SEC;
#define LOCAL_NOTE(...)
#define LOCAL_ONLY(...) do { } while (false)
#define LOCAL_TIME(trace, key, ...) (__VA_ARGS__)()
#define LOCAL_SECONDS(value) (PROGRAM_TIME_LIMIT_SEC * ((value) / 1.930) * (1.880 / 1.900))
#endif

#ifdef LOCAL

struct TwoOrderStats {

    explicit DependencyTimer(bool enabled):active(enabled) {
        if(active)begin=chrono::steady_clock::now();
    }

        if(active)local_dependency.seconds+=chrono::duration<double>(chrono::steady_clock::now()-begin).count();
    }
};
