// 塔は下から4 bitずつ色c+1を詰める。空は0。色符号は1..K、巣なしは0。
using TowerBits = uint32_t;
constexpr int max_cells = 400, infinite_cost = 30000;
// 盤面は床数分の領域を借りる。2塔の再構築3枚と再挿入1枚で最大4枚を同時に使う。
struct StatePool {
    static constexpr int slots = 4;
    unique_ptr<TowerBits[]> storage;
    TowerBits* free_slot[slots];
    size_t bytes = 0;
    int free_count = 0;
    void prepare(int floors) {
        storage.reset(new TowerBits[size_t(floors) * slots]);
        bytes = size_t(floors) * sizeof(TowerBits);
        free_count = slots;
        for(int i=0;i<slots;i++)free_slot[i]=storage.get()+size_t(i)*floors;
    }
    TowerBits* take() { return free_slot[--free_count]; }
    void give(TowerBits* cell) { free_slot[free_count++]=cell; }
} state_pool;

class State {
    TowerBits* cell;
public:
    // 引数なしでは領域だけ取得する。利用前にIdentityState::wordsで全床を書き込む。
    State():cell(state_pool.take()) {}
    State(const array<TowerBits,max_cells>& other):State() {
        memcpy(cell,other.data(),state_pool.bytes);
    }
    State(const State& other):State() {
        memcpy(cell,other.cell,state_pool.bytes);
    }
    State(State&& other) noexcept:cell(exchange(other.cell,nullptr)) {}
    ~State() { if(cell)state_pool.give(cell); }
    State& operator=(const State& other) {
        if(this!=&other) {
            if(!cell)cell=state_pool.take();
            memcpy(cell,other.cell,state_pool.bytes);
        }
        return *this;
    }
    State& operator=(const array<TowerBits,max_cells>& other) {
        if(!cell)cell=state_pool.take();
        memcpy(cell,other.data(),state_pool.bytes);
        return *this;
    }
    State& operator=(State&& other) noexcept {
        swap(cell,other.cell);
        return *this;
    }
    TowerBits& operator[](int p) { return cell[p]; }
    const TowerBits& operator[](int p) const { return cell[p]; }
    bool operator==(const State& other) const {
        return memcmp(cell,other.cell,state_pool.bytes)==0;
    }
};

struct Deadline {};
// 非LOCALは起動時、LOCALはmain先頭から計時する。各照会で時計を読む。
struct TimeKeeper {
    double time_limit_sec = LOCAL_SECONDS(1.89);
#ifdef LOCAL
    chrono::steady_clock::time_point start_;
#else
    chrono::steady_clock::time_point start_ = chrono::steady_clock::now();
#endif
    double exact_elapsed_sec() const {
        return chrono::duration<double>(chrono::steady_clock::now()-start_).count();
    }
    void check() const { if (exact_elapsed_sec() > time_limit_sec) throw Deadline(); }
} time_keeper;
struct Rng {
    uint64_t x = 0x123456789abcdefULL;
    uint64_t next() { x ^= x<<7; x ^= x>>9; return x; }
    // 64 bit積の上位を使い、整数除算なしで[0,n)へ写す。
    int operator()(int n) { return int((__uint128_t(next())*uint64_t(n))>>64); }
    double unit() { return (next()>>11)*0x1.0p-53; }
} rng;

constexpr TowerBits tower_mask[9]={0u,0xfu,0xffu,0xfffu,0xffffu,0xfffffu,0xffffffu,0xfffffffu,0xffffffffu};
inline int height(TowerBits a) { return a ? (32-__builtin_clz(a)+3)/4 : 0; }
inline TowerBits prefix(TowerBits a,int k) { return a & tower_mask[k]; }
inline TowerBits reverse_tower(TowerBits a) {
    if(!a)return 0;
    int shift=(8-height(a))*4;
    a=((a>>4)&0x0f0f0f0fu)|((a&0x0f0f0f0fu)<<4);
    return __builtin_bswap32(a)>>shift;
}
inline TowerBits append_tower(TowerBits lower,TowerBits upper) {
    int h=height(lower);
    if(h==8) {if(upper)throw logic_error("tower overflow");return lower;}
    return lower|(upper<<(4*h));
}
inline int top_color_code(TowerBits a) {return a ? a>>(4*(height(a)-1)) : 0;}
inline int mono_color_code(TowerBits a) {
    if(!a)return 0;
    int current_code=a&15;
    return ((a^(0x11111111u*current_code))&tower_mask[height(a)]) ? -1 : current_code;
}

// 単位費用の探索用。費用別の連結リストを使い、古い候補の検査は呼び出し側で行う。
class IntegerQueue {
    struct Node {int id,next;};
    vector<int> head;
    vector<Node> node;
    int count=0,lowest=infinite_cost;
public:
    IntegerQueue():head(128,-1) {node.reserve(1600);}
    bool empty()const{return count==0;}
    void emplace(int cost,int id) {
        if(cost>=int(head.size()))head.resize(cost+128,-1);
        node.push_back({id,head[cost]});head[cost]=int(node.size())-1;
        lowest=min(lowest,cost);++count;
    }
    pair<int,int> top() {
        while(head[lowest]<0)++lowest;
        return {lowest,node[head[lowest]].id};
    }
    void pop(){head[lowest]=node[head[lowest]].next;--count;}
};

inline int color_mask(TowerBits a) {
    int m=0;
    for(;a;a>>=4) m|=1<<((a&15)-1);
    return m;
}
inline int runs(TowerBits a) {
    int r=0, last=-1;
    for(;a;a>>=4) { int current_code=a&15; r+=current_code!=last; last=current_code; }
    return r;
}

// pは床ID、dはU/D/L/Rの0..3。地形のrayは飛距離lをそのまま添字にする。
struct Move { int16_t p; uint8_t k,d,l; };
// 地形と初期入力。nest_codeは色符号、nest_pos_by_codeは符号から巣への逆引き。
struct Board {
    int N,K,cell_count=0,M=0;
    string C[20];
    int cell_id[20][20], row[max_cells], col[max_cells], parity[max_cells], nest_code[max_cells], nest_pos_by_code[13];
    int16_t adj[max_cells][4], ray[max_cells][4][9];
    uint8_t ray_count[max_cells][4]{};
    uint16_t floor_dist[max_cells][max_cells];
    array<TowerBits,max_cells> initial{};
    const int di[4]={-1,1,0,0}, dj[4]={0,0,-1,1};
    const char dirs[5]="UDLR";
    void read() {
        cin>>N>>K;
        memset(cell_id,-1,sizeof(cell_id)); memset(nest_pos_by_code,-1,sizeof(nest_pos_by_code));
        for(int i=0;i<N;i++) cin>>C[i];
        for(int i=0;i<N;i++) for(int j=0;j<N;j++) if(C[i][j]!='#') {
            int v=cell_id[i][j]=cell_count++; row[v]=i; col[v]=j; parity[v]=(i+j)&1;
            char input_char=C[i][j]; nest_code[v]=0;
            if(input_char>='A'&&input_char<='L') { nest_code[v]=input_char-'A'+1; nest_pos_by_code[nest_code[v]]=v; }
            if(input_char>='a'&&input_char<='l') { initial[v]=input_char-'a'+1; M++; }
        }
        for(int v=0;v<cell_count;v++) for(int d=0;d<4;d++) {
            fill(ray[v][d],ray[v][d]+9,-1);
            for(int l=1;l<=8;l++) {
                int i=row[v]+di[d]*l,j=col[v]+dj[d]*l;
                if(i<0||j<0||i>=N||j>=N||cell_id[i][j]<0) break;
                ray[v][d][l]=cell_id[i][j];ray_count[v][d]=uint8_t(l);
            }
            adj[v][d]=ray[v][d][1];
        }
        for(int s=0;s<cell_count;s++) {
            fill(floor_dist[s],floor_dist[s]+cell_count,uint16_t(infinite_cost));
            int q[max_cells],a=0,b=0; q[b++]=s; floor_dist[s][s]=0;
            while(a<b) {
                int v=q[a++];
                for(int d=0;d<4;d++) {
                    int w=adj[v][d];
                    if(w>=0&&floor_dist[s][w]==infinite_cost) { floor_dist[s][w]=floor_dist[s][v]+1; q[b++]=w; }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    TowerBits normalize(TowerBits a,int p) const {
        int code=nest_code[p];
        if(!code||!a)return a;
        TowerBits different=(a^(0x11111111u*code))&tower_mask[height(a)];
        return prefix(a,height(different));
    }
    // 帰巣する前の着地容量を含め、操作の合法性を検査する。
    int apply(State &b,Move m) const {
        int p=m.p;
        if(p<0||p>=cell_count)throw logic_error("invalid departure cell");
        int h=height(b[p]);
        if(m.k>=h||m.l<1||m.l>8||m.l>m.k+1||m.d>=4)
            throw logic_error("invalid departure");
        int q=ray[p][m.d][m.l];
        if(q<0) throw logic_error("wall or boundary");
        int g=height(b[q]);
        if(g+h-m.k>8) throw logic_error("landing capacity");
        TowerBits flight=reverse_tower(b[p]>>(4*m.k));
        TowerBits left=prefix(b[p],m.k), land=append_tower(b[q],flight);
        b[p]=normalize(left,p); b[q]=normalize(land,q);
        return h+g-height(b[p])-height(b[q]);
    }
    int count(const State &b) const {
        int ans=0; for(int p=0;p<cell_count;p++) ans+=height(b[p]); return ans;
    }
} board_info;

// 未使用500件の5分割比較で決めた入力条件。NNの重みと探索時間は親から保持する。
// 特徴の順序: floors。最終200件の結果による条件調整はしない。
static array<double,1> nn_selector_features() {
    [[maybe_unused]] const Board &b=board_info;
    return {double(b.cell_count)};
}
static bool nn_selector_choice([[maybe_unused]] const array<double,1> &f) {
    return (f[0]<=192.5 ? false : true);
}

class PortionRouter {
    int bg[max_cells], source, keep, amount;
    TowerBits packet;
    int16_t ds[2*max_cells], parent[2*max_cells];
    uint8_t dir[2*max_cells], len[2*max_cells];
public:
    void run(const State& b,int s,int k,int goal,int max_length=infinite_cost) {
        source=s;keep=k;packet=b[s]>>(4*k);amount=height(packet);
        for(int p=0;p<board_info.cell_count;p++) bg[p]=height(b[p]);
        bg[s]=height(board_info.normalize(prefix(b[s],k),s));
        fill(ds,ds+2*board_info.cell_count,int16_t(-1));
        int que[2*max_cells],head=0,tail=0;
        ds[s*2]=0;parent[s*2]=-1;que[tail++]=s*2;
        int top[2]={top_color_code(packet),int(packet&15)};
        while(head<tail) {
            int z=que[head++],p=z/2,o=z&1;
            if(ds[z]>=max_length)continue;
            if(ds[goal*2]>=0&&ds[goal*2+1]>=0)break;
            if(z!=s*2&&board_info.nest_code[p]==top[o]) continue;
            int reach=(z==s*2?keep:bg[p])+1;
            for(int d=0;d<4;d++) for(int l=1;l<=min(8,reach);l++) {
                int q=board_info.ray[p][d][l];if(q<0)break;
                if(bg[q]+amount>8)continue;
                int no=o^1,nz=q*2+no;
                if(q!=goal&&board_info.nest_code[q]==top[no])continue;
                if(ds[nz]>=0)continue;
                ds[nz]=ds[z]+1;parent[nz]=z;dir[nz]=d;len[nz]=l;
                que[tail++]=nz;
            }
        }
    }
    int cost(int goal,int o) const {return ds[goal*2+o];}
    vector<Move> path(int goal,int o) const {
        vector<Move> a;
        int z=goal*2+o;if(ds[z]<0)return a;
        while(parent[z]>=0) {
            int v=parent[z],p=v/2;
            a.push_back({int16_t(p),uint8_t(v==source*2?keep:bg[p]),dir[z],len[z]});
            z=v;
        }
        reverse(a.begin(),a.end());return a;
    }
};

// 連続した輸送を、終了時の全盤面が等しい短い経路へ置き換える。
vector<Move> smooth_routes(const vector<Move>& answer) {
    State b=board_info.initial;vector<Move> out;out.reserve(answer.size());
    for(int i=0;i<int(answer.size());) {
        if((i&31)==0)time_keeper.check();
        Move first=answer[i];
        int p=first.p,k=first.k,h=height(b[p])-k;
        TowerBits word=b[p]>>(4*k);State end=b;
        int q=p,j=i;TowerBits traveling=word;
        for(;j<int(answer.size())&&j<i+64;j++) {
            Move m=answer[j];
            if(m.p!=q||height(end[q])-m.k!=h||end[q]>>(4*m.k)!=traveling)break;
            int dest=board_info.ray[q][m.d][m.l];
            board_info.apply(end,m);q=dest;traveling=reverse_tower(traveling);
            if(board_info.normalize(traveling,q)!=traveling){j++;break;}
        }
        if(j==i) { // Defensive only: the first operation necessarily qualifies.
            board_info.apply(b,first);out.push_back(first);i++;continue;
        }
        vector<Move> replacement;
        int best_length_value=j-i;
        if(best_length_value>=2) {
            PortionRouter router;router.run(b,p,k,q,best_length_value-1);
            for(int o=0;o<2;o++) {
                int d=router.cost(q,o);if(d<0||d>=best_length_value)continue;
                auto path=router.path(q,o);State check=b;bool ok=true;
                try {for(auto m:path)board_info.apply(check,m);}catch(const exception&){ok=false;}
                if(ok&&check==end){best_length_value=d;replacement=move(path);}
            }
        }
        if(best_length_value<j-i)out.insert(out.end(),replacement.begin(),replacement.end());
        else out.insert(out.end(),answer.begin()+i,answer.begin()+j);
        b=end;i=j;
    }
    if(board_info.count(b))throw logic_error("smoothing incomplete");
    return out;
}

// 帰巣のない2マス間の転送では、一方を下から、他方を上から連結した列が不変。
// 両端へ触れない操作をまたいで正味の転送をまとめ、最初の転送位置へ置く。
vector<Move> compress_pair_transfers(const vector<Move>& answer) {
    struct Entry {
        Move op;
        int p,q,previous_p,previous_q,last_input,last_amount;
        TowerBits before_p,before_q;
        bool active,no_return;
    };
    vector<Entry> entries;entries.reserve(answer.size());
    array<int,max_cells> last_touch;last_touch.fill(-1);
    State board=board_info.initial;
    LOCAL_NOTE(int64_t merges=0,cancellations=0,changed_amount=0,separated=0,long_merges=0;)
    for(int i=0;i<int(answer.size());i++) {
        const Move m=answer[i];
        // 着地点を使う前に、元の操作の合法性を検査する。
        const State before=board;
        const int returned=board_info.apply(board,m);
        const int p=m.p,q=board_info.ray[p][m.d][m.l];
        const int amount=height(before[p])-m.k;
        const int previous=last_touch[p];
        if(!returned&&previous>=0&&previous==last_touch[q]&&entries[previous].no_return) {
            Entry& entry=entries[previous];
            State check=board;
            check[entry.p]=entry.before_p;check[entry.q]=entry.before_q;
            const int delta=height(entry.before_p)-height(board[entry.p]);
            if(delta) {
                const int source=delta>0?entry.p:entry.q;
                // 元の最後の出発で下段がl-1匹以上残るため、正味の向きが反転しても直接転送できる。
                const Move replacement{int16_t(source),uint8_t(height(board[source])),
                                       uint8_t(m.d^(source==p?0:1)),m.l};
                board_info.apply(check,replacement);
                entry.op=replacement;
            } else {
                entry.active=false;
                // 相殺された往復より前の最終接触を戻し、さらに前の転送とも相殺できるようにする。
                last_touch[entry.p]=entry.previous_p;
                last_touch[entry.q]=entry.previous_q;
            }
            // 不変条件の破れは、入力をそのまま返して隠さず例外にする。
            if(check!=board)throw logic_error("pair transfer board mismatch");
            LOCAL_NOTE(
                ++merges;cancellations+=delta==0;
                changed_amount+=entry.last_amount!=amount;
                separated+=i>entry.last_input+1;long_merges+=m.l>1;
            )
            entry.last_input=i;entry.last_amount=amount;
        } else {
            entries.push_back({m,p,q,last_touch[p],last_touch[q],i,amount,
                               before[p],before[q],true,returned==0});
            last_touch[p]=last_touch[q]=int(entries.size())-1;
        }
    }
    vector<Move> out;out.reserve(entries.size());
    for(const Entry& entry:entries)if(entry.active)out.push_back(entry.op);
    LOCAL_NOTE(
        trace.count_by("pair_transfer_merges",merges);
        trace.count_by("pair_transfer_cancellations",cancellations);
        trace.count_by("pair_transfer_changed_amount_merges",changed_amount);
        trace.count_by("pair_transfer_separated_merges",separated);
        trace.count_by("pair_transfer_long_merges",long_merges);
        trace.count_by("pair_transfer_saved",int64_t(answer.size())-int64_t(out.size()));
    )
    return out;
}

// 区間の入口と出口だけを固定し、途中の分割・合流を探索する。
// 各塔は8匹まで。参加総数の増加は状態数上限と期限で抑える。
using JointState = array<TowerBits,4>;
struct JointWindowStats {
    int64_t candidates=0,searches=0,expanded=0,state_caps=0,deadlines=0;
    int64_t replacements=0,saved=0,independent=0,changed_first_split=0;
    int64_t large_candidates=0,large_searches=0,large_replacements=0,large_saved=0;
    void summary() const {
        LOCAL_NOTE(
            trace.count_by("joint_window_candidates",candidates);
            trace.count_by("joint_window_searches",searches);
            trace.count_by("joint_window_expanded",expanded);
            trace.count_by("joint_window_state_caps",state_caps);
            trace.count_by("joint_window_deadlines",deadlines);
            trace.count_by("joint_window_replacements",replacements);
            trace.count_by("joint_window_saved",saved);
            trace.count_by("joint_window_independent",independent);
            trace.count_by("joint_window_changed_first_split",changed_first_split);
            trace.count_by("joint_window_large_candidates",large_candidates);
            trace.count_by("joint_window_large_searches",large_searches);
            trace.count_by("joint_window_large_replacements",large_replacements);
            trace.count_by("joint_window_large_saved",large_saved);
        )
    }
};
struct JointStateHash {
    size_t operator()(const JointState& s) const {
        uint64_t h=0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
        for(TowerBits w:s)h=(h^w)*0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
        return h^(h>>32);
    }
};
class JointWindowRouter {
    struct Edge {int a,b,d,l;};
    struct Node {JointState state;int parent;Move move;};
    vector<Edge> edges;
    array<int,4> cells;
    array<vector<Node>,2> nodes;
    array<unordered_map<JointState,int,JointStateHash>,2> visited;
public:
    static constexpr int state_cap=16384;
    JointWindowRouter() {
        for(int side=0;side<2;side++) {
            nodes[side].reserve(state_cap);
            visited[side].reserve(state_cap);
        }
    }
    void prepare(const array<int,4>& positions,int count) {
        cells=positions;edges.clear();
        for(int a=0;a<count;a++)for(int b=0;b<count;b++)if(a!=b)
            for(int d=0;d<4;d++)for(int l=1;l<=8;l++)
                if(board_info.ray[cells[a]][d][l]==cells[b])edges.push_back({a,b,d,l});
    }
    template<class Emit>
    bool transitions(const JointState& s,bool backward,Emit emit) const {
        for(auto [a,b,d,l]:edges) {
            int ha=height(s[a]),hb=height(s[b]);
            if(!backward) {
                for(int k=max(l-1,ha+hb-8);k<ha;k++) {
                    JointState next=s;
                    next[a]=prefix(s[a],k);
                    next[b]=append_tower(s[b],reverse_tower(s[a]>>(4*k)));
                    if(!emit(next,Move{int16_t(cells[a]),uint8_t(k),uint8_t(d),uint8_t(l)}))return false;
                }
            } else if(ha>=l-1) {
                // 逆探索では順方向に合法な出発の直前を列挙する。物理的な逆ジャンプの支持は要求しない。
                for(int amount=1;amount<=min(hb,8-ha);amount++) {
                    JointState previous=s;
                    previous[a]=append_tower(s[a],reverse_tower(s[b]>>(4*(hb-amount))));
                    previous[b]=prefix(s[b],hb-amount);
                    if(!emit(previous,Move{int16_t(cells[a]),uint8_t(ha),uint8_t(d),uint8_t(l)}))return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
    bool search(const JointState& begin,const JointState& end,int max_moves,
                double deadline,JointWindowStats& stats,vector<Move>& result) {
        ++stats.searches;result.clear();
        if(begin==end)return true;
        for(int side=0;side<2;side++){nodes[side].clear();visited[side].clear();}
        nodes[0].push_back({begin,-1,{}});visited[0].emplace(begin,0);
        nodes[1].push_back({end,-1,{}});visited[1].emplace(end,0);
        array<vector<int>,2> frontier;frontier[0]={0};frontier[1]={0};
        int depth[2]={0,0},meeting[2]={-1,-1};
        while(depth[0]+depth[1]<max_moves&&!frontier[0].empty()&&!frontier[1].empty()) {
            int side=frontier[0].size()<=frontier[1].size()?0:1;
            vector<int> next;
            for(int parent:frontier[side]) {
                if((stats.expanded&127)==0&&time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()>=deadline){++stats.deadlines;return false;}
                ++stats.expanded;
                const JointState s=nodes[side][parent].state;
                bool completed=transitions(s,side!=0,[&](const JointState& state,Move move) {
                    if(visited[side].contains(state))return true;
                    if(nodes[0].size()+nodes[1].size()>=state_cap){++stats.state_caps;return false;}
                    int index=int(nodes[side].size());
                    nodes[side].push_back({state,parent,move});visited[side].emplace(state,index);
                    auto other=visited[side^1].find(state);
                    if(other!=visited[side^1].end()) {
                        meeting[side]=index;meeting[side^1]=other->second;return false;
                    }
                    next.push_back(index);return true;
                });
                if(meeting[0]>=0) {
                    for(int at=meeting[0];nodes[0][at].parent>=0;at=nodes[0][at].parent)
                        result.push_back(nodes[0][at].move);
                    reverse(result.begin(),result.end());
                    for(int at=meeting[1];nodes[1][at].parent>=0;at=nodes[1][at].parent)
                        result.push_back(nodes[1][at].move);
                    return true;
                }
                if(!completed)return false;
            }
            frontier[side]=move(next);++depth[side];
        }
        return false;
    }
};

struct JointWindow {
    int first,last,count=0,pieces=0,priority=0;
    array<int,4> cells{};
    JointState begin{},end{};
    vector<int> operations;
};
struct JointPatch {JointWindow window;vector<Move> replacement;};

vector<JointWindow> collect_joint_windows(const vector<Move>& answer,double deadline,
                                      JointWindowStats& stats,int first_begin=0,int first_end=INT_MAX) {
    vector<JointWindow> windows;State boundary=board_info.initial;
    for(int first=0;first<int(answer.size());first++) {
        if((first&15)==0&&time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()>=deadline){++stats.deadlines;break;}
        if(first<first_begin||first>=first_end){board_info.apply(boundary,answer[first]);continue;}
        array<int16_t,max_cells> local;local.fill(-1);
        array<bool,max_cells> blocked{};
        JointWindow w;w.first=w.last=first;
        for(int at=first;at<min(int(answer.size()),first+64);at++) {
            Move m=answer[at];int p=m.p,q=board_info.ray[p][m.d][m.l];
            if(at!=first&&local[p]<0&&local[q]<0) {
                blocked[p]=blocked[q]=true;continue;
            }
            bool allowed=true;int new_cells=0;
            for(int cell:{p,q})if(local[cell]<0) {
                if(blocked[cell]||board_info.nest_code[cell])allowed=false;
                ++new_cells;
            }
            if(!allowed||w.count+new_cells>4)break;
            for(int cell:{p,q})if(local[cell]<0) {
                int index=w.count++;local[cell]=int16_t(index);w.cells[index]=cell;
                w.begin[index]=w.end[index]=boundary[cell];
                w.pieces+=height(boundary[cell]);
            }
            int a=local[p],b=local[q];
            TowerBits parcel=w.end[a]>>(4*m.k);
            w.end[a]=prefix(w.end[a],m.k);
            w.end[b]=append_tower(w.end[b],reverse_tower(parcel));
            w.operations.push_back(at);w.last=at;
            if(w.operations.size()==8)break;
        }
        if(w.count>=3&&w.operations.size()>=3&&w.pieces>=2) {
            int changed=0;for(int j=0;j<w.count;j++)changed+=w.begin[j]!=w.end[j];
            w.priority=int(w.operations.size())-changed+(w.begin==w.end?16:0);
            stats.large_candidates+=w.pieces>8;
            windows.push_back(move(w));
        }
        board_info.apply(boundary,answer[first]);
    }
    stats.candidates+=windows.size();
    stable_sort(windows.begin(),windows.end(),[](const JointWindow& a,const JointWindow& b) {
        if(a.priority!=b.priority)return a.priority>b.priority;
        if(a.pieces!=b.pieces)return a.pieces<b.pieces;
        return a.operations.size()>b.operations.size();
    });
    return windows;
}

vector<Move> compress_joint_windows(const vector<Move>& answer,double deadline,
                                 JointWindowStats& stats,
                                 int first_begin=0,int first_end=INT_MAX) {
    auto windows=collect_joint_windows(answer,deadline,stats,first_begin,first_end);
    JointWindowRouter router;
    vector<JointPatch> patches;
    vector<bool> occupied(answer.size(),false);
    for(const auto& w:windows) {
        if(time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()>=deadline){++stats.deadlines;break;}
        bool overlap=false;for(int at=w.first;at<=w.last;at++)overlap|=occupied[at];
        if(overlap)continue;
        router.prepare(w.cells,w.count);vector<Move> replacement;
        stats.large_searches+=w.pieces>8;
        if(!router.search(w.begin,w.end,int(w.operations.size())-1,deadline,stats,replacement))continue;
        JointState check=w.begin;
        for(Move m:replacement) {
            int a=int(find(w.cells.begin(),w.cells.begin()+w.count,m.p)-w.cells.begin());
            int q=board_info.ray[m.p][m.d][m.l];
            int b=int(find(w.cells.begin(),w.cells.begin()+w.count,q)-w.cells.begin());
            if(a==w.count||b==w.count||m.k>=height(check[a])||m.l>m.k+1||
               height(check[b])+height(check[a])-m.k>8)throw logic_error("joint window illegal move");
            check[b]=append_tower(check[b],reverse_tower(check[a]>>(4*m.k)));
            check[a]=prefix(check[a],m.k);
        }
        if(check!=w.end||replacement.size()>=w.operations.size())throw logic_error("joint window endpoint mismatch");
        ++stats.replacements;stats.saved+=w.operations.size()-replacement.size();
        if(w.pieces>8){++stats.large_replacements;stats.large_saved+=w.operations.size()-replacement.size();}
        stats.independent+=w.last-w.first+1-int(w.operations.size());
        if(!replacement.empty())stats.changed_first_split+=replacement.front().k!=answer[w.first].k;
        for(int at=w.first;at<=w.last;at++)occupied[at]=true;
        patches.push_back({w,move(replacement)});
    }
    sort(patches.begin(),patches.end(),[](const JointPatch& a,const JointPatch& b){return a.window.first<b.window.first;});
    vector<Move> result;result.reserve(answer.size());int position=0;
    for(const auto& patch:patches) {
        const auto& w=patch.window;
        result.insert(result.end(),answer.begin()+position,answer.begin()+w.first);
        result.insert(result.end(),patch.replacement.begin(),patch.replacement.end());
        // 省略した操作の接触先はblockedで窓への追加を禁止したため、置換全体と交換できる。
        size_t related=0;
        for(int at=w.first;at<=w.last;at++) {
            if(related<w.operations.size()&&w.operations[related]==at)++related;
            else result.push_back(answer[at]);
        }
        position=w.last+1;
    }
    result.insert(result.end(),answer.begin()+position,answer.end());
    State original=board_info.initial,checked=board_info.initial;
    for(Move m:answer)board_info.apply(original,m);
    for(Move m:result)board_info.apply(checked,m);
    if(checked!=original)throw logic_error("joint window full board mismatch");
    return result;
}

#ifdef LOCAL
struct MonoDispatchStats {
    int64_t scans=0,windows=0,routes=0,replacements=0,saved=0,independent=0;
    void summary() const {
        auto count=[&](const char* key,int64_t value){trace.count_by(string("mono_dispatch_")+key,value);};
        count("scans",scans);count("windows",windows);count("routes",routes);
        count("replacements",replacements);count("saved",saved);count("independent",independent);
    }
} local_mono_dispatch;
#endif

// 同色のp→q、p→r、r→qを、踏み台部分もまとめて輸送する。
// p/q/rへ触れない操作は交換し、それ以外の接触で区間を閉じる。
int consolidate_mono_dispatch(const vector<Move>& answer,int begin,State& board,
                            vector<Move>& out,int& saved) {
    saved=0;
    const Move first=answer[begin];const int p=first.p;
    if(first.k==0)return begin;
    const TowerBits first_word=board[p]>>(4*first.k);
    const int color_code=mono_color_code(first_word);
    if(color_code<=0||top_color_code(prefix(board[p],first.k))!=color_code)return begin;
    LOCAL_NOTE(++local_mono_dispatch.scans;)
    const int q=board_info.ray[p][first.d][first.l];
    const int stop=min(int(answer.size()),begin+64);
    auto dest=[&](int i){const Move m=answer[i];return board_info.ray[m.p][m.d][m.l];};
    auto touches=[&](int i,int cell){return answer[i].p==cell||dest(i)==cell;};
    int second=begin+1;
    while(second<stop&&!touches(second,p)&&!touches(second,q))++second;
    if(second==stop||answer[second].p!=p)return begin;
    const int r=dest(second);
    if(r==p||r==q)return begin;
    // 置換を最初の出発へ移すため、中継塔に変更がないことを積み順まで確認する。
    for(int i=begin+1;i<second;i++)if(touches(i,r))return begin;
    int third=second+1;
    while(third<stop&&!touches(third,p)&&!touches(third,q)&&!touches(third,r))++third;
    if(third==stop||answer[third].p!=r||dest(third)!=q)return begin;
    LOCAL_NOTE(++local_mono_dispatch.windows;)
    State end=board;
    TowerBits second_word=0;
    for(int i=begin;i<=third;i++) {
        const Move m=answer[i];
        if(i==second) {
            second_word=end[p]>>(4*m.k);
            if(mono_color_code(second_word)!=color_code)return begin;
        }
        if(i==third&&(m.k!=height(board[r])||end[r]>>(4*m.k)!=second_word))return begin;
        board_info.apply(end,m);
    }
    const int keep=answer[second].k;
    if(keep>=first.k||mono_color_code(board[p]>>(4*keep))!=color_code)return begin;
    LOCAL_NOTE(++local_mono_dispatch.routes;)
    PortionRouter router;router.run(board,p,keep,q,2);
    for(int orientation=0;orientation<2;orientation++) {
        const int length=router.cost(q,orientation);
        if(length<0||length>=3)continue;
        const auto path=router.path(q,orientation);
        State check=board;bool legal=true;
        try {
            for(const Move m:path)board_info.apply(check,m);
            for(int i=begin+1;i<third;i++)if(i!=second)board_info.apply(check,answer[i]);
        }catch(const exception&){legal=false;}
        // 容量は帰巣前に検査する。全盤面の一致で、踏み台や中継巣の帰巣も確認する。
        if(!legal||!(check==end))continue;
        out.insert(out.end(),path.begin(),path.end());
        for(int i=begin+1;i<third;i++)if(i!=second)out.push_back(answer[i]);
        saved=3-length;board=end;
        LOCAL_NOTE(++local_mono_dispatch.replacements;local_mono_dispatch.saved+=saved;
                   local_mono_dispatch.independent+=third-begin-2;)
        return third+1;
    }
    return begin;
}

vector<Move> compress_mono_dispatch(const vector<Move>& answer,double deadline) {
    State board=board_info.initial;vector<Move> result;result.reserve(answer.size());
    for(int i=0;i<int(answer.size());) {
        if(time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()>=deadline) {
            result.insert(result.end(),answer.begin()+i,answer.end());return result;
        }
        int saved=0;int next=consolidate_mono_dispatch(answer,i,board,result,saved);
        if(next>i)i=next;
        else{board_info.apply(board,answer[i]);result.push_back(answer[i++]);}
    }
    if(board_info.count(board))throw logic_error("mono dispatch incomplete");
    return result;
}

struct CoupledRoutesStats {
    int64_t calls=0,seeds=0,routes=0,branches=0,replacements=0,saved=0;
    int64_t independent=0,deadlines=0,beam_caps=0,depth_caps=0;
    int64_t overrun_seeds=0,relaxed_branches=0,relaxed_replacements=0,relaxed_saved=0;
    int64_t overrun_replacements=0,overrun_saved=0;
    double seconds=0;
#ifdef LOCAL
    void summary() const {
        auto count=[&](const char* key,int64_t value){trace.count_by(string("coupled_routes_")+key,value);};
        count("calls",calls);count("seeds",seeds);count("routes",routes);count("branches",branches);
        count("replacements",replacements);count("saved",saved);count("independent",independent);
        count("deadlines",deadlines);count("beam_caps",beam_caps);count("depth_caps",depth_caps);
        count("overrun_seeds",overrun_seeds);count("relaxed_branches",relaxed_branches);
        count("relaxed_replacements",relaxed_replacements);count("relaxed_saved",relaxed_saved);
        count("overrun_replacements",overrun_replacements);count("overrun_saved",overrun_saved);
        trace.add_time_ms("coupled_routes",seconds*1000.0);
    }
#endif
} coupled_routes;
struct CoupledRoutesTimer {
    double begin=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec();
    ~CoupledRoutesTimer(){coupled_routes.seconds+=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()-begin;}
};
struct RouteVariant {
    array<TowerBits,max_cells> words{};
    vector<Move> moves;
    int peak_excess=0;
    bool relaxed=false;
};

// 合流・分割・帰巣までを1輸送として読む。次の輸送では束の構成が変わり得る。
int consume_transfer(const vector<Move>& answer,int begin,int stop,State& board,int& goal) {
    const Move first=answer[begin];
    const int amount=height(board[first.p])-first.k;
    TowerBits traveling=board[first.p]>>(4*first.k);goal=first.p;
    int end=begin;
    for(;end<stop;end++) {
        const Move m=answer[end];
        if(m.p!=goal||height(board[goal])-m.k!=amount||board[goal]>>(4*m.k)!=traveling)break;
        board_info.apply(board,m);goal=board_info.ray[m.p][m.d][m.l];traveling=reverse_tower(traveling);
        if(board_info.normalize(traveling,goal)!=traveling){++end;break;}
    }
    if(end==begin)throw logic_error("empty transfer segment");
    return end;
}

// 区間境界で高さを保ち、最大2塔の積み順の差を後の輸送まで引き継ぐ。
int route_difference(const State& candidate,const State& original) {
    int different=0;
    for(int p=0;p<board_info.cell_count;p++)if(candidate[p]!=original[p]) {
        if(height(candidate[p])!=height(original[p])||++different>2)return -1;
    }
    return different;
}
RouteVariant route_variant(const State& board,vector<Move> moves) {
    RouteVariant result;
    for(int p=0;p<board_info.cell_count;p++)result.words[p]=board[p];
    result.moves=move(moves);return result;
}

int couple_transfer_window(const vector<Move>& answer,int begin,State& board,
                         vector<Move>& output,int& saved,double deadline,bool allow_slack=true) {
    saved=0;
    const int stop=min(int(answer.size()),begin+64);
    State original=board;int goal=-1;
    int end=consume_transfer(answer,begin,stop,original,goal);
    if(!allow_slack&&end-begin<2)return begin;
    const Move first=answer[begin];
    // 上下対称な束には、後の輸送まで引き継ぐ向きの差がない。
    const TowerBits packet=board[first.p]>>(4*first.k);
    if(packet==reverse_tower(packet))return begin;
    // 厳密側は最初から短縮する経路を残す。緩和側は1手の超過を許し、候補4本で追跡する。
    const int first_cap=end-begin+(allow_slack?1:-1);
    PortionRouter router;router.run(board,first.p,first.k,goal,first_cap);++coupled_routes.routes;
    vector<RouteVariant> active;
    for(int o=0;o<2;o++) {
        int length=router.cost(goal,o);if(length<0||length>first_cap)continue;
        State candidate=board;auto path=router.path(goal,o);bool legal=true;
        try{for(auto m:path)board_info.apply(candidate,m);}catch(const exception&){legal=false;}
        if(legal&&route_difference(candidate,original)>0) {
            auto variant=route_variant(candidate,move(path));
            variant.peak_excess=max(0,length-(end-begin));
            variant.relaxed=length>=end-begin;
            coupled_routes.overrun_seeds+=variant.peak_excess>0;
            active.push_back(move(variant));
        }
    }
    if(active.empty())return begin;
    ++coupled_routes.seeds;
    for(int depth=1;depth<8&&end<stop;depth++) {
        if(time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()>=deadline){++coupled_routes.deadlines;return begin;}
        array<TowerBits,max_cells> before{};for(int p=0;p<board_info.cell_count;p++)before[p]=original[p];
        const int start=end;const Move next=answer[start];
        end=consume_transfer(answer,start,stop,original,goal);
        vector<RouteVariant> children;
        for(const auto& variant:active) {
            if(time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()>=deadline){++coupled_routes.deadlines;return begin;}
            State working=variant.words;
            bool touches=false;
            for(int i=start;i<end;i++) {
                const Move m=answer[i];const int q=board_info.ray[m.p][m.d][m.l];
                touches|=before[m.p]!=variant.words[m.p]||before[q]!=variant.words[q];
            }
            // 無関係な操作は順序を保って再生し、経路を探索し直さない。
            if(!touches) {
                auto path=variant.moves;
                for(int i=start;i<end;i++){board_info.apply(working,answer[i]);path.push_back(answer[i]);}
                auto child=route_variant(working,move(path));
                child.peak_excess=variant.peak_excess;child.relaxed=variant.relaxed;
                children.push_back(move(child));++coupled_routes.independent;
                continue;
            }
            // 先の短縮を後の輸送に使えるよう、手数上限は区間全体へ適用する。
            const int budget=allow_slack?end-begin+1-int(variant.moves.size()):end-start;
            router.run(working,next.p,next.k,goal,budget);++coupled_routes.routes;
            for(int o=0;o<2;o++) {
                const int length=router.cost(goal,o);if(length<0||length>budget)continue;
                auto path=router.path(goal,o);State candidate=working;bool legal=true;
                try{for(auto m:path)board_info.apply(candidate,m);}catch(const exception&){legal=false;}
                if(!legal)continue;
                const int difference=route_difference(candidate,original);
                if(difference<0)continue;
                vector<Move> complete=variant.moves;
                complete.insert(complete.end(),path.begin(),path.end());++coupled_routes.branches;
                auto child=route_variant(candidate,move(complete));
                child.peak_excess=max(variant.peak_excess,int(child.moves.size())-(end-begin));
                child.relaxed=variant.relaxed||length>end-start;
                coupled_routes.relaxed_branches+=length>end-start;
                if(difference==0&&int(child.moves.size())<end-begin) {
                    saved=end-begin-int(child.moves.size());
                    board=original;output.insert(output.end(),child.moves.begin(),child.moves.end());
                    ++coupled_routes.replacements;coupled_routes.saved+=saved;
                    coupled_routes.relaxed_replacements+=child.relaxed;
                    coupled_routes.relaxed_saved+=child.relaxed?saved:0;
                    coupled_routes.overrun_replacements+=child.peak_excess>0;
                    coupled_routes.overrun_saved+=child.peak_excess>0?saved:0;
                    return end;
                }
                children.push_back(move(child));
            }
        }
        if(children.empty())return begin;
        stable_sort(children.begin(),children.end(),[](const auto& a,const auto& b){return a.moves.size()<b.moves.size();});
        active.clear();
        for(auto& child:children) {
            bool duplicate=false;for(const auto& kept:active)duplicate|=child.words==kept.words;
            if(duplicate)continue;
            if(active.size()==4){++coupled_routes.beam_caps;break;}
            active.push_back(move(child));
        }
    }
    ++coupled_routes.depth_caps;return begin;
}

vector<Move> polish_coupled_routes(const vector<Move>& answer,double deadline,int& saved,bool allow_slack=true,
                                 int first_begin=0,int first_end=INT_MAX) {
    CoupledRoutesTimer timer;++coupled_routes.calls;saved=0;
    State board=board_info.initial;vector<Move> output;output.reserve(answer.size());
    for(int i=0;i<int(answer.size());) {
        // 置換後の全盤面が一致しているため、期限到達時は残りの操作をそのままつなげられる。
        if(time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()>=deadline) {
            ++coupled_routes.deadlines;
            output.insert(output.end(),answer.begin()+i,answer.end());return output;
        }
        if(i<first_begin||i>=first_end){board_info.apply(board,answer[i]);output.push_back(answer[i++]);continue;}
        int shorter=0;const int end=couple_transfer_window(answer,i,board,output,shorter,deadline,allow_slack);
        if(end>i){saved+=shorter;i=end;}
        else{board_info.apply(board,answer[i]);output.push_back(answer[i++]);}
    }
    if(board_info.count(board))throw logic_error("coupled routes incomplete");
    return output;
}

// 1回のBFSで全着地点を調べる。帰巣が起きた着地で輸送を切り、変化後の盤面から再探索する。
class FiniteRouter {
    int source,keep,bg[max_cells];
    int16_t distance[2*max_cells],previous[2*max_cells];
    uint8_t direction[2*max_cells],length[2*max_cells];
public:
    void run(const State& b,int p,int k,int cap) {
        source=p;keep=k;TowerBits packet=b[p]>>(4*k);int amount=height(packet);
        for(int v=0;v<board_info.cell_count;v++)bg[v]=height(b[v]);
        bg[p]=height(board_info.normalize(prefix(b[p],k),p));
        fill(distance,distance+2*board_info.cell_count,int16_t(-1));
        int queue[2*max_cells],head=0,tail=0;queue[tail++]=p*2;distance[p*2]=0;previous[p*2]=-1;
        int top[2]={top_color_code(packet),int(packet&15)};
        while(head<tail) {
            int at=queue[head++],v=at/2,o=at&1;
            if(distance[at]>=cap||(at!=p*2&&board_info.nest_code[v]==top[o]))continue;
            int reach=(at==p*2?keep:bg[v])+1;
            for(int d=0;d<4;d++)for(int l=1;l<=min(reach,int(board_info.ray_count[v][d]));l++) {
                int q=board_info.ray[v][d][l],next=q*2+(o^1);
                if(bg[q]+amount>8||distance[next]>=0)continue;
                distance[next]=distance[at]+1;previous[next]=at;direction[next]=d;length[next]=l;
                queue[tail++]=next;
            }
        }
    }
    int cost(int q,int o) const {return distance[q*2+o];}
    vector<Move> path(int q,int o) const {
        vector<Move> moves;int at=q*2+o;
        if(distance[at]<0)return moves;
        while(previous[at]>=0) {
            int prev=previous[at],p=prev/2;
            moves.push_back({int16_t(p),uint8_t(prev==source*2?keep:bg[p]),direction[at],length[at]});
            at=prev;
        }
        reverse(moves.begin(),moves.end());return moves;
    }
};

// 固定背景上の全可動塔をマスと色順で表す。個体IDは区別せず、積み順と容量を保つ。
using FiniteState=array<uint64_t,16>;
struct FiniteHash {
    size_t operator()(const FiniteState& s) const {
        uint64_t h=0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
        for(uint64_t x:s)h=(h^x)*0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
        return h^(h>>32);
    }
};
int common_tower_prefix(TowerBits a,TowerBits b) {
    int n=0;while(a&&b&&(a&15)==(b&15)){++n;a>>=4;b>>=4;}return n;
}
struct FiniteWindow {
    int first=0,last=0,count=0,pieces=0,towers=0,priority=0;
    bool has_nest=false;
    array<int,8> cells{};
    array<TowerBits,8> begin{},end{};
    vector<int> operations;
};
struct FinitePatch {FiniteWindow window;vector<Move> replacement;};
struct FiniteStats {
    int64_t candidates=0,eligible=0,attempts=0,searches=0,expanded=0,generated=0;
    int64_t bound_prunes=0,returned_prunes=0,beam_pruned=0,completed=0,capped=0,deadlines=0,global_deadlines=0;
    int64_t replacements=0,saved=0,nonempty_replacements=0,nonempty_saved=0,nest_saved=0;
    int64_t merges=0,split_moves=0,source_returns=0,independent=0;
    void summary() const {
        LOCAL_NOTE(
            auto count=[&](const char* key,int64_t value){trace.count_by(string("finite_")+key,value);};
            count("candidates",candidates);count("eligible",eligible);count("attempts",attempts);
            count("searches",searches);count("expanded",expanded);count("generated",generated);
            count("bound_prunes",bound_prunes);count("returned_prunes",returned_prunes);count("beam_pruned",beam_pruned);
            count("completed",completed);count("capped",capped);count("deadlines",deadlines);
            count("global_deadlines",global_deadlines);count("replacements",replacements);count("saved",saved);
            count("nonempty_replacements",nonempty_replacements);count("nonempty_saved",nonempty_saved);
            count("nest_saved",nest_saved);count("merges",merges);count("split_moves",split_moves);
            count("source_returns",source_returns);count("independent",independent);
        )
    }
};

vector<FiniteWindow> collect_finite_windows(const vector<Move>& answer,double deadline,FiniteStats& stats,
                                            int first_begin=0,int first_end=INT_MAX) {
    vector<FiniteWindow> windows;State boundary=board_info.initial;
    for(int first=0;first<int(answer.size());first++) {
        if((first&15)==0&&time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()>=deadline){++stats.global_deadlines;break;}
        if(first<first_begin||first>=first_end){board_info.apply(boundary,answer[first]);continue;}
        array<int16_t,max_cells> local;local.fill(-1);array<bool,max_cells> blocked{};
        FiniteWindow w;w.first=w.last=first;
        for(int at=first;at<min(int(answer.size()),first+64);at++) {
            Move m=answer[at];int p=m.p,q=board_info.ray[p][m.d][m.l];
            if(at!=first&&local[p]<0&&local[q]<0){blocked[p]=blocked[q]=true;continue;}
            bool allowed=true;int cells=0,pieces=0,towers=0;
            for(int v:{p,q})if(local[v]<0) {
                allowed&=!blocked[v];++cells;pieces+=height(boundary[v]);towers+=boundary[v]!=0;
            }
            if(!allowed||w.count+cells>8||w.pieces+pieces>16||w.towers+towers>6)break;
            w.pieces+=pieces;w.towers+=towers;
            for(int v:{p,q})if(local[v]<0) {
                int i=w.count++;local[v]=int16_t(i);w.cells[i]=v;
                w.begin[i]=w.end[i]=boundary[v];w.has_nest|=board_info.nest_code[v]!=0;
            }
            const int a=local[p],b=local[q];TowerBits packet=w.end[a]>>(4*m.k);
            w.end[a]=board_info.normalize(prefix(w.end[a],m.k),p);
            w.end[b]=board_info.normalize(append_tower(w.end[b],reverse_tower(packet)),q);
            w.operations.push_back(at);w.last=at;
            if(w.operations.size()>=3&&w.pieces>=2) {
                int sources=0,destinations=0;
                for(int i=0;i<w.count;i++) {
                    int matched=common_tower_prefix(w.begin[i],w.end[i]);
                    sources+=height(w.begin[i])>matched;destinations+=height(w.end[i])>matched;
                }
                w.priority=int(w.operations.size())-max(sources,destinations);
                windows.push_back(w);stats.eligible+=w.has_nest||w.count>4;
            }
            if(w.operations.size()==12)break;
        }
        board_info.apply(boundary,answer[first]);
    }
    stats.candidates+=windows.size();
    stable_sort(windows.begin(),windows.end(),[](const FiniteWindow& a,const FiniteWindow& b) {
        if(a.priority!=b.priority)return a.priority>b.priority;
        if(a.operations.size()!=b.operations.size())return a.operations.size()<b.operations.size();
        if(a.pieces!=b.pieces)return a.pieces<b.pieces;
        return a.first<b.first;
    });
    return windows;
}

class FinitePlanner {
    friend struct FiniteProbe;
    struct Node {
        FiniteState state{};
        int cost=0,parent=-1,source=-1,keep=0,destination=-1,orientation=0;
        double priority=0;
    };
    array<TowerBits,max_cells> goal_word{};
    array<int,13> required{};
    array<array<int,max_cells>,13> target_distance{};
    FiniteState target{};
    int jump=1;
    static TowerBits word(uint64_t x){return TowerBits(x);}
    static int cell(uint64_t x){return int(x>>32);}
    static FiniteState encode(const State& b,const State& background) {
        FiniteState s{};int n=0;
        for(int p=0;p<board_info.cell_count;p++) {
            int h=height(background[p]);
            if(prefix(b[p],h)!=background[p])throw logic_error("finite background changed");
            TowerBits w=h==8?0:b[p]>>(4*h);
            if(w){if(n==16)throw logic_error("finite capacity");s[n++]=(uint64_t(p)<<32)|w;}
        }
        return s;
    }
    static void decode(const FiniteState& s,const State& background,State& b) {
        b=background;for(uint64_t x:s)if(x)b[cell(x)]=append_tower(b[cell(x)],word(x));
    }
    int assess(const FiniteState& s,double& estimate) const {
        array<int,13> actual{};array<TowerBits,max_cells> current{};
        int sources=0,destinations=0,unsettled=0;double distance=0;
        for(uint64_t x:s)if(x) {
            int p=cell(x);TowerBits w=word(x);current[p]=w;
            for(TowerBits a=w;a;a>>=4)++actual[a&15];
            int matched=common_tower_prefix(w,goal_word[p]);
            sources+=height(w)>matched;unsettled+=height(w)-matched;
            TowerBits suffix=matched==8?0:w>>(4*matched);
            for(unsigned mask=color_mask(suffix);mask;mask&=mask-1)
                distance+=target_distance[1+__builtin_ctz(mask)][p];
        }
        // 帰巣した個体は戻せないため、出口で必要な匹数を下回った状態を捨てる。
        for(int current_code=1;current_code<=board_info.K;current_code++)if(actual[current_code]<required[current_code])return -1;
        for(uint64_t x:target)if(x)
            destinations+=height(word(x))>common_tower_prefix(current[cell(x)],word(x));
        const int lower=max(sources,destinations);
        // 枝刈りは下界だけで行う。距離と積み順は、出口に塔を残す場合も含めた探索順位に使う。
        estimate=lower+0.5*distance/jump+0.125*unsettled;
        return lower;
    }
public:
    bool plan(const State& initial,const FiniteWindow& window,int cap,double deadline,
              vector<Move>& answer,FiniteStats& stats) {
        ++stats.searches;answer.clear();State background=initial;
        goal_word.fill(0);required.fill(0);target.fill(0);
        array<int,13> initial_counts{};unsigned colors=0;vector<int> start_cells;
        for(int i=0;i<window.count;i++) {
            int p=window.cells[i];background[p]=0;goal_word[p]=window.end[i];
            if(initial[p])start_cells.push_back(p);
            colors|=color_mask(initial[p]);
            for(TowerBits w=initial[p];w;w>>=4)++initial_counts[w&15];
            for(TowerBits w=window.end[i];w;w>>=4)++required[w&15];
        }
        int next_target=0;
        for(int p=0;p<board_info.cell_count;p++)if(goal_word[p])target[next_target++]=(uint64_t(p)<<32)|goal_word[p];
        for(int current_code=1;current_code<=board_info.K;current_code++)for(int p=0;p<board_info.cell_count;p++) {
            int distance=initial_counts[current_code]>required[current_code]?board_info.floor_dist[p][board_info.nest_pos_by_code[current_code]]:infinite_cost;
            for(uint64_t x:target)if(x&&(color_mask(word(x))&(1u<<(current_code-1))))
                distance=min(distance,int(board_info.floor_dist[p][cell(x)]));
            target_distance[current_code][p]=distance;
        }
        array<bool,max_cells> destination{};
        for(int i=0;i<window.count;i++)destination[window.cells[i]]=true;
        for(unsigned mask=colors;mask;mask&=mask-1) {
            int nest_pos_by_code=board_info.nest_pos_by_code[1+__builtin_ctz(mask)];destination[nest_pos_by_code]=true;
            for(int d=0;d<4;d++)if(board_info.adj[nest_pos_by_code][d]>=0)destination[board_info.adj[nest_pos_by_code][d]]=true;
        }
        vector<pair<int,int>> meeting;
        if(start_cells.size()>=2)for(int p=0;p<board_info.cell_count;p++) {
            int a=infinite_cost,b=infinite_cost;
            for(int q:start_cells){int d=board_info.floor_dist[p][q];if(d<a){b=a;a=d;}else b=min(b,d);}
            meeting.push_back({a+b,p});
        }
        sort(meeting.begin(),meeting.end());
        for(int i=0;i<min(4,int(meeting.size()));i++)destination[meeting[i].second]=true;
        int max_background=0;for(int p=0;p<board_info.cell_count;p++)max_background=max(max_background,height(background[p]));
        jump=min(8,1+max_background+min(3,window.pieces-1));
        vector<Node> history(1);history[0].state=encode(initial,background);
        if(history[0].state==target){++stats.completed;return true;}
        double estimate=0;const int root_lower=assess(history[0].state,estimate);
        if(root_lower<0||root_lower>cap){++stats.bound_prunes;return false;}
        vector<int> beam={0};Node goal;int best=cap+1,expanded=0;
        unordered_map<FiniteState,int,FiniteHash> settled;settled.reserve(256);
        State board=initial;FiniteRouter router;bool stop=false;
        for(int depth=0;depth<cap&&!beam.empty()&&!stop;depth++) {
            vector<Node> next;unordered_map<FiniteState,int,FiniteHash> index;index.reserve(2048);
            for(int id:beam) {
                if(time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()>=deadline){++stats.deadlines;stop=true;break;}
                if(expanded==192){++stats.capped;stop=true;break;}
                Node from=history[id];
                auto done=settled.find(from.state);
                if(done!=settled.end()&&done->second<=from.cost)continue;
                settled[from.state]=from.cost;++expanded;++stats.expanded;
                decode(from.state,background,board);
                auto targets=destination;for(uint64_t x:from.state)if(x)targets[cell(x)]=true;
                for(uint64_t x:from.state)if(x) {
                    int p=cell(x),base=height(background[p]);TowerBits w=word(x);
                    for(int k=0;k<height(w);k++) {
                        if(time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()>=deadline){++stats.deadlines;stop=true;break;}
                        const int keep=base+k,limit=best-1-from.cost;
                        if(limit<=0)continue;
                        router.run(board,p,keep,limit);
                        TowerBits packet=board[p]>>(4*keep);bool mono=mono_color_code(packet)>0;
                        for(int q=0;q<board_info.cell_count;q++)if(targets[q])for(int o=0;o<2;o++) {
                            int d=router.cost(q,o);if(d<=0||d>limit)continue;
                            if(mono&&router.cost(q,o^1)>0&&
                               (router.cost(q,o^1)<d||(router.cost(q,o^1)==d&&o==1)))continue;
                            State after=board;auto path=router.path(q,o);for(Move m:path)board_info.apply(after,m);
                            Node child;child.state=encode(after,background);child.cost=from.cost+d;
                            child.parent=id;child.source=p;child.keep=keep;child.destination=q;child.orientation=o;
                            ++stats.generated;
                            if(child.state==target){if(child.cost<best){best=child.cost;goal=child;}continue;}
                            int lower=assess(child.state,estimate);
                            if(lower<0){++stats.returned_prunes;continue;}
                            if(child.cost+lower>=best){++stats.bound_prunes;continue;}
                            auto old=settled.find(child.state);
                            if(old!=settled.end()&&old->second<=child.cost)continue;
                            child.priority=child.cost+estimate;
                            auto [it,fresh]=index.emplace(child.state,int(next.size()));
                            if(fresh)next.push_back(child);
                            else if(child.cost<next[it->second].cost)next[it->second]=child;
                        }
                        if(best==root_lower){stop=true;break;}
                    }
                    if(stop)break;
                }
                if(stop)break;
            }
            auto order=[](const Node& a,const Node& b) {
                if(a.priority!=b.priority)return a.priority<b.priority;
                if(a.cost!=b.cost)return a.cost<b.cost;
                return a.state<b.state;
            };
            int width=min(12,int(next.size()));stats.beam_pruned+=int(next.size())-width;
            partial_sort(next.begin(),next.begin()+width,next.end(),order);
            beam.clear();for(int i=0;i<width;i++){beam.push_back(int(history.size()));history.push_back(move(next[i]));}
        }
        if(best>cap)return false;
        vector<Node> path;for(Node n=goal;n.parent>=0;n=history[n.parent])path.push_back(n);
        reverse(path.begin(),path.end());board=initial;
        for(const Node& n:path) {
            router.run(board,n.source,n.keep,n.cost);
            for(Move m:router.path(n.destination,n.orientation)) {
                int q=board_info.ray[m.p][m.d][m.l];
                stats.merges+=height(board[q])>height(background[q]);
                stats.split_moves+=m.k>height(background[m.p]);
                stats.source_returns+=height(prefix(board[m.p],m.k))-height(board_info.normalize(prefix(board[m.p],m.k),m.p));
                board_info.apply(board,m);answer.push_back(m);
            }
        }
        if(encode(board,background)!=target||int(answer.size())!=best)throw logic_error("finite reconstruction mismatch");
        ++stats.completed;return true;
    }
};

vector<Move> compress_finite_windows(const vector<Move>& answer,double deadline,FiniteStats& stats,
                                    int first_begin=0,int first_end=INT_MAX) {
    auto windows=collect_finite_windows(answer,deadline,stats,first_begin,first_end);
    vector<bool> occupied(answer.size(),false),attempted(answer.size(),false);
    vector<FinitePatch> patches;FinitePlanner planner;int trials=0;
    for(const auto& w:windows) {
        if(time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()>=deadline){++stats.global_deadlines;break;}
        if(!(w.has_nest||w.count>4)||w.priority<=0||attempted[w.first])continue;
        bool overlap=false;for(int t=w.first;t<=w.last;t++)overlap|=occupied[t];
        if(overlap)continue;
        if(trials==32)break;
        attempted[w.first]=true;++trials;++stats.attempts;
        State boundary=board_info.initial;for(int t=0;t<w.first;t++)board_info.apply(boundary,answer[t]);
        // 1回の探索予算を全体の期限内に収める。無限の期限は固定仕事量の診断だけで使う。
        const double trial_deadline=isfinite(deadline)?min(deadline,time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()+PROGRAM_TIME_LIMIT_SEC*0.003):deadline;
        vector<Move> replacement;
        if(!planner.plan(boundary,w,int(w.operations.size())-1,trial_deadline,replacement,stats))continue;
        ++stats.replacements;const int saved=int(w.operations.size()-replacement.size());stats.saved+=saved;
        bool nonempty=any_of(w.end.begin(),w.end.begin()+w.count,[](TowerBits x){return x!=0;});
        stats.nonempty_replacements+=nonempty;stats.nonempty_saved+=nonempty?saved:0;
        stats.nest_saved+=w.has_nest?saved:0;stats.independent+=w.last-w.first+1-int(w.operations.size());
        for(int t=w.first;t<=w.last;t++)occupied[t]=true;
        patches.push_back({w,move(replacement)});
    }
    sort(patches.begin(),patches.end(),[](const FinitePatch& a,const FinitePatch& b){return a.window.first<b.window.first;});
    vector<Move> result;result.reserve(answer.size());int position=0;
    for(const auto& patch:patches) {
        const auto& w=patch.window;
        result.insert(result.end(),answer.begin()+position,answer.begin()+w.first);
        result.insert(result.end(),patch.replacement.begin(),patch.replacement.end());
        size_t related=0;
        for(int t=w.first;t<=w.last;t++) {
            if(related<w.operations.size()&&w.operations[related]==t)++related;
            else result.push_back(answer[t]);
        }
        position=w.last+1;
    }
    result.insert(result.end(),answer.begin()+position,answer.end());
    State original=board_info.initial,checked=board_info.initial;
    for(Move m:answer)board_info.apply(original,m);
    for(Move m:result)board_info.apply(checked,m);
    if(checked!=original)throw logic_error("finite full board mismatch");
    return result;
}

enum FinalReduction { FINAL_ROUTE,FINAL_PAIR,FINAL_JOINT,FINAL_MONO,FINAL_STRICT,FINAL_SLACK,FINAL_FINITE,FINAL_KINDS };
constexpr const char* final_reduction_names[]={"route","pair","joint","mono","strict","slack","finite"};
struct FinalReductionStats {
    array<int64_t,FINAL_KINDS> calls{},saved{};
    array<double,FINAL_KINDS> seconds{};
    int64_t cheap_rounds=0,finite_rounds=0,deadlines=0,finite_budget_stops=0,stable=0;
    JointWindowStats joint;
    FiniteStats finite;
    int64_t total_saved() const {return accumulate(saved.begin(),saved.end(),int64_t(0));}
    void summary() const {
        LOCAL_NOTE(
            auto count=[&](const string& key,int64_t n){trace.count_by("final_reductions_"+key,n);};
            count("saved",total_saved());count("cheap_rounds",cheap_rounds);count("finite_rounds",finite_rounds);
            count("deadlines",deadlines);count("finite_budget_stops",finite_budget_stops);count("stable",stable);
            for(int kind=0;kind<FINAL_KINDS;kind++) {
                const string name=final_reduction_names[kind];count(name+"_calls",calls[kind]);count(name+"_saved",saved[kind]);
                trace.add_time_ms("final_reductions_"+name,seconds[kind]*1000.0);
            }
            count("joint_searches",joint.searches);count("joint_expanded",joint.expanded);
            count("joint_state_caps",joint.state_caps);count("joint_deadlines",joint.deadlines);
            count("joint_replacements",joint.replacements);count("joint_large_saved",joint.large_saved);
            local_mono_dispatch.summary();coupled_routes.summary();finite.summary();
        )
    }
};

template<class Reduce>
void apply_final_reduction(vector<Move>& answer,int kind,FinalReductionStats& stats,Reduce reduce) {
    const double begin=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec();
    auto reduced=reduce();
    if(reduced.size()>answer.size())throw logic_error("final reduction increased moves");
    ++stats.calls[kind];stats.saved[kind]+=answer.size()-reduced.size();
    stats.seconds[kind]+=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()-begin;answer=move(reduced);
}

// 短縮で分割・接触順が変わるため、各巡で短くなる間は再適用する。
// 有限区間の探索後にも安い短縮を行えるよう、終了予算を少し残す。
void polish_final_reductions(vector<Move>& answer,FinalReductionStats& stats) {
    const double deadline=time_keeper.time_limit_sec;
    const double finite_deadline=isfinite(deadline)?deadline-0.001*PROGRAM_TIME_LIMIT_SEC:deadline;
    auto check=[&]{if(time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()>=deadline)throw Deadline();};
    auto route_deadline=[&]{return isfinite(deadline)?min(deadline,time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()+0.003*PROGRAM_TIME_LIMIT_SEC):deadline;};
    try {
        while(true) {
            while(true) {
                const size_t before=answer.size();
                check();apply_final_reduction(answer,FINAL_ROUTE,stats,[&]{return smooth_routes(answer);});
                check();apply_final_reduction(answer,FINAL_PAIR,stats,[&]{return compress_pair_transfers(answer);});
                check();apply_final_reduction(answer,FINAL_JOINT,stats,[&]{return compress_joint_windows(answer,deadline,stats.joint);});
                check();apply_final_reduction(answer,FINAL_MONO,stats,[&]{return compress_mono_dispatch(answer,deadline);});
                check();apply_final_reduction(answer,FINAL_STRICT,stats,[&]{int saved;return polish_coupled_routes(answer,route_deadline(),saved,false);});
                check();apply_final_reduction(answer,FINAL_SLACK,stats,[&]{int saved;return polish_coupled_routes(answer,route_deadline(),saved,true);});
                ++stats.cheap_rounds;
                if(answer.size()==before)break;
            }
            check();
            if(time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()>=finite_deadline){++stats.finite_budget_stops;break;}
            const size_t before=answer.size();
            apply_final_reduction(answer,FINAL_FINITE,stats,[&]{return compress_finite_windows(answer,finite_deadline,stats.finite);});
            ++stats.finite_rounds;
            if(answer.size()==before){stats.stable=1;break;}
        }
    } catch(const Deadline&) {
        // 期限到達までに完了した短縮は残す。その他の例外は呼び出し側へ伝える。
        ++stats.deadlines;
    }
}

// 受理判定前に候補を短縮する。変更付近への重点化は選択基準であり、境界一致の保証ではない。
struct SearchFocus {
    int first=0,last=0;
    static bool same(Move a,Move b) {
        return a.p==b.p&&a.k==b.k&&a.d==b.d&&a.l==b.l;
    }
    static SearchFocus between(const vector<Move>& current,const vector<Move>& candidate) {
        int prefix=0,old_end=int(current.size()),new_end=int(candidate.size());
        while(prefix<min(old_end,new_end)&&same(current[prefix],candidate[prefix]))++prefix;
        if(prefix==old_end&&prefix==new_end)return {};
        while(old_end>prefix&&new_end>prefix&&same(current[old_end-1],candidate[new_end-1])){--old_end;--new_end;}
        return {max(0,prefix-64),min(int(candidate.size()),new_end+64)};
    }
};
struct SearchReductions {
    array<int64_t,FINAL_KINDS> calls{},saved{},accepted_saved{};
    array<double,FINAL_KINDS> seconds{};
    int64_t candidates=0,shortened=0,initial_saved=0,initial_reorder_saved=0;
    int64_t nonworse_created=0,best_created=0,probability_changed=0,accepted_shortened=0;
    int64_t eligible=0,budget_skips=0,heavy_calls=0,focus_operations=0,cheap_rounds=0;
    int64_t interrupted=0,budget_overruns=0;
    double heavy_seconds=0;
    int next_heavy=0;
    JointWindowStats joint;
    FiniteStats finite;
    static constexpr array<int,4> heavy_kinds={FINAL_JOINT,FINAL_STRICT,FINAL_SLACK,FINAL_FINITE};

    template<class Reduce>
    void apply(vector<Move>& answer,int kind,Reduce reduce) {
        const double begin=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec();++calls[kind];
        try {
            auto result=reduce();
            if(result.size()>answer.size())throw logic_error("candidate reduction increased moves");
            saved[kind]+=answer.size()-result.size();answer=move(result);
        } catch(const Deadline&) {
            seconds[kind]+=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()-begin;++interrupted;throw;
        }
        seconds[kind]+=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()-begin;
    }
    void cheap(vector<Move>& answer,double deadline) {
        while(time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()<deadline) {
            const size_t before=answer.size();++cheap_rounds;
            apply(answer,FINAL_PAIR,[&]{return compress_pair_transfers(answer);});
            if(time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()<deadline)
                apply(answer,FINAL_MONO,[&]{return compress_mono_dispatch(answer,deadline);});
            if(answer.size()==before)break;
            if(time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()<deadline)
                apply(answer,FINAL_ROUTE,[&]{return smooth_routes(answer);});
        }
    }
    static bool near_best(int iteration,size_t candidate,size_t current,size_t best) {
        return iteration%4==0&&candidate<=current+2&&candidate<=best+4;
    }
    static double remaining_budget(double exact_elapsed_sec,double spent) {
        // LNS経過時間の一定割合を予算とし、開始直後にも少量の余裕を与える。
        return 0.03*max(PROGRAM_TIME_LIMIT_SEC*0.02,exact_elapsed_sec)-spent;
    }
    void heavy(vector<Move>& answer,int kind,double deadline,SearchFocus focus) {
        switch(kind) {
            case FINAL_JOINT:
                apply(answer,kind,[&]{return compress_joint_windows(answer,deadline,joint,focus.first,focus.last);});break;
            case FINAL_STRICT:case FINAL_SLACK:
                apply(answer,kind,[&]{int removed;return polish_coupled_routes(answer,deadline,removed,kind==FINAL_SLACK,focus.first,focus.last);});break;
            case FINAL_FINITE:
                apply(answer,kind,[&]{return compress_finite_windows(answer,deadline,finite,focus.first,focus.last);});break;
            default:throw logic_error("unknown candidate reduction");
        }
    }
    void candidate(vector<Move>& answer,const vector<Move>& current,size_t best,
                   int iteration,double started,double end) {
        cheap(answer,end);
        if(!near_best(iteration,answer.size(),current.size(),best))return;
        const SearchFocus focus=SearchFocus::between(current,answer);
        if(focus.first==focus.last)return;
        ++eligible;
        const double begin=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec(),allowance=remaining_budget(begin-started,heavy_seconds);
        if(allowance<PROGRAM_TIME_LIMIT_SEC*0.0005||begin>=end){++budget_skips;return;}
        const double deadline=min(end,begin+min(allowance,PROGRAM_TIME_LIMIT_SEC*0.0015));
        const int kind=heavy_kinds[next_heavy];next_heavy=(next_heavy+1)%int(heavy_kinds.size());
        ++heavy_calls;focus_operations+=focus.last-focus.first;
        const size_t before=answer.size();
        heavy(answer,kind,deadline,focus);
        const double finish=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec();heavy_seconds+=finish-begin;
        budget_overruns+=finish>deadline;
        if(answer.size()<before)cheap(answer,end);
    }
    void summary() const {
        LOCAL_NOTE(
            auto count=[&](const string& key,int64_t n){trace.count_by("search_reductions_"+key,n);};
            count("candidates",candidates);count("shortened",shortened);count("initial_saved",initial_saved);
            count("initial_reorder_saved",initial_reorder_saved);count("nonworse_created",nonworse_created);
            count("best_created",best_created);count("probability_changed",probability_changed);
            count("accepted_shortened",accepted_shortened);count("eligible",eligible);count("budget_skips",budget_skips);
            count("heavy_calls",heavy_calls);count("focus_operations",focus_operations);count("cheap_rounds",cheap_rounds);
            count("interrupted",interrupted);count("budget_overruns",budget_overruns);
            for(int kind=0;kind<FINAL_KINDS;kind++) {
                const string name=final_reduction_names[kind];
                count(name+"_calls",calls[kind]);count(name+"_saved",saved[kind]);count(name+"_accepted_saved",accepted_saved[kind]);
                trace.add_time_ms("search_reductions_"+name,seconds[kind]*1000.0);
            }
            trace.add_time_ms("search_reductions_heavy",heavy_seconds*1000.0);
            count("joint_searches",joint.searches);count("joint_caps",joint.state_caps);count("joint_deadlines",joint.deadlines);
            count("finite_attempts",finite.attempts);count("finite_deadlines",finite.deadlines);
            count("finite_global_deadlines",finite.global_deadlines);count("finite_caps",finite.capped);
        )
    }
} search_reductions;

// 個体IDは初期位置の床ID。同色の束の除去では、色順が同じなら個体IDの割り当てを交換できる。
struct IdentityState {
    array<array<int16_t,8>,max_cells> a{};
    array<uint8_t,max_cells> h{};
    array<uint8_t,max_cells> alive{};
    IdentityState(const vector<int>& deleted={}) {
        array<bool,max_cells> omit{};for(int id:deleted)omit[id]=true;
        for(int p=0;p<board_info.cell_count;p++)if(board_info.initial[p]&&!omit[p]){
            a[p][0]=p;h[p]=1;alive[p]=1;
        }
    }
    TowerBits word(int p) const {
        TowerBits w=0;
        for(int i=0;i<h[p];i++)w|=board_info.initial[a[p][i]]<<(4*i);
        return w;
    }
    State words() const {State b{};for(int p=0;p<board_info.cell_count;p++)b[p]=word(p);return b;}
    void apply(Move m) {
        int p=m.p,q=board_info.ray[p][m.d][m.l];
        if(m.k>=h[p]||m.l<1||m.l>m.k+1||q<0||h[q]+h[p]-m.k>8)
            throw logic_error("identity move invalid");
        for(int i=h[p]-1;i>=m.k;i--)a[q][h[q]++]=a[p][i];
        h[p]=m.k;
        for(int v:{p,q})while(h[v]&&board_info.initial[a[v][h[v]-1]]==TowerBits(board_info.nest_code[v])){
            alive[a[v][h[v]-1]]=0;h[v]--;
        }
    }
};

struct Removal {
    vector<int> ids;
    double priority=0;
    uint64_t hash=0;
    int broken_support_jumps=0;
    int gross_removed=0;
    double rank_noise=1.0,rank_divisor=1.0;
    uint64_t score_generation=0;
};

b5680000000p-8f,-0x1.ee1f280000000p-5f,0x1.11a7400000000p-5f,0x1.8d8fee0000000p-4f,0x1.b4903c0000000p-7f},{0x1.6760760000000p-7f,-0x1.6307200000000p-2f,-0x1.272c3c0000000p-5f,-0x1.533f720000000p-4f,-0x1.91e07e0000000p-3f,0x1.ec58940000000p-2f,0x1.33fa9e0000000p-2f,0x1.b6ca9e0000000p-5f,-0x1.40083e0000000p-3f,0x1.f5f5980000000p-4f,0x1.4a8e4a0000000p-5f,0x1.b29ab00000000p-3f,-0x1.8efdba0000000p-4f,0x1.ce2cd00000000p-6f,-0x1.0653700000000p-3f,0x1.1d1dce0000000p-4f,0x1.57f4900000000p-2f,-0x1.31ffa40000000p-4f,-0x1.87c7c80000000p-3f,-0x1.ea09f80000000p-4f,-0x1.ac0a6e0000000p-6f,0x1.02f6ec0000000p-3f,0x1.27d7de0000000p-3f,0x1.19ff8c0000000p-3f,0x1.d443d60000000p-5f,-0x1.f88b460000000p-4f,0x1.3014fc0000000p-3f,-0x1.5886440000000p-4f,-0x1.c950c80000000p-2f,-0x1.f9b1f80000000p-2f,-0x1.6247560000000p-5f,-0x1.6e0c020000000p-4f},{0x1.91926e0000000p-3f,-0x1.76d2ba0000000p-5f,-0x1.1501fa0000000p-2f,0x1.5a71a60000000p-3f,0x1.4d9c520000000p-2f,0x1.0336e20000000p-3f,0x1.682e640000000p-3f,-0x1.3a65020000000p-4f,0x1.426ed00000000p-2f,-0x1.14ac2e0000000p-3f,0x1.62c4b40000000p-4f,-0x1.c90be00000000p-3f,-0x1.11e38c0000000p-3f,-0x1.6528800000000p-3f,0x1.3d6d740000000p-4f,-0x1.ee0ee20000000p-2f,-0x1.4cec020000000p-2f,0x1.c42c140000000p-3f,0x1.984f4a0000000p-3f,0x1.5b00ee0000000p-3f,-0x1.26a2000000000p-3f,-0x1.12453a0000000p-2f,0x1.cf82560000000p-7f,0x1.f358540000000p-4f,0x1.5dc82e0000000p-3f,0x1.0f1aea0000000p-5f,0x1.8e3bd80000000p-7f,-0x1.ba90120000000p-4f,0x1.f9adf20000000p-4f,-0x1.cbfa400000000p-7f,0x1.0fd1ea0000000p-4f,-0x1.a408320000000p-7f}};
constexpr float b1[16]={0x1.a7008e0000000p-4f,0x1.0968300000000p-3f,0x1.a17d0e0000000p-2f,-0x1.297f2c0000000p-2f,0x1.63bba20000000p-6f,0x1.299f7e0000000p-2f,-0x1.c67a640000000p-1f,0x1.34ec2c0000000p-2f,-0x1.f47b220000000p-4f,-0x1.0f76980000000p-2f,0x1.3491620000000p-1f,0x1.48caca0000000p-4f,-0x1.26d02c0000000p-2f,-0x1.ed40a20000000p-2f,-0x1.59e1080000000p-3f,-0x1.24dad80000000p-5f};
constexpr float w2[16]={-0x1.a57ff00000000p-2f,0x1.091da60000000p-2f,-0x1.ca0d480000000p-2f,0x1.5575be0000000p-2f,-0x1.f118cc0000000p-2f,-0x1.f48c4a0000000p-2f,0x1.2572860000000p+0f,0x1.bc2d260000000p-2f,0x1.527eac0000000p-2f,0x1.9dc2d20000000p-1f,0x1.6a9b960000000p-1f,0x1.104ae00000000p-1f,0x1.021f880000000p-1f,-0x1.52c99e0000000p-1f,0x1.d19cd40000000p-2f,-0x1.32af3e0000000p-1f};
constexpr float b2[1]={0x1.11cbd40000000p-8f};
float predict(const array<double,32>& raw) {
    array<float,32> x;
    // 学習時と同じく、rawをfloat32へ丸めてから正規化する。
    for(int j=0;j<32;j++)x[j]=(float(raw[j])-mean[j])/scale[j];
    float answer=b2[0];
    for(int i=0;i<16;i++){
        float value=b1[i];
        for(int j=0;j<32;j++)value+=w1[i][j]*x[j];
        answer+=w2[i]*max(0.0f,value);
    }
    return answer;
}
}
#ifdef LOCAL
struct NeuralRankStats {
    int64_t calls=0,scored=0,changed=0,rank_sum=0;
    double feature_ms=0,inference_ms=0,total_ms=0;
    void summary() const {
        trace.count_by("nn_calls",calls);trace.count_by("nn_candidates_scored",scored);
        trace.count_by("nn_changed_choices",changed);trace.count_by("nn_selected_original_rank_sum",rank_sum);
        trace.add_time_ms("nn_features",feature_ms);trace.add_time_ms("nn_inference",inference_ms);
        trace.add_time_ms("nn_selection",total_ms);
    }
} local_nn;
#endif

class TemporalLNS {
#ifdef AHC072_NN_PROBE
    friend struct NeuralRankProbe;
#endif
    // 候補の索引を再利用する。世代番号で初期化し、候補ごとの要素確保・解放を避ける。
    class EpochIndex {
        struct Slot {uint64_t key=0;int value=0;uint32_t epoch=0;};
        vector<Slot> table;
        uint32_t epoch=0;
        static uint64_t hash(uint64_t x) {
            x^=x>>30;x*=0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;x^=x>>27;x*=0x94d049bb133111ebULL;return x^(x>>31);
        }
    public:
        void reset(size_t expected) {
            size_t n=16;while(n<expected*2+16)n<<=1;
            if(table.size()<n){table.assign(n,Slot{});epoch=0;}
            if(++epoch==0){for(auto& e:table)e.epoch=0;epoch=1;}
        }
        pair<int,bool> put(uint64_t key,int value) {
            size_t at=hash(key)&(table.size()-1);
            while(table[at].epoch==epoch) {
                if(table[at].key==key)return {table[at].value,false};
                at=(at+1)&(table.size()-1);
            }
            table[at]={key,value,epoch};return {value,true};
        }
    } build_seen,build_packets,build_patterns;
    struct Pattern {array<int16_t,8> ids{};int n=0;vector<int> times;};
    vector<Pattern> pattern_buffer;
    vector<int> pattern_anchors[max_cells];
    vector<array<int16_t,8>> cut_ids;

    struct Link {int previous,time;Move move;bool extra;};
    struct Label {
        int cost=infinite_cost,bonus=-1000000,path=-1;
    };
    // 同じ予定時刻の空間探索。費用の小さい順に確定し、同費用では補助評価だけを更新する。
    class SpatialQueue {
        struct Item {int state,next;};
        vector<int> head;
        // 上位を世代、下位を待機費用に使う。各時刻の初期化を費用桶の数に抑える。
        vector<uint64_t> queued;
        vector<Item> nodes;
        uint32_t epoch=0;
        int low=infinite_cost;
    public:
        void prepare(int states) {
            if(int(queued.size())<states)queued.resize(states,0);
            if(nodes.capacity()<size_t(states)*2)nodes.reserve(size_t(states)*2);
        }
        void reset(int cap) {
            if(++epoch==0){fill(queued.begin(),queued.end(),0);epoch=1;}
            head.assign(max(1,cap+1),-1);nodes.clear();low=infinite_cost;
        }
        void push(int state,int cost) {
            if(cost<0||cost>=int(head.size()))return;
            uint64_t tag=(uint64_t(epoch)<<32)|uint32_t(cost);
            if(queued[state]==tag)return;
            queued[state]=tag;
            nodes.push_back({state,head[cost]});head[cost]=int(nodes.size())-1;
            low=min(low,cost);
        }
        bool pop(int ceiling,int &state,int &cost) {
            while(low<=ceiling&&low<int(head.size())&&head[low]<0)++low;
            if(low>ceiling||low>=int(head.size()))return false;
            int id=head[low];head[low]=nodes[id].next;
            state=nodes[id].state;cost=low;return true;
        }
    } small_queue,packet_queue;
    vector<Label> small_labels,packet_labels;
    vector<array<Label,8>> packet_from_p,packet_from_q;
    static constexpr int CHECKPOINT=16;
    vector<IdentityState> checkpoints;
    struct Flight {array<int16_t,8> id{};uint8_t n=0;};
    // 下段の個体は複数の便を支え得る。slack匹までなら失ってもそのジャンプは成立する。
    struct SupportUse {Flight lower;int time,slack;};
    struct PacketCut {
        int time=-1,keep=0,size=0,saved=0;
        uint64_t hash=0;
        double priority=0;
        bool later=false,before_split=false;
    };
    vector<Flight> flights;
    vector<SupportUse> support_uses;
    vector<PacketCut> packet_cuts;
    vector<PacketCut> later_cuts;
    vector<vector<int>> spatial_cuts;
    vector<Link> links;
    vector<Removal> candidates;
    unordered_map<uint64_t,int> last_tried;
    int iteration=0;
    int regular_selections=0;
#ifdef LOCAL
    friend struct DependencyProbe;
    bool local_dependency_extract=false;
#endif
    int support_weight=5,ride_weight=1;
    double lns_start=0;
    vector<Move> current;
    int stagnant=0;
    bool initialized=false;
    double active_seconds=0;
    uint64_t trajectory_generation=1,history_generation=0,packet_generation=0;
    int candidate_age=4;
    static constexpr int candidate_refresh_interval=4;
    void trajectory_changed() {
        ++candidate_age;
        if(++trajectory_generation==0) {
            trajectory_generation=1;history_generation=packet_generation=0;
            for(auto& c:candidates)c.score_generation=0;
            candidate_age=candidate_refresh_interval;
        }
    }
    // These histories are correctness-critical. They may never refer to an older
    // move sequence, even when the heuristic nomination order is reused.
    void refresh_history(const vector<Move>& answer) {
        flights.clear();support_uses.clear();checkpoints.clear();
        flights.reserve(answer.size());checkpoints.reserve((answer.size()+CHECKPOINT-1)/CHECKPOINT);
        IdentityState board;
        for(int t=0;t<int(answer.size());t++) {
            if((t&127)==0)time_keeper.check();
            if(t%CHECKPOINT==0)checkpoints.push_back(board);
            const Move m=answer[t];Flight flight;
            for(int i=m.k;i<board.h[m.p];i++)flight.id[flight.n++]=board.a[m.p][i];
            flights.push_back(flight);
            if(m.l>1) {
                SupportUse use{{},t,int(m.k)+1-int(m.l)};
                for(int i=0;i<m.k;i++)use.lower.id[use.lower.n++]=board.a[m.p][i];
                support_uses.push_back(use);
            }
            board.apply(m);
        }
        history_generation=trajectory_generation;++history_only_builds;
    }
    void ensure_history(const vector<Move>& answer) {
        if(history_generation==trajectory_generation)return;
        const double begin=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec();
        refresh_history(answer);
        const double duration=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()-begin;
        build_seconds+=duration;history_only_seconds+=duration;
    }
    void ensure_packets(const vector<Move>& answer) {
        if(packet_generation==trajectory_generation)return;
        const double begin=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec();
        make_candidates(answer,false);
        const double duration=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()-begin;
        build_seconds+=duration;packet_only_seconds+=duration;
    }
    void rebuild_all(const vector<Move>& answer) {
        const double begin=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec();
        make_candidates(answer,true);
        const double duration=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()-begin;
        build_seconds+=duration;full_build_seconds+=duration;
    }
    void refresh_regular_score(Removal& c) {
