        if(c.score_generation==trajectory_generation)return;
        assert(history_generation==trajectory_generation);
        c.gross_removed=removed_commands(c.ids);
        c.broken_support_jumps=broken_support_jumps_value(c.ids);
        c.priority=(c.gross_removed-repair_weight*c.broken_support_jumps+0.25)/c.rank_divisor*c.rank_noise;
        c.score_generation=trajectory_generation;++score_refreshes;
    }
    // 支持不足になる残存便を数える。修復負担の代理値であり、追加手数の下界ではない。
    int broken_support_jumps_value(const vector<int>& ids) const {
        array<bool,max_cells> removed{};
        for(int id:ids)removed[id]=true;
        int broken=0;
        for(const auto& use:support_uses) {
            int lost=0;
            for(int j=0;j<use.lower.n;j++)lost+=removed[use.lower.id[j]];
            if(lost<=use.slack)continue;
            const auto& flight=flights[use.time];
            for(int j=0;j<flight.n;j++)if(!removed[flight.id[j]]){++broken;break;}
        }
        return broken;
    }
    // 壊れる便の乗客を除去集合へ追加し、最大8匹まで依存を追う。
    int grow_dependency(Removal& cand) const {
        if(cand.ids.size()>=8)return 0;
        array<bool,max_cells> removed{};
        for(int id:cand.ids)removed[id]=true;
        int rounds=0;
        while(cand.ids.size()<8) {
            int chosen=-1,best_added=9,best_length=-1;
            for(int i=0;i<int(support_uses.size());i++) {
                const auto& use=support_uses[i];
                int lost=0;
                for(int j=0;j<use.lower.n;j++)lost+=removed[use.lower.id[j]];
                if(lost<=use.slack)continue;
                const auto& flight=flights[use.time];
                int added=0;
                for(int j=0;j<flight.n;j++)added+=!removed[flight.id[j]];
                if(!added||cand.ids.size()+added>8)continue;
                const int length=int(use.lower.n)+1-use.slack;
                if(added<best_added||(added==best_added&&length>best_length)) {
                    chosen=i;best_added=added;best_length=length;
                }
            }
            if(chosen<0)break;
            const auto& flight=flights[support_uses[chosen].time];
            for(int j=0;j<flight.n;j++)if(!removed[flight.id[j]]) {
                removed[flight.id[j]]=true;cand.ids.push_back(flight.id[j]);
            }
            ++rounds;
        }
        if(rounds) {
            sort(cand.ids.begin(),cand.ids.end());
            cand.hash=0x51ed270b3a4dc107ULL;
            for(int id:cand.ids)cand.hash=(cand.hash^(id+1))*0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
        }
        return rounds;
    }
// NN特徴にも使うため、提出ビルドでも有効にする。
    int removed_commands(const vector<int>& ids) const {
        array<bool,max_cells> removed{};for(int id:ids)removed[id]=true;
        int count=0;
        for(const auto& flight:flights) {
            bool all=true;
            for(int j=0;j<flight.n;j++)all&=removed[flight.id[j]];
            count+=all;
        }
        return count;
    }

    static bool better(int cost,int bonus,const Label& b) {
        return cost<b.cost||(cost==b.cost&&bonus>b.bonus);
    }
    // Gaps at the two sides of a background slime of color_code yield the
    // same physical tower. Transitivity holds only through consecutive equal
    // colors; it must NOT cross a differently colored slime. A copied path is
    // valid because its entire colored board, not merely the multiset, agrees.
    // At event boundaries all gaps matter. Between events only the top gap
    // can make an extra move, so promoting that representative is sufficient.
    void close_color_gaps(int p,TowerBits word,int h,int color_code,bool top_only) {
        ++alias_closures;
        if(h==0||h>=8)return;
        Label* labels=small_labels.data()+8*p;
        if(top_only) {
            if(int((word>>(4*(h-1)))&15)!=color_code)return;
            int first=h-1;
            while(first>0&&int((word>>(4*(first-1)))&15)==color_code)--first;
            Label best=labels[h];
            for(int g=first;g<h;g++)if(better(labels[g].cost,labels[g].bonus,best))best=labels[g];
            if(better(best.cost,best.bonus,labels[h])) {
                alias_cost_relaxations+=best.cost<labels[h].cost;
                ++alias_top_relaxations;labels[h]=best;
            }
            return;
        }
        // Each nibble is independent: (d & 7)+7 has no carry into its next
        // nibble. A high bit is zero iff the original nibble was zero.
        const TowerBits diff=word^(0x11111111u*unsigned(color_code));
        TowerBits matches=~(((diff&0x77777777u)+0x77777777u)|diff)&0x88888888u;
        while(matches) {
            const int first=__builtin_ctz(matches)/4;
            int last=first+1;
            while(last<h&&int((word>>(4*last))&15)==color_code)++last;
            Label best=labels[first];
            for(int g=first+1;g<=last;g++)if(better(labels[g].cost,labels[g].bonus,best))best=labels[g];
            for(int g=first;g<=last;g++)if(better(best.cost,best.bonus,labels[g])) {
                alias_cost_relaxations+=best.cost<labels[g].cost;
                ++alias_gap_relaxations;labels[g]=best;
            }
            matches&=~tower_mask[last];
        }
    }

    // 元の段と匹数を保持する。背景へ同色で接触してもこの単位へ取り込まない。
    struct RunMember {int16_t source;uint8_t rank,shade,count;};
    static int append_runs(array<RunMember,8>& members,int n,TowerBits word,int source,int rank) {
        while(word) {
            const int shade=word&15;int count=0;
            do {++count;word>>=4;} while(word&&int(word&15)==shade);
            members[n++]={int16_t(source),uint8_t(rank),uint8_t(shade),uint8_t(count)};
            rank+=count;
        }
        return n;
    }
    static TowerBits run_word(const RunMember& m) {
        return prefix(0x11111111u*m.shade,m.count);
    }
    static TowerBits insert_run_word(TowerBits w,int gap,const RunMember& m) {
        // 上端が8段のときも、32 bit幅のシフトを避けて元の順序を復元する。
        const uint64_t upper=(uint64_t(w)>>(4*gap))<<(4*(gap+m.count));
        return TowerBits(prefix(w,gap)|(uint64_t(run_word(m))<<(4*gap))|upper);
    }
    int link(int prev,int time,Move m,bool extra) {
        links.push_back({prev,time,m,extra});return int(links.size())-1;
    }
    vector<Move> reconstruct(const vector<Move>& base,int ptr) const {
        vector<int> path;
        while(ptr>=0){path.push_back(ptr);ptr=links[ptr].previous;}
        reverse(path.begin(),path.end());
        vector<Move> out;out.reserve(base.size()+path.size());
        int j=0;
        for(int t=0;t<=int(base.size());t++) {
            while(j<int(path.size())&&links[path[j]].time==t&&links[path[j]].extra)
                out.push_back(links[path[j++]].move);
            if(t<int(base.size())) {
                if(j<int(path.size())&&links[path[j]].time==t&&!links[path[j]].extra)
                    out.push_back(links[path[j++]].move);
                else out.push_back(base[t]);
            }
        }
        if(j!=int(path.size()))throw logic_error("reinsertion chronology");
        return out;
    }

    // 個体の除去で壊れたジャンプを修復し、残存便の色順を保つ。露出した巣上の個体は先に帰巣できる。
    bool extract(const vector<Move>& original,const vector<int>& removed,
                 vector<Move>& base,State& initial) {
        IdentityState cur(removed);initial=cur.words();
        return extract_from(original,cur,0,base);
    }
    bool extract_from(const vector<Move>& original,IdentityState& cur,int start,
                     vector<Move>& base) {
        base.clear();base.reserve(original.size());
        for(int t=start;t<int(original.size());t++) {
            if((t&63)==0)time_keeper.check();
            Move m=original[t];int p=m.p,q=board_info.ray[p][m.d][m.l];
            array<int,8> flight;int take=0;
            for(int i=0;i<flights[t].n;i++) {
                int id=flights[t].id[i];if(cur.alive[id])flight[take++]=id;
            }
            if(take) {
                int keep=int(cur.h[p])-take;
                if(keep<0)return false;
                for(int i=0;i<take;i++)if(cur.a[p][keep+i]!=flight[i])return false;
                Move adjusted=m;adjusted.k=keep;
                if(m.l<=keep+1&&cur.h[q]+take<=8) {
                    cur.apply(adjusted);base.push_back(adjusted);
                }else {
                    State before=cur.words();PortionRouter rt;rt.run(before,p,keep,q);
                    TowerBits moving=before[p]>>(4*keep);bool mono=mono_color_code(moving)>0;
                    int orientation=1;
                    if(mono&&rt.cost(q,0)>=0&&(rt.cost(q,1)<0||rt.cost(q,0)<rt.cost(q,1)))orientation=0;
                    if(rt.cost(q,orientation)<0)return false;
                    auto path=rt.path(q,orientation);
                    LOCAL_NOTE(if(local_dependency_extract){
                        ++local_dependency.repaired_jumps;
                        local_dependency.repair_extra_commands+=int(path.size())-1;
                    })
                    for(auto z:path){cur.apply(z);base.push_back(z);}
                    if(mono&&orientation==0) {
                        // 同色の個体を交換し、後の分割で使うIDの割り当てを保つ。
                        bool all_alive=true;for(int i=0;i<take;i++)all_alive&=cur.alive[flight[i]];
                        if(all_alive) {
                            if(cur.h[q]<take)return false;
                            for(int i=0;i<take;i++)cur.a[q][cur.h[q]-take+i]=flight[take-1-i];
                        }
                    }
                }
                if(base.size()>original.size()-start+4)return false;
            }
        }
        for(int p=0;p<board_info.cell_count;p++)if(cur.h[p])return false;
        return true;
    }

    // 同費用なら、次の同色2便を1操作へまとめられる支持を優先する。
    // 短縮後に操作の合法性と境界の全盤面一致を検査する。
    static int shortcut_support_need(const State& b,const vector<Move>& base,int t,int q,int hq) {
        if(t+1>=int(base.size()))return infinite_cost;
        const Move& a=base[t];const Move& z=base[t+1];
        if(z.p!=q||z.d!=a.d||z.k!=hq)return infinite_cost;
        const int shade=mono_color_code(b[a.p]>>(4*a.k));
        if(shade<=0||board_info.nest_code[q]==shade)return infinite_cost;
        return max(1,int(a.l)+z.l-a.k-1);
    }

    // 固定した予定への1匹の再挿入。状態はマスと塔内の隙間で、追加移動は費用1、予定操作は費用0。
    // 同費用では同乗と踏み台の利用を優先する。
    bool insert_at(const State& initial,const vector<Move>& base,int source,int color_code,
                  int initial_gap,int cap,vector<Move>& result) {
        LOCAL_NOTE(++local_single_insert_calls;)
        if(cap<0||source<0||source>=board_info.cell_count)return false;
        int cell_count=board_info.cell_count,GOAL=8*cell_count,S=GOAL+1;
        State b=initial;
        if(height(b[source])>=8||initial_gap<0||initial_gap>height(b[source]))return false;
        // 初期の隙間を指定し、任意の挿入順から元の塔の積み順を復元する。
        small_labels.assign(S,Label());auto& label=small_labels;
        links.clear();links.reserve(32768);
        label[source*8+initial_gap]={0,0,-1};
        auto& queue=small_queue;queue.prepare(cell_count);queue.reset(cap);
        auto ceiling=[&](){return min(cap,label[GOAL].cost);};
        array<int,max_cells> h{};for(int p=0;p<cell_count;p++)h[p]=height(b[p]);
        auto seed=[&](int p) {
            if(p<0||h[p]>=8)return;
            int s=p*8+h[p];auto z=label[s];
            if(z.cost<=ceiling())queue.push(p,z.cost);
        };
        auto seed_neighborhood=[&](int p,bool changed) {
            seed(p);
            if(!changed)return;
            for(int d=0;d<4;d++)for(int l=1;l<=board_info.ray_count[p][d];l++) {
                int q=board_info.ray[p][d][l];
                if(h[q]<8&&l<=h[q]+1)seed(q);
            }
        };
        close_color_gaps(source,b[source],h[source],color_code,true);
        seed(source);
        for(int t=0;t<=int(base.size());t++) {
            if((t&31)==0)time_keeper.check();
            int pops=0,queued_position,queued_cost;
            while(queue.pop(ceiling(),queued_position,queued_cost)) {
                int p=queued_position,s=p*8+h[p];
                Label at=label[s];
                if(at.cost!=queued_cost||h[p]>=8)continue;
                if(at.cost>=ceiling())continue;
                int next_cost=at.cost+1;
                for(int d=0;d<4;d++)for(int l=1,far=min(h[p]+1,int(board_info.ray_count[p][d]));l<=far;l++) {
                    int q=board_info.ray[p][d][l];
                    if(h[q]>=8)continue;
                    int ns=board_info.nest_code[q]==color_code?GOAL:q*8+h[q];
                    if(better(next_cost,at.bonus,label[ns])) {
                        Move m={int16_t(p),uint8_t(h[p]),uint8_t(d),uint8_t(l)};
                        int ptr=link(at.path,t,m,true);
                        label[ns]={next_cost,at.bonus,ptr};
                        if(ns!=GOAL)queue.push(q,next_cost);
                    }
                }
                if((++pops&1023)==0)time_keeper.check();
            }
            if(t==int(base.size()))break;
            queue.reset(ceiling());
            Move m=base[t];int p=m.p,q=board_info.ray[p][m.d][m.l];
            int hp=h[p],hq=h[q];
            const int support_need=support_weight?shortcut_support_need(b,base,t,q,hq):infinite_cost;
            close_color_gaps(p,b[p],hp,color_code,false);
            close_color_gaps(q,b[q],hq,color_code,false);
            array<Label,8> from_p,from_q;
            for(int g=0;g<8;g++) {
                from_p[g]=label[p*8+g];from_q[g]=label[q*8+g];
                label[p*8+g]=Label();label[q*8+g]=Label();
            }
            board_info.apply(b,m);h[p]=height(b[p]);h[q]=height(b[q]);
            // 追加個体が帰巣へ影響するのは、残る背景の最上段以上にいる場合だけ。
            // 位置を式で求め、各隙間での塔の再構築を省く。容量は帰巣前の高さで判定する。
            auto transition=[&](int location,int gap,int added_keep,const Label& at) {
                if(at.cost>ceiling())return;
                const bool held=location==p&&added_keep;
                if(!held&&hq+hp-m.k+1>8)return;
                const int tag_cell=held?p:q;
                const int tag_index=location==p&&!held?hq+hp-gap:gap;
                const bool gone=board_info.nest_code[tag_cell]==color_code&&tag_index>=h[tag_cell];
                int bonus=at.bonus;
                if(location==p)bonus+=held?support_weight:ride_weight;
                if(held&&support_need<=1)bonus+=3*support_weight;
                LOCAL_NOTE(local_shortcut_support_states += held && support_need <= 1;)
                Move adjusted=m;adjusted.k+=added_keep;
                if(gone||tag_index<=h[tag_cell]) {
                    const int ns=gone?GOAL:tag_cell*8+tag_index;
                    if(!gone&&h[tag_cell]>=8)return;
                    if(better(at.cost,bonus,label[ns])) {
                        int ptr=at.path;
                        if(added_keep)ptr=link(ptr,t,adjusted,false);
                        label[ns]={at.cost,bonus,ptr};
                    }
                }else if(at.cost<ceiling()) {
                    // 背景の帰巣を止める異色の追加個体は、即座に出発すれば予定を復元でき、下段も支持に使える。
                    const int keep=tag_index,new_cost=at.cost+1;
                    const int nb=bonus+3*keep;
                    int ptr=at.path;
                    if(added_keep)ptr=link(ptr,t,adjusted,false);
                    for(int d=0;d<4;d++)for(int l=1,far=min(keep+1,int(board_info.ray_count[tag_cell][d]));l<=far;l++) {
                        const int dest=board_info.ray[tag_cell][d][l];
                        if(h[dest]>=8)continue;
                        const int ns=board_info.nest_code[dest]==color_code?GOAL:dest*8+h[dest];
                        if(!better(new_cost,nb,label[ns]))continue;
                        Move escape={int16_t(tag_cell),uint8_t(keep),uint8_t(d),uint8_t(l)};
                        int np=link(ptr,t+1,escape,true);
                        label[ns]={new_cost,nb,np};
                        if(ns!=GOAL)queue.push(dest,new_cost);
                    }
                }
            };
            if(hp<8)for(int g=0;g<=hp;g++) {
                if(g>=m.k)transition(p,g,0,from_p[g]);
                if(g<=m.k)transition(p,g,1,from_p[g]);
            }
            if(hq<8)for(int g=0;g<=hq;g++)transition(q,g,0,from_q[g]);
            close_color_gaps(p,b[p],h[p],color_code,true);
            close_color_gaps(q,b[q],h[q],color_code,true);
            // 変更された2マスとそこへ入る辺を開き直す。巣の帰巣で最上段の状態が悪化した場合は、
            // 高さが同じでも再探索が必要。他の状態はそのまま待機できる。
            bool reopen_p=h[p]!=hp,reopen_q=h[q]!=hq;
            if(hp<8&&!reopen_p)reopen_p=better(from_p[hp].cost,from_p[hp].bonus,label[p*8+hp]);
            if(hq<8&&!reopen_q)reopen_q=better(from_q[hq].cost,from_q[hq].bonus,label[q*8+hq]);
            LOCAL_NOTE(++local_event_layers; local_event_reopens += reopen_p + reopen_q;)
            seed_neighborhood(p,reopen_p);seed_neighborhood(q,reopen_q);
        }
        if(label[GOAL].cost>cap)return false;
        result=reconstruct(base,label[GOAL].path);
        if(int(result.size())!=int(base.size())+label[GOAL].cost)
            throw logic_error("reinsertion cost mismatch");
        return true;
    }

    bool insert(const State& initial,const vector<Move>& base,int token,int cap,
                vector<Move>& result) {
        if(initial[token])throw logic_error("insertion initial cell occupied");
        return insert_at(initial,base,token,int(board_info.initial[token]),0,cap,result);
    }

    // 第2順序では先頭2匹だけを交換する。後続の個体は、それぞれで作られた経路へ挿入する。
    bool insert_two_orders(const State& initial,const vector<Move>& base,
                         const vector<pair<double,int>>& order,int allowance,
                         vector<Move>& result) {
        const int n=int(order.size());assert(2<=n&&n<=4);
        LOCAL_NOTE(
            ++local_two_order.attempts;++local_two_order.sizes[n];
            local_two_order_alternate=false;
        )
        int best_length_value=allowance+1;
        for(int branch=0;branch<2;branch++) {
            LOCAL_NOTE(++local_two_order.started[branch];TwoOrderTimer timer(branch);)
            State restored=initial;vector<Move> tail=base;bool ok=true;
            for(int j=0;j<n;j++) {
                time_keeper.check();
                const int at=branch&&j<2?1-j:j,id=order[at].second;
                vector<Move> next;
                // 挿入では操作数が増えるため、完成済みの解より短くならない第2順序を枝刈りする。
                const int cap=min(allowance,best_length_value-1)-int(tail.size());
                LOCAL_NOTE(++local_two_order.insert_calls[branch];)
                if(!insert(restored,tail,id,cap,next)){ok=false;break;}
                ++insertions;tail=move(next);restored[id]=board_info.initial[id];
            }
            if(!ok){LOCAL_NOTE(++local_two_order.failed[branch];)continue;}
            LOCAL_NOTE(
                ++local_two_order.finished[branch];
                if(branch) {
                    ++local_two_order.alternate_selected;local_two_order_alternate=true;
                    if(best_length_value<=allowance) {
                        ++local_two_order.shorter;
                        local_two_order.raw_saved+=best_length_value-int(tail.size());
                    }else ++local_two_order.rescued;
                }
            )
            best_length_value=int(tail.size());result=move(tail);
        }
        const bool ok=best_length_value<=allowance;
        LOCAL_NOTE(local_two_order.completed+=ok;)
        return ok;
    }

    // 連続した束を一単位で再挿入する。状態は上下反転と最上段の帰巣で到達する列だけに絞る。
    bool insert_packet(const State& initial,const vector<Move>& base,int source,
                      TowerBits packet,int cap,vector<Move>& result,int initial_gap=-1) {
        LOCAL_NOTE(++local_packet_insert_calls;)
        if(cap<0||!packet)return false;
        vector<TowerBits> words;unordered_map<TowerBits,int> index;
        auto add_word=[&](TowerBits w) {
            if(!w||index.count(w))return;
            int id=words.size();index.emplace(w,id);words.push_back(w);
        };
        add_word(packet);
        for(int i=0;i<int(words.size());i++) {
            TowerBits w=words[i];add_word(reverse_tower(w));
            add_word(board_info.normalize(w,board_info.nest_pos_by_code[top_color_code(w)]));
        }
        int W=words.size(),cell_count=board_info.cell_count,stride=cell_count*8,GOAL=W*stride;
        vector<int> sz(W),rev(W);vector<array<int,13>> trimmed(W);
        for(int i=0;i<W;i++) {
            sz[i]=height(words[i]);rev[i]=index.at(reverse_tower(words[i]));
            for(int current_code=0;current_code<=board_info.K;current_code++) {
                TowerBits w=current_code?board_info.normalize(words[i],board_info.nest_pos_by_code[current_code]):words[i];
                trimmed[i][current_code]=w?index.at(w):-1;
            }
        }
        State b=initial;array<int,max_cells> h{};
        for(int p=0;p<cell_count;p++)h[p]=height(b[p]);
        if(h[source]+height(packet)>8||board_info.normalize(b[source],source)!=b[source])return false;
        packet_labels.assign(GOAL+1,Label());auto& labels=packet_labels;
        // 途中再構築では元の段順を保つため、既に戻した塊の下にも挿入する。
        const int gap=initial_gap<0?h[source]:initial_gap;
        if(gap>h[source])return false;
        labels[source*8+gap]={0,0,-1};
        links.clear();links.reserve(32768);
        auto& queue=packet_queue;queue.prepare(W*cell_count);queue.reset(cap);
        auto ceiling=[&](){return min(cap,labels[GOAL].cost);};
        auto seed=[&](int p) {
            if(p<0)return;
            for(int i=0;i<W;i++)if(h[p]+sz[i]<=8) {
                const Label& a=labels[i*stride+p*8+h[p]];
                if(a.cost<=ceiling())queue.push(i*cell_count+p,a.cost);
            }
        };
        auto seed_neighborhood=[&](int p,bool changed) {
            seed(p);if(!changed)return;
            for(int d=0;d<4;d++)for(int l=1;l<=8;l++) {
                int q=board_info.ray[p][d][l];if(q<0)break;
                if(l<=h[q]+1)seed(q);
            }
        };
        packet_from_p.resize(W);packet_from_q.resize(W);
        auto& from_p=packet_from_p;auto& from_q=packet_from_q;
        seed(source);
        for(int t=0;t<=int(base.size());t++) {
            if((t&15)==0)time_keeper.check();
            int state,queued_cost,pops=0;
            while(queue.pop(ceiling(),state,queued_cost)) {
                int i=state/cell_count,p=state%cell_count,n=sz[i];
                if(h[p]+n>8)continue;
                Label at=labels[i*stride+p*8+h[p]];
                if(at.cost!=queued_cost||at.cost>=ceiling())continue;
                int nc=at.cost+1,ri=rev[i];
                for(int d=0;d<4;d++)for(int l=1,far=min(h[p]+1,int(board_info.ray_count[p][d]));l<=far;l++) {
                    int q=board_info.ray[p][d][l];
                    if(h[q]+n>8)continue;
                    int ni=trimmed[ri][board_info.nest_code[q]];
                    int ns=ni<0?GOAL:ni*stride+q*8+h[q];
                    if(!better(nc,at.bonus,labels[ns]))continue;
                    Move m={int16_t(p),uint8_t(h[p]),uint8_t(d),uint8_t(l)};
                    labels[ns]={nc,at.bonus,link(at.path,t,m,true)};
                    if(ni>=0)queue.push(ni*cell_count+q,nc);
                }
                if((++pops&2047)==0)time_keeper.check();
            }
            if(t==int(base.size()))break;
            queue.reset(ceiling());
            Move m=base[t];int p=m.p,q=board_info.ray[p][m.d][m.l];
            int hp=h[p],hq=h[q];
            const int support_need=support_weight?shortcut_support_need(b,base,t,q,hq):infinite_cost;
            for(int i=0;i<W;i++)for(int g=0;g<8;g++) {
                from_p[i][g]=labels[i*stride+p*8+g];
                from_q[i][g]=labels[i*stride+q*8+g];
                labels[i*stride+p*8+g]=Label();
                labels[i*stride+q*8+g]=Label();
            }
            board_info.apply(b,m);h[p]=height(b[p]);h[q]=height(b[q]);
            auto transition=[&](int i,int location,int gap,bool retained,const Label& at) {
                if(at.cost>ceiling())return;
                const int n=sz[i];
                const bool held=location==p&&retained;
                if(!held&&hq+hp-m.k+n>8)return;
                const int add=held?n:0;
                const int tag_cell=held?p:q;
                const int tag_index=location==p&&!held?hq+hp-gap:gap;
                const int wi=location==p&&!held?rev[i]:i;
                // 束の上に背景の異色があれば帰巣を止める。なければ束の最上段の同色部分だけを帰す。
                const int ni=tag_index<h[tag_cell]?wi:trimmed[wi][board_info.nest_code[tag_cell]];
                const int remain=ni<0?0:sz[ni];
                int bonus=at.bonus;
                if(location==p)bonus+=(held?support_weight:ride_weight)*n;
                if(held&&support_need<=n)bonus+=3*support_weight;
                LOCAL_NOTE(local_shortcut_support_states += held && support_need <= n;)
                Move adj=m;adj.k+=add;
                if(!remain||tag_index<=h[tag_cell]) {
                    const int ns=ni<0?GOAL:ni*stride+tag_cell*8+tag_index;
                    if(ni>=0&&h[tag_cell]+remain>8)return;
                    if(better(at.cost,bonus,labels[ns])) {
                        int ptr=at.path;if(add)ptr=link(ptr,t,adj,false);
                        labels[ns]={at.cost,bonus,ptr};
                    }
                }else if(at.cost<ceiling()) {
                    const int nc=at.cost+1,nb=bonus+3*tag_index;
                    int ptr=at.path;if(add)ptr=link(ptr,t,adj,false);
                    const int ri=rev[ni];
                    for(int d=0;d<4;d++)for(int l=1,far=min(tag_index+1,int(board_info.ray_count[tag_cell][d]));l<=far;l++) {
                        const int dest=board_info.ray[tag_cell][d][l];
                        if(h[dest]+remain>8)continue;
                        const int next=trimmed[ri][board_info.nest_code[dest]];
                        const int ns=next<0?GOAL:next*stride+dest*8+h[dest];
                        if(!better(nc,nb,labels[ns]))continue;
                        Move escape={int16_t(tag_cell),uint8_t(tag_index),uint8_t(d),uint8_t(l)};
                        labels[ns]={nc,nb,link(ptr,t+1,escape,true)};
                        if(next>=0)queue.push(next*cell_count+dest,nc);
                    }
                }
            };
            for(int i=0;i<W;i++) {
                if(hp+sz[i]<=8)for(int g=0;g<=hp;g++) {
                    if(g>=m.k)transition(i,p,g,false,from_p[i][g]);
                    if(g<=m.k)transition(i,p,g,true,from_p[i][g]);
                }
                if(hq+sz[i]<=8)for(int g=0;g<=hq;g++)transition(i,q,g,false,from_q[i][g]);
            }
            bool reopen_p=h[p]!=hp,reopen_q=h[q]!=hq;
            for(int i=0;i<W;i++) {
                if(!reopen_p&&hp+sz[i]<=8)
                    reopen_p=better(from_p[i][hp].cost,from_p[i][hp].bonus,labels[i*stride+p*8+hp]);
                if(!reopen_q&&hq+sz[i]<=8)
                    reopen_q=better(from_q[i][hq].cost,from_q[i][hq].bonus,labels[i*stride+q*8+hq]);
            }
            LOCAL_NOTE(++local_event_layers; local_event_reopens += reopen_p + reopen_q;)
            seed_neighborhood(p,reopen_p);seed_neighborhood(q,reopen_q);
        }
        if(labels[GOAL].cost>cap)return false;
        result=reconstruct(base,labels[GOAL].path);
        if(int(result.size())!=int(base.size())+labels[GOAL].cost)
            throw logic_error("packet reinsertion cost mismatch");
        return true;
    }

    bool packet_neighbor(const vector<Move>& current,const PacketCut& cut,int slack,
                        vector<Move>& result) {
        IdentityState cur=checkpoint_at(current,cut.time);
        int source=current[cut.time].p;
        TowerBits before=cur.word(source),packet=before>>(4*cut.keep);
        if(height(packet)<2||board_info.normalize(prefix(before,cut.keep),source)!=prefix(before,cut.keep))return false;
        for(int i=cut.keep;i<cur.h[source];i++)cur.alive[cur.a[source][i]]=0;
        cur.h[source]=cut.keep;State initial=cur.words();
        vector<Move> base;
        if(!extract_from(current,cur,cut.time,base))return false;
        int cap=int(current.size())-cut.time+slack-int(base.size());
        vector<Move> tail;
        if(!insert_packet(initial,base,source,packet,cap,tail))return false;
        result.assign(current.begin(),current.begin()+cut.time);
        result.insert(result.end(),tail.begin(),tail.end());
        return true;
    }

    IdentityState checkpoint_at(const vector<Move>& current,int time) const {
        IdentityState b=checkpoints[time/CHECKPOINT];
        for(int t=time/CHECKPOINT*CHECKPOINT;t<time;t++)b.apply(current[t]);
        return b;
    }

    bool insert_run(const State& initial,const vector<Move>& base,const RunMember& m,
                    int gap,int cap,vector<Move>& result) {
        LOCAL_NOTE(
            ++local_run_reinsert_calls;local_run_reinserted_slimes+=m.count;
            local_run_multi_calls+=m.count>1;
        )
        const bool ok=m.count==1?insert_at(initial,base,m.source,m.shade,gap,cap,result):
            insert_packet(initial,base,m.source,run_word(m),cap,result,gap);
        LOCAL_NOTE(
            local_run_completed+=ok;local_run_completed_slimes+=ok?m.count:0;
            local_run_multi_completed+=ok&&m.count>1;
        )
        return ok;
    }

    // 選択した上側だけを同色の塊へまとめる。切出し位置と背景の分割は親と同じ自由度を持つ。
    bool flexible_neighbor(const vector<Move>& current,const PacketCut& cut,int slack,
                          vector<Move>& result) {
        LOCAL_NOTE(++local_run_flexible_calls;)
        IdentityState cur=checkpoint_at(current,cut.time);
        int source=current[cut.time].p;
        TowerBits before=cur.word(source),packet=before>>(4*cut.keep);
        const int slime_count=height(packet);
        if(slime_count<2||slime_count>8||board_info.normalize(prefix(before,cut.keep),source)!=prefix(before,cut.keep))return false;
        array<RunMember,8> members{};
        const int n=append_runs(members,0,packet,source,cut.keep);
        for(int i=cut.keep;i<cur.h[source];i++)cur.alive[cur.a[source][i]]=0;
        cur.h[source]=uint8_t(cut.keep);
        const State initial=cur.words();
        vector<Move> skeleton;
        if(!extract_from(current,cur,cut.time,skeleton))return false;
        const int allowance=int(current.size())-cut.time+slack;
        int best_length_value=allowance+1;
        vector<Move> best_tail;
        // 追加順序の匹数条件は親を維持する。1塊では同じ順序の再試行を省く。
        int mode=rng(4),rounds=n>1&&slime_count<=4?2:1;
        for(int round=0;round<rounds;round++) {
            time_keeper.check();
            array<int,8> order{};
            iota(order.begin(),order.begin()+n,0);
            int style=(mode+round)%4;
            if(style==0)reverse(order.begin(),order.begin()+n);
            else if(style==2) {
                stable_sort(order.begin(),order.begin()+n,[&](int a,int b){
                    return board_info.floor_dist[source][board_info.nest_pos_by_code[members[a].shade]]>
                           board_info.floor_dist[source][board_info.nest_pos_by_code[members[b].shade]];
                });
            }else if(style==3) {
                for(int j=n-1;j>0;j--)swap(order[j],order[rng(j+1)]);
            }
            // 巣では帰巣しない元の最上段の塊を先に戻す。
            if(board_info.nest_code[source]) {
                auto at=find(order.begin(),order.begin()+n,n-1);
                rotate(order.begin(),at,at+1);
            }
            State restored=initial;unsigned inserted=0;
            vector<Move> tail=skeleton;bool ok=true;
            for(int j=0;j<n;j++) {
                int rank=order[j],gap=cut.keep;
                for(int k=0;k<rank;k++)if(inserted>>k&1)gap+=members[k].count;
                TowerBits next_initial=insert_run_word(restored[source],gap,members[rank]);
                if(board_info.normalize(next_initial,source)!=next_initial){ok=false;break;}
                vector<Move> next;
                int cap=min(allowance,best_length_value-1)-int(tail.size());
                if(!insert_run(restored,tail,members[rank],gap,cap,next)){ok=false;break;}
                ++insertions;tail=move(next);restored[source]=next_initial;inserted|=1u<<rank;
            }
            if(ok&&restored[source]==before&&int(tail.size())<best_length_value) {
                best_length_value=int(tail.size());best_tail=move(tail);
            }
        }
        if(best_length_value>allowance)return false;
        result.assign(current.begin(),current.begin()+cut.time);
        result.insert(result.end(),best_tail.begin(),best_tail.end());
        return true;
    }

    // 同じ途中時点の2塔の上側を再構築する。各塊の出発点と元の段を保持し、
    // 開始盤面と既存の前半操作を保ったまま、その後の経路を作り直す。
    bool paired_neighbor(const vector<Move>& current,int slack,vector<Move>& result) {
        LOCAL_NOTE(local_late_paired_anchor=false;++local_run_paired_calls;)
        const int T=int(current.size());if(T<3)return false;
        int time;
        if(!packet_cuts.empty()&&rng(3)) {
            int z=rng(4)?min(rng(int(packet_cuts.size())),rng(int(packet_cuts.size()))):rng(int(packet_cuts.size()));
            time=packet_cuts[z].time;
            LOCAL_NOTE(
                local_late_paired_anchor=packet_cuts[z].later;
                local_late_start.attempts[2]+=local_late_paired_anchor;
            )
        } else {
            // 既に行った集荷を残しつつ、早い時点を優先する。
            time=min(rng(T),rng(T));
        }
        IdentityState cur=checkpoint_at(current,time);
        const State boundary=cur.words();
        int p=current[time].p,hp=cur.h[p];
        if(!hp)return false;
        const int total_limit=rng(4)?6:8;
        auto keep_for=[&](int cell,int maximum,bool randomize) {
            const int h=cur.h[cell];int ks[8],count=0;
            for(int k=max(0,h-maximum);k<h;k++)
                if(board_info.normalize(prefix(boundary[cell],k),cell)==prefix(boundary[cell],k))ks[count++]=k;
            if(!count)return -1;
            return ks[randomize?rng(count):0];
        };
        int keep_p=keep_for(p,min(hp,total_limit-1),rng(4)==0);
        if(keep_p<0)return false;
        int np=hp-keep_p;
        unsigned pc=unsigned(color_mask(boundary[p]>>(4*keep_p)));
        struct Partner {int cell,keep;double cost;};
        vector<Partner> partners;partners.reserve(board_info.M);
        int first_destination=board_info.ray[p][current[time].d][current[time].l];
        for(int q=0;q<board_info.cell_count;q++)if(q!=p&&cur.h[q]) {
            int distance=board_info.floor_dist[p][q];
            if(distance>10)continue;
            int k=keep_for(q,min(int(cur.h[q]),total_limit-np),false);
            if(k<0)continue;
            unsigned qc=unsigned(color_mask(boundary[q]>>(4*k)));
            int home_distance=infinite_cost;
            for(unsigned a=pc;a;a&=a-1)for(unsigned b=qc;b;b&=b-1) {
                int ca=1+__builtin_ctz(a),cb=1+__builtin_ctz(b);
                home_distance=min(home_distance,int(board_info.floor_dist[board_info.nest_pos_by_code[ca]][board_info.nest_pos_by_code[cb]]));
            }
            double score=distance+0.22*home_distance-0.45*(q==first_destination);
            partners.push_back({q,k,score});
        }
        if(partners.empty())return false;
        sort(partners.begin(),partners.end(),[](const Partner& a,const Partner& b){
            return a.cost<b.cost||(a.cost==b.cost&&a.cell<b.cell);
        });
        int pool=min(12,int(partners.size()));
        int at=rng(4)?min(rng(pool),rng(pool)):rng(int(partners.size()));
        int q=partners[at].cell,keep_q=partners[at].keep;
        if(rng(4)==0)keep_q=keep_for(q,total_limit-np,true);
        if(keep_q<0)return false;
        array<RunMember,8> members{};int n=0,slime_count=0;
        uint64_t key=0xb5ad4eceda1ce2a9ULL;
        for(auto [cell,keep]:{pair<int,int>{p,keep_p},pair<int,int>{q,keep_q}}) {
            n=append_runs(members,n,boundary[cell]>>(4*keep),cell,keep);
            slime_count+=cur.h[cell]-keep;
            for(int k=keep;k<cur.h[cell];k++) {
                int id=cur.a[cell][k];
                key=(key^uint64_t(id+1))*0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
                cur.alive[id]=0;
            }
            cur.h[cell]=uint8_t(keep);
        }
        auto seen=last_tried.find(key);
        if(seen!=last_tried.end()&&iteration-seen->second<24)return false;
        last_tried[key]=iteration;
        const State initial=cur.words();
        vector<Move> skeleton;
        if(!extract_from(current,cur,time,skeleton))return false;
        const int allowance=T-time+slack;
        int best_length_value=allowance+1;
        vector<Move> best_tail;
        const int mode=rng(5),rounds=slime_count<=3?2:1;
        for(int round=0;round<rounds;round++) {
            time_keeper.check();State restored=initial;
            vector<Move> tail=skeleton;
            unsigned inserted=0;bool ok=true;
            const int style=(mode+round)%5;
            array<double,8> priority{};
            for(int j=0;j<n;j++) {
                const auto& m=members[j];
                double d=board_info.floor_dist[m.source][board_info.nest_pos_by_code[m.shade]];
                if(style==0)priority[j]=d*(0.8+0.4*rng.unit());
                else if(style==1)priority[j]=-d+0.2*rng.unit();
                else if(style==2)priority[j]=rng.unit()*32;
                else if(style==3)priority[j]=(m.source==p?16:0)-m.rank;
                else priority[j]=(m.source==q?16:0)+m.rank;
            }
            for(int j=0;j<n;j++) {
                int who=-1;
                for(int k=0;k<n;k++)if(!(inserted>>k&1)) {
                    const auto& m=members[k];bool eligible=true;
                    // 巣では元の上側の塊から戻し、その下へ置く塊の帰巣を防ぐ。
                    if(board_info.nest_code[m.source])for(int z=0;z<n;z++)
                        if(members[z].source==m.source&&members[z].rank>m.rank&&!(inserted>>z&1))eligible=false;
                    if(eligible&&(who<0||priority[k]>priority[who]))who=k;
                }
                if(who<0){ok=false;break;}
                const auto& m=members[who];int gap=m.source==p?keep_p:keep_q;
                for(int k=0;k<n;k++)if((inserted>>k&1)&&members[k].source==m.source&&members[k].rank<m.rank)gap+=members[k].count;
                TowerBits next_initial=insert_run_word(restored[m.source],gap,m);
                if(board_info.normalize(next_initial,m.source)!=next_initial){ok=false;break;}
                vector<Move> next;
                const int cap=min(allowance,best_length_value-1)-int(tail.size());
                if(!insert_run(restored,tail,m,gap,cap,next)){ok=false;break;}
                ++insertions;restored[m.source]=next_initial;tail=move(next);inserted|=1u<<who;
            }
            if(ok&&restored==boundary&&int(tail.size())<best_length_value) {
                best_length_value=int(tail.size());best_tail=move(tail);
            }
        }
        if(best_length_value>allowance)return false;
        result.assign(current.begin(),current.begin()+time);
        result.insert(result.end(),best_tail.begin(),best_tail.end());
        return true;
    }

    static bool same_moves(const vector<Move>& a,const vector<Move>& b) {
        if(a.size()!=b.size())return false;
        for(size_t i=0;i<a.size();i++)
            if(a[i].p!=b[i].p||a[i].k!=b[i].k||a[i].d!=b[i].d||a[i].l!=b[i].l)return false;
        return true;
    }

    // 同じ端点へ触れる操作の順序を保つ。他の操作は交換できるため、
    // トポロジカル順序の変更で便をつなぎ、後の再挿入の同乗・支持機会を変える。
    vector<Move> causal_reorder(const vector<Move>& original,int style) {
        const int n=int(original.size());
        vector<array<int,2>> next(n,array<int,2>{-1,-1}),prev(n,array<int,2>{-1,-1});
        vector<uint8_t> degree(n),out_degree(n);
        vector<int> destination(n),ready;ready.reserve(n);
        array<int,max_cells> previous;previous.fill(-1);
        auto edge=[&](int a,int b) {
            if(a<0)return;
            next[a][out_degree[a]++]=b;prev[b][degree[b]++]=a;
        };
        for(int t=0;t<n;t++) {
            int p=original[t].p,q=destination[t]=board_info.ray[p][original[t].d][original[t].l];
            int a=previous[p],b=previous[q];edge(a,t);if(b!=a)edge(b,t);
            previous[p]=previous[q]=t;
        }
        const bool backward=style>=3;style%=3;
        vector<Move> out;out.reserve(n);
        State b=board_info.initial;
        if(backward) {
            vector<TowerBits> departing(n),landing(n);
            for(int t=0;t<n;t++) {
                const Move& m=original[t];departing[t]=b[m.p]>>(4*m.k);
                landing[t]=board_info.normalize(reverse_tower(departing[t]),destination[t]);
                board_info.apply(b,m);
                if(!out_degree[t])ready.push_back(t);
            }
            int next_cell=-1;TowerBits required=0;
            while(!ready.empty()) {
                if((out.size()&63)==0)time_keeper.check();
                int chosen=0;int64_t best_priority=LLONG_MIN;
                for(int j=0;j<int(ready.size());j++) {
                    int t=ready[j];
                    bool continuation=destination[t]==next_cell&&required&&landing[t]==required;
                    int64_t priority=continuation?1000000:0;
                    if(style==1)priority-=t;
                    else if(style==2)priority+=int(rng.next()&255)+2*t;
                    else priority+=t;
                    if(priority>best_priority){best_priority=priority;chosen=j;}
                }
                int t=ready[chosen];ready[chosen]=ready.back();ready.pop_back();
                out.push_back(original[t]);next_cell=original[t].p;required=departing[t];
                for(int j=0;j<degree[t];j++) {
                    int u=prev[t][j];if(--out_degree[u]==0)ready.push_back(u);
                }
            }
            reverse(out.begin(),out.end());b=board_info.initial;
            for(const Move& m:out)board_info.apply(b,m);
        } else {
            for(int t=0;t<n;t++)if(!degree[t])ready.push_back(t);
            int last_cell=-1;TowerBits travelling=0;
            while(!ready.empty()) {
                if((out.size()&63)==0)time_keeper.check();
                int chosen=0;int64_t best_priority=LLONG_MIN;
                for(int j=0;j<int(ready.size());j++) {
                    int t=ready[j];const Move& m=original[t];
                    bool continuation=m.p==last_cell&&travelling&&(b[m.p]>>(4*m.k))==travelling;
                    int64_t priority=continuation?1000000:0;
                    if(style==1)priority+=t;
                    else if(style==2)priority+=int(rng.next()&255)-2*t;
                    else priority-=t;
                    if(priority>best_priority){best_priority=priority;chosen=j;}
                }
                int t=ready[chosen];ready[chosen]=ready.back();ready.pop_back();
                Move m=original[t];TowerBits flying=b[m.p]>>(4*m.k);
                board_info.apply(b,m);out.push_back(m);
                last_cell=destination[t];travelling=board_info.normalize(reverse_tower(flying),last_cell);
                for(int j=0;j<out_degree[t];j++) {
                    int u=next[t][j];if(--degree[u]==0)ready.push_back(u);
                }
            }
        }
        if(int(out.size())!=n||board_info.count(b))throw logic_error("causal reorder invariant");
        return out;
    }

    void initialize_spatial_cuts() {
        vector<int> tokens;for(int p=0;p<board_info.cell_count;p++)if(board_info.initial[p])tokens.push_back(p);
        for(int p:tokens) {
            vector<pair<double,int>> near,same,different;
            for(int q:tokens)if(q!=p) {
                double d=board_info.floor_dist[p][q]+0.20*board_info.floor_dist[board_info.nest_pos_by_code[board_info.initial[p]]][board_info.nest_pos_by_code[board_info.initial[q]]];
                near.emplace_back(d,q);
                if(board_info.initial[p]==board_info.initial[q])same.emplace_back(d,q);
                else different.emplace_back(d,q);
            }
            for(auto* list:{&near,&same,&different}) {
                sort(list->begin(),list->end());vector<int> ids{p};
                int limit=list==&different?1:2;
                for(int j=0;j<min(limit,int(list->size()));j++) {
                    ids.push_back((*list)[j].second);spatial_cuts.push_back(ids);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    static uint64_t later_start_hash(uint64_t hash,int time) {
        // 開始時刻ごとに異なるキーを使い、別時刻の試行による再試行待ちを共有させない。
        return hash^((uint64_t(time)+1)*0x94d049bb133111ebULL);
    }
    void make_candidates(const vector<Move>& answer,bool refresh_regular=true) {
        if(refresh_regular)candidates.clear();
        packet_cuts.clear();flights.clear();checkpoints.clear();
        support_uses.clear();later_cuts.clear();
        flights.reserve(answer.size());checkpoints.reserve((answer.size()+CHECKPOINT-1)/CHECKPOINT);
        IdentityState board;
        // 各操作から最大8色列、便、下段、着地先との和集合を取り、空間・単独・同色候補を加える。
        if(refresh_regular)build_seen.reset(12*answer.size()+spatial_cuts.size()+board_info.M+board_info.K+64);
        build_packets.reset(3*answer.size()+16);build_patterns.reset(answer.size()+16);
        auto& patterns=pattern_buffer;int pattern_count=0;
        auto& anchored=pattern_anchors;
        for(int p=0;p<board_info.cell_count;p++)anchored[p].clear();
        cut_ids.clear();
        auto hash_of=[](const vector<int>& ids) {
            uint64_t hash=0x51ed270b3a4dc107ULL;
            for(int id:ids)hash=(hash^(id+1))*0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
            return hash;
        };
        auto add=[&](vector<int> ids) {
            if(!refresh_regular)return;
            if(ids.empty()||ids.size()>12)return;
            sort(ids.begin(),ids.end());ids.erase(unique(ids.begin(),ids.end()),ids.end());
            uint64_t hash=hash_of(ids);
            if(build_seen.put(hash,int(candidates.size())).second)candidates.push_back({move(ids),0,hash});
        };
        if(refresh_regular) {
        vector<int> colors[13];
        for(int p=0;p<board_info.cell_count;p++)if(board_info.initial[p]) {
            add({p});colors[board_info.initial[p]].push_back(p);
        }
        for(int current_code=1;current_code<=board_info.K;current_code++)add(colors[current_code]);
        for(const auto& ids:spatial_cuts)add(ids);
        }
        for(int t=0;t<int(answer.size());t++) {
            if((t&127)==0)time_keeper.check();
            if(t%CHECKPOINT==0)checkpoints.push_back(board);
            Move m=answer[t];int p=m.p;
            vector<int> flight;Flight f;
            flight.reserve(8);
            for(int i=m.k;i<board.h[p];i++) {
                int id=board.a[p][i];flight.push_back(id);f.id[f.n++]=id;
            }
            flights.push_back(f);if(refresh_regular)add(flight);
            if(m.l>1) {
                SupportUse use{{},t,int(m.k)+1-int(m.l)};
                for(int i=0;i<m.k;i++)use.lower.id[use.lower.n++]=board.a[p][i];
                support_uses.push_back(use);
            }
            sort(flight.begin(),flight.end());uint64_t fh=hash_of(flight);
            auto ins=build_patterns.put(fh,pattern_count);
            if(ins.second){
                const int at=pattern_count++;
                anchored[flight.front()].push_back(at);
                if(at==int(patterns.size()))patterns.emplace_back();
                auto& pat=patterns[at];pat.n=int(flight.size());
                copy(flight.begin(),flight.end(),pat.ids.begin());
                pat.times.clear();pat.times.push_back(t);
            }else patterns[ins.first].times.push_back(t);
            if(refresh_regular) {
            vector<int> run;
            for(int i=0;i<board.h[p];i++) {
                int id=board.a[p][i];
                if(!run.empty()&&board_info.initial[run.back()]!=board_info.initial[id]){add(run);run.clear();}
                run.push_back(id);
            }
            add(run);
            if(m.k){vector<int> lower;for(int i=0;i<m.k;i++)lower.push_back(board.a[p][i]);add(lower);}
            }
            // 塔全体、跳ぶ束、上側2匹を候補にする。再挿入時には個体へ分解できる。
            TowerBits word=board.word(p);
            for(int k:{0,int(m.k),int(board.h[p])-2}) {
                int n=int(board.h[p])-k;if(k<0||n<2)continue;
                if(board_info.normalize(prefix(word,k),p)!=prefix(word,k))continue;
                vector<int> ids;for(int i=k;i<board.h[p];i++)ids.push_back(board.a[p][i]);
                sort(ids.begin(),ids.end());uint64_t hash=hash_of(ids)^0xd1b54a32d192ed03ULL;
                const bool before_split=k<int(m.k);
                auto slot=build_packets.put(hash,int(packet_cuts.size()));
                if(slot.second){
                    packet_cuts.push_back({t,k,n,0,hash,0.0,false,before_split});
                    later_cuts.emplace_back();
                    array<int16_t,8> selected{};
                    for(int i=0;i<n;i++)selected[i]=int16_t(ids[i]);
                    cut_ids.push_back(selected);
                }else if(t>packet_cuts[slot.first].time) {
                    // 同じ個体集合に開始時刻を1枠追加する。分離する境界を優先し、同条件なら遅い時刻を選ぶ。
                    auto& late=later_cuts[slot.first];
                    if(late.time<0||before_split>late.before_split||
                       (before_split==late.before_split&&t>late.time))
                        late={t,k,n,0,later_start_hash(hash,t),0.0,true,before_split};
                }
            }
            // 同じ端点で相互作用した束の和集合を、位置の近さだけによらない除去候補にする。
            int dest=board_info.ray[p][m.d][m.l];
            if(refresh_regular&&board.h[dest]&&board.h[dest]+f.n<=8){
                vector<int> both=flight;
                for(int i=0;i<board.h[dest];i++)both.push_back(board.a[dest][i]);
                add(move(both));
            }
            board.apply(m);
        }
        // 最初の候補群を集めてから追加し、候補順と優先度の乱数消費順を固定する。
        const int primary_count=int(packet_cuts.size());
        LOCAL_NOTE(++local_late_start.builds;local_late_start.primary+=primary_count;)
        for(int i=0;i<primary_count;i++)if(later_cuts[i].time>=0) {
            LOCAL_NOTE(
                ++local_late_start.generated;
                local_late_start.split_generated+=later_cuts[i].before_split;
                local_late_start.delay_sum+=later_cuts[i].time-packet_cuts[i].time;
            )
            packet_cuts.push_back(later_cuts[i]);
            const auto ids=cut_ids[i];cut_ids.push_back(ids);
        }
        array<int,max_cells> member{};int stamp=0;
        // 各集合を最小IDへ登録する。除去対象に含まれる最小IDの集合だけを調べ、
        // 操作時刻の一覧から区間内の削除費用を求める。
        auto saved_by=[&](const auto& ids,int n,int start) {
            ++stamp;for(int j=0;j<n;j++)member[ids[j]]=stamp;
            int saved=0;
            for(int j=0;j<n;j++)for(int pi:anchored[ids[j]]) {
                const auto& p=patterns[pi];if(p.n>n)continue;
                bool all=true;
                for(int k=0;k<p.n;k++)if(member[p.ids[k]]!=stamp){all=false;break;}
                if(all)saved+=int(p.times.end()-lower_bound(p.times.begin(),p.times.end(),start));
            }
            return saved;
        };
        double divisor[13],packet_divisor[9];
        for(int n=1;n<=12;n++)divisor[n]=pow(double(n),removal_size_exponent);
        for(int n=1;n<=8;n++)packet_divisor[n]=pow(double(n),0.45);
        LOCAL_NOTE(const double repair_priority_start=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec();int repair_priority_penalized=0;)
        if(refresh_regular)for(auto& cand:candidates) {
            int saved=saved_by(cand.ids,int(cand.ids.size()),0);
            cand.broken_support_jumps=broken_support_jumps_value(cand.ids);
            // 候補ごとに乱数を1回引く。負の優先度も候補として保持する。
            const double noise=(1.0-priority_noise_amplitude)+2.0*priority_noise_amplitude*rng.unit();
            cand.priority=(saved-repair_weight*cand.broken_support_jumps+0.25)/divisor[cand.ids.size()]*noise;
            cand.gross_removed=saved;cand.rank_noise=noise;cand.rank_divisor=divisor[cand.ids.size()];
            cand.score_generation=trajectory_generation;
            LOCAL_NOTE(repair_priority_penalized+=cand.broken_support_jumps>0;)
        }
        LOCAL_NOTE(trace.count_by("lns_repair_priority_evaluated",int(candidates.size()));
                   trace.count_by("lns_repair_priority_penalized",repair_priority_penalized);
                   trace.add_time_ms("lns_repair_priority",(time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()-repair_priority_start)*1000.0);)
        for(size_t i=0;i<packet_cuts.size();i++) {
            auto& cut=packet_cuts[i];cut.saved=saved_by(cut_ids[i],cut.size,cut.time);
            cut.priority=(cut.saved+0.25)/packet_divisor[cut.size]*((1.0-priority_noise_amplitude)+2.0*priority_noise_amplitude*rng.unit());
        }
        if(refresh_regular)sort(candidates.begin(),candidates.end(),[](const Removal&a,const Removal&b){return a.priority>b.priority;});
        sort(packet_cuts.begin(),packet_cuts.end(),[](const PacketCut&a,const PacketCut&b){return a.priority>b.priority;});
        history_generation=packet_generation=trajectory_generation;
        if(refresh_regular){candidate_age=0;++full_builds;}else ++packet_only_builds;
    }

public:
    uint64_t full_builds=0,history_only_builds=0,packet_only_builds=0;
    uint64_t score_refreshes=0,stale_bank_selections=0;
    double full_build_seconds=0,history_only_seconds=0,packet_only_seconds=0;
    int attempts=0,insertions=0,accepted=0,improvements=0,shortcuts=0;
    int block_attempts=0,block_accepted=0,block_improvements=0,uphill=0,restarts=0;
    int flexible_attempts=0,flexible_accepted=0,flexible_improvements=0,flexible_saved=0;
    int reorder_attempts=0,reorder_improvements=0,reorder_saved=0,duplicates=0;
    int paired_attempts=0,paired_accepted=0,paired_improvements=0,paired_saved=0;
    int backward_attempts=0,backward_improvements=0,backward_saved=0;
    double paired_seconds=0;
    double block_seconds=0,build_seconds=0,started_at=0;
    array<double,32> nn_features(const Removal& c,const vector<Move>& current,const vector<Move>& best,
                                  int stagnant,double progress)const{
        array<double,32>x{};const double n=c.ids.size(),T=max<size_t>(1,current.size());
        x[0]=board_info.N;x[1]=board_info.K;x[2]=board_info.M;x[3]=board_info.cell_count;
        x[4]=double(board_info.M)/board_info.cell_count;x[5]=current.size();x[6]=best.size();
        x[7]=double(current.size())-double(best.size());x[8]=progress;x[9]=stagnant;
        x[10]=n;x[11]=n/max(1,board_info.M);
        array<int,13>colors{};array<bool,max_cells>selected{};
        double sum=0,lo=1e9,hi=0;
        for(int id:c.ids){
            selected[id]=true;int color=int(board_info.initial[id]);++colors[color];
            double d=board_info.floor_dist[id][board_info.nest_pos_by_code[color]];
            sum+=d;lo=min(lo,d);hi=max(hi,d);
        }
        for(int count:colors)if(count){++x[12];x[13]=max(x[13],count/n);x[14]-=(count/n)*log(count/n);}
        const bool fresh=c.score_generation==trajectory_generation;
        x[15]=fresh?c.gross_removed:removed_commands(c.ids);
        x[16]=fresh?c.broken_support_jumps:broken_support_jumps_value(c.ids);
        x[17]=x[15]/n;x[18]=x[16]/n;
        x[19]=fresh?c.priority:(x[15]-repair_weight*x[16]+0.25)/c.rank_divisor*c.rank_noise;x[20]=sum/n;x[21]=hi;x[22]=lo;
        int pairs=0;for(int a:c.ids)for(int b:c.ids)if(a<b){
            double d=board_info.floor_dist[a][b];x[23]+=d;x[24]=max(x[24],d);++pairs;
        }
        x[23]/=max(1,pairs);
        int first=-1,last=-1,used=0,passengers=0;
        for(int t=0;t<int(flights.size());t++){
            int count=0;for(int i=0;i<flights[t].n;i++)count+=selected[flights[t].id[i]];
            if(count){if(first<0)first=t;last=t;++used;passengers+=count;}
        }
        x[25]=first<0?0:first/T;x[26]=last<0?0:last/T;x[27]=first<0?0:(last-first)/T;
        x[28]=used/T;x[29]=passengers/n;x[30]=support_weight;x[31]=ride_weight;
        for(double value:x)if(!isfinite(value))throw logic_error("nonfinite teacher feature");
        return x;
    }
    vector<int> nn_eligible;
    array<int,32> nn_ranks{};
    int nn_sample(int cooldown) {
        nn_eligible.clear();
        for(int i=0;i<int(candidates.size());i++){
            auto it=last_tried.find(candidates[i].hash);
            if(it==last_tried.end()||iteration-it->second>cooldown)nn_eligible.push_back(i);
        }
        const int n=int(nn_eligible.size());
        if(n<=32){copy(nn_eligible.begin(),nn_eligible.end(),nn_ranks.begin());return n;}
        copy(nn_eligible.begin(),nn_eligible.begin()+16,nn_ranks.begin());
        const int rest=n-16;
        for(int band=0;band<16;band++){
            const int left=rest*band/16,right=rest*(band+1)/16;
            nn_ranks[16+band]=nn_eligible[16+(left+right-1)/2];
        }
        return 32;
    }
    int nn_choose(const vector<Move>& current,const vector<Move>& best,
                  int stagnant,double progress,int cooldown) {
        LOCAL_NOTE(const auto begin=chrono::steady_clock::now();)
        const int n=nn_sample(cooldown);
        if(n==0)throw logic_error("NN called without eligible candidates");
        array<array<double,32>,32> features;
        LOCAL_NOTE(const auto feature_begin=chrono::steady_clock::now();)
        for(int i=0;i<n;i++) {
            refresh_regular_score(candidates[nn_ranks[i]]);
            features[i]=nn_features(candidates[nn_ranks[i]],current,best,stagnant,progress);
        }
        LOCAL_NOTE(const auto inference_begin=chrono::steady_clock::now();)
        int choice=nn_ranks[0];float value=numeric_limits<float>::infinity();
        for(int i=0;i<n;i++){
            const float predicted=neural_rank::predict(features[i]);
            if(!isfinite(predicted))throw logic_error("nonfinite NN prediction");
            // ranksは元順位順。厳密な同点では最初の候補を保持する。
            if(predicted<value){value=predicted;choice=nn_ranks[i];}
        }
        LOCAL_NOTE(
            const auto finished=chrono::steady_clock::now();
            ++local_nn.calls;local_nn.scored+=n;local_nn.changed+=choice!=nn_ranks[0];local_nn.rank_sum+=choice;
            local_nn.feature_ms+=chrono::duration<double,milli>(inference_begin-feature_begin).count();
            local_nn.inference_ms+=chrono::duration<double,milli>(finished-inference_begin).count();
            local_nn.total_ms+=chrono::duration<double,milli>(finished-begin).count();
        )
        return choice;
    }

    uint64_t alias_closures=0,alias_gap_relaxations=0,alias_top_relaxations=0,alias_cost_relaxations=0;
    uint64_t random_state=1;
    void accumulate(const TemporalLNS& other) {
        alias_closures+=other.alias_closures;alias_gap_relaxations+=other.alias_gap_relaxations;
        alias_top_relaxations+=other.alias_top_relaxations;alias_cost_relaxations+=other.alias_cost_relaxations;
        attempts+=other.attempts;
        insertions+=other.insertions;
        accepted+=other.accepted;
        improvements+=other.improvements;
        shortcuts+=other.shortcuts;
        block_attempts+=other.block_attempts;
        block_accepted+=other.block_accepted;
        block_improvements+=other.block_improvements;
        uphill+=other.uphill;
        restarts+=other.restarts;
        flexible_attempts+=other.flexible_attempts;
        flexible_accepted+=other.flexible_accepted;
        flexible_improvements+=other.flexible_improvements;
        flexible_saved+=other.flexible_saved;
        reorder_attempts+=other.reorder_attempts;
        reorder_improvements+=other.reorder_improvements;
        reorder_saved+=other.reorder_saved;
        duplicates+=other.duplicates;
        paired_attempts+=other.paired_attempts;
        paired_accepted+=other.paired_accepted;
        paired_improvements+=other.paired_improvements;
        paired_saved+=other.paired_saved;
        backward_attempts+=other.backward_attempts;
        backward_improvements+=other.backward_improvements;
        backward_saved+=other.backward_saved;
        paired_seconds+=other.paired_seconds;
        block_seconds+=other.block_seconds;
        build_seconds+=other.build_seconds;
        full_builds+=other.full_builds;history_only_builds+=other.history_only_builds;packet_only_builds+=other.packet_only_builds;
        score_refreshes+=other.score_refreshes;stale_bank_selections+=other.stale_bank_selections;
        full_build_seconds+=other.full_build_seconds;history_only_seconds+=other.history_only_seconds;packet_only_seconds+=other.packet_only_seconds;
    }
    void optimize(vector<Move>& best,double slice_end,double search_start,double search_end) {
        const double slice_begin=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec();
        // 中断期間を候補自身の費用へ含めず、乱数も候補ごとに続きから使う。
        struct SliceScope {
            double& seconds;uint64_t& state;uint64_t previous;double begin;
            ~SliceScope(){seconds+=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()-begin;state=rng.x;rng.x=previous;}
        } scope{active_seconds,random_state,rng.x,slice_begin};
        rng.x=random_state;
        lns_start=search_start;
        auto rebuild=[&](){rebuild_all(current);};
        LOCAL_ONLY(trace.count_by("race_resumes",initialized));
        const auto selector_values=nn_selector_features();
        const bool use_nn=nn_selector_choice(selector_values);
        if(!initialized) {
            started_at=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec();
            current=smooth_routes(best);
            const size_t before_initial_reductions=current.size();
            search_reductions.cheap(current,min(slice_end,search_end));
            search_reductions.initial_saved+=before_initial_reductions-current.size();
            LOCAL_NOTE(trace.count_by("lns_initial_shortcut_saved", int(best.size()) - int(current.size()));)
            shortcuts+=int(best.size())-int(current.size());
            if(current.size()<best.size())best=current;
            // 最初の並べ替えは短くなった場合に採用し、同手数の別順序は後の探索で扱う。
            auto reordered=causal_reorder(current,0);++reorder_attempts;
            if(!same_moves(reordered,current)) {
                auto polished=smooth_routes(reordered);
                const size_t before_initial_reductions=polished.size();
                search_reductions.cheap(polished,min(slice_end,search_end));
                search_reductions.initial_reorder_saved+=before_initial_reductions-polished.size();
                if(polished.size()<current.size()) {
                    LOCAL_NOTE(trace.count_by("lns_initial_reorder_saved", int(current.size()) - int(polished.size()));)
                    ++reorder_improvements;reorder_saved+=int(current.size()-polished.size());
                    current=move(polished);if(current.size()<best.size())best=current;
                }
            }
            initialize_spatial_cuts();
            rebuild();initialized=true;
        }
        const double end=search_end;
        // 切り替え時の再加熱を避け、全候補を同じ全体進捗で冷却する。
        const double initial_progress=clamp((time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()-lns_start)/max(PROGRAM_TIME_LIMIT_SEC*(0.02/1.90),end-lns_start),0.0,1.0);
        double temperature=lns_start_temperature*pow(lns_end_temperature/lns_start_temperature,initial_progress);
        while(time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()<min(slice_end,end)) {
            time_keeper.check();++iteration;++stagnant;
            if(stagnant>max(100,board_info.M*3)&&current.size()>best.size()) {
                current=best;trajectory_changed();stagnant=0;++restarts;rebuild();
            }
            if(candidate_age>=candidate_refresh_interval)rebuild();
            if((iteration&31)==1) {
                double progress=clamp((time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()-lns_start)/max(LOCAL_SECONDS(0.02),end-lns_start),0.0,1.0);
                temperature=lns_start_temperature*pow(lns_end_temperature/lns_start_temperature,progress);
            }
            // 支持の重みは近傍の種類と独立に抽選する。
            int tie_mode=rng(12);
            support_weight=tie_mode==0?0:tie_mode==1?2:tie_mode==2?8:5;
            ride_weight=tie_mode==3?4:1;
            vector<Move> trial;
            bool reorder=iteration%113==0;
            bool paired=!reorder&&iteration%17==0&&
                        paired_seconds<0.11*max(PROGRAM_TIME_LIMIT_SEC*(0.03/1.90),active_seconds+time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()-slice_begin);
            bool backward=false;
            const bool packet_slot=!reorder&&!paired&&iteration%7==0&&
                        block_seconds<0.22*max(PROGRAM_TIME_LIMIT_SEC*(0.03/1.90),active_seconds+time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()-slice_begin);
            if(paired||packet_slot)ensure_packets(current);
            else if(!reorder)ensure_history(current);
            bool packet=packet_slot&&!packet_cuts.empty();
            bool flexible=packet&&(rng(3)!=0);
            // 指定した通常選択の間隔で、最大8匹の依存拡張へ割り当てる。
            bool dependency=!reorder&&!paired&&!packet&&++regular_selections%dependency_interval==0;
            LOCAL_NOTE(DependencyTimer dependency_timer(dependency);)
            bool ok=true;
            LOCAL_NOTE(int late_kind=-1;bool two_order=false;int repair_priority_broken=-1;)
            if(reorder) {
                ++attempts;++reorder_attempts;
                int style=rng(6);backward=style>=3;backward_attempts+=backward;
                trial=causal_reorder(current,style);
            }else if(paired) {
                ++attempts;++paired_attempts;double begin=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec();
                LOCAL_NOTE(const double previous_paired_seconds=paired_seconds;)
                ok=paired_neighbor(current,3,trial);
                paired_seconds+=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()-begin;
                LOCAL_NOTE(if(local_late_paired_anchor){
                    late_kind=2;local_late_start.completed[2]+=ok;
                    local_late_start.seconds[2]+=paired_seconds-previous_paired_seconds;
                })
            }else if(packet) {
                int choice=-1,cooldown=max(24,min(160,int(packet_cuts.size()/2)));
                uint64_t salt=flexible?0xa0761d6478bd642fULL:0;
                if(rng(4)==0)choice=rng(int(packet_cuts.size()));
                else for(int i=0;i<int(packet_cuts.size());i++) {
                    auto it=last_tried.find(packet_cuts[i].hash^salt);
                    if(it==last_tried.end()||iteration-it->second>cooldown){choice=i;break;}
                }
                if(choice<0)choice=rng(int(packet_cuts.size()));
                PacketCut cut=packet_cuts[choice];last_tried[cut.hash^salt]=iteration;
                ++attempts;++block_attempts;double begin=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec();
                LOCAL_NOTE(
                    const double previous_block_seconds=block_seconds;
                    if(cut.later){late_kind=flexible?1:0;++local_late_start.attempts[late_kind];}
                )
                if(flexible){++flexible_attempts;ok=flexible_neighbor(current,cut,3,trial);}
                else {ok=packet_neighbor(current,cut,3,trial);if(ok)++insertions;}
                block_seconds+=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()-begin;
                LOCAL_NOTE(if(late_kind>=0){
                    local_late_start.completed[late_kind]+=ok;
                    local_late_start.seconds[late_kind]+=block_seconds-previous_block_seconds;
                })
            }else {
                if(candidates.empty())break;
                int choice=-1,cooldown=max(24,min(200,int(candidates.size()*regular_cooldown_fraction)));
                int mode=rng(10);
                if(mode==0)choice=rng(int(candidates.size()));
                else if(mode<=2)choice=rng(min(100,int(candidates.size())));
                else for(int i=0;i<int(candidates.size());i++) {
                    auto it=last_tried.find(candidates[i].hash);
                    if(it==last_tried.end()||iteration-it->second>cooldown){choice=i;break;}
                }
                // 元の順位選択で適格候補がある場合だけ、同じ待ち条件でNNを使う。
                const bool nn_eligible=use_nn&&!dependency&&mode>=3&&choice>=0;
                if(choice<0)choice=rng(int(candidates.size()));
                if(nn_eligible){
                    const double progress=clamp((time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()-lns_start)/max(LOCAL_SECONDS(0.02),end-lns_start),0.0,1.0);
                    choice=nn_choose(current,best,stagnant,progress,cooldown);
                }
                refresh_regular_score(candidates[choice]);
                stale_bank_selections+=candidate_age>0;
                Removal cand=candidates[choice];last_tried[cand.hash]=iteration;
                LOCAL_NOTE(repair_priority_broken=cand.broken_support_jumps;trace.count_by("lns_repair_priority_selected",1);
                           trace.count_by("lns_repair_priority_selected_broken_jumps",cand.broken_support_jumps);)
                if(dependency) {
                    LOCAL_NOTE(++local_dependency.selected;const int seed_size=int(cand.ids.size());)
                    const int rounds=grow_dependency(cand);
                    if(!rounds)continue;
                    LOCAL_NOTE(
                        ++local_dependency.generated;local_dependency.multistep+=rounds>1;
                        local_dependency.added+=int(cand.ids.size())-seed_size;
                    )
                    auto previous=last_tried.find(cand.hash);
                    if(previous!=last_tried.end()&&iteration-previous->second<24) {
                        LOCAL_NOTE(++local_dependency.cooldown;)
                        continue;
                    }
                    last_tried[cand.hash]=iteration;
                    LOCAL_NOTE(++local_dependency.attempts;)
                }
                ++attempts;
                vector<Move> base;State initial;
                LOCAL_NOTE(local_dependency_extract=dependency;)
                const bool extracted=extract(current,cand.ids,base,initial);
                LOCAL_NOTE(local_dependency_extract=false;)
                if(!extracted) {
                    LOCAL_NOTE(local_dependency.extract_failed+=dependency;)
                    continue;
                }
                LOCAL_NOTE(if(dependency){
                    ++local_dependency.extracted;
                    local_dependency.gross_removed+=removed_commands(cand.ids);
                    local_dependency.net_removed+=int(current.size())-int(base.size());
                })
                vector<pair<double,int>> order;order.reserve(cand.ids.size());
                for(int id:cand.ids) {
                    double weight=board_info.floor_dist[id][board_info.nest_pos_by_code[board_info.initial[id]]];
                    weight*=0.6+0.8*rng.unit();order.emplace_back(-weight,id);
                }
                sort(order.begin(),order.end());
                if(order.size()>=2&&order.size()<=4) {
                    LOCAL_NOTE(two_order=true;)
                    vector<Move> rebuilt;
                    ok=insert_two_orders(initial,base,order,int(current.size())+4,rebuilt);
                    if(ok)base=move(rebuilt);
                    else {LOCAL_NOTE(local_dependency.insert_failed+=dependency;)}
                }else {
                    for(auto [unused,id]:order) {
                        time_keeper.check();vector<Move> next;
                        int cap=int(current.size())+4-int(base.size());
                        if(!insert(initial,base,id,cap,next)){
                            LOCAL_NOTE(local_dependency.insert_failed+=dependency;)
                            ok=false;break;
                        }
                        ++insertions;base=move(next);initial[id]=board_info.initial[id];
                    }
                }
                if(ok){trial=move(base);LOCAL_NOTE(local_dependency.completed+=dependency;)}
            }
            if(!ok)continue;
            LOCAL_NOTE(if(repair_priority_broken>=0){
                trace.count_by("lns_repair_priority_completed",1);
                trace.count_by("lns_repair_priority_penalized_completed",repair_priority_broken>0);
            })
            if(same_moves(trial,current)){++duplicates;continue;}
            vector<Move> polished;
            // 経路短縮は操作を再生し、変更区間の全盤面一致と最後の全帰巣を確認する。
            try{polished=smooth_routes(trial);}catch(const exception&){LOCAL_NOTE(trace.count_by("lns_invalid_candidates", 1);)continue;}
            shortcuts+=int(trial.size())-int(polished.size());
            LOCAL_NOTE(const size_t before_reductions=polished.size();const auto savings_before=search_reductions.saved;)
            search_reductions.candidate(polished,current,best.size(),iteration,lns_start,end);
            LOCAL_NOTE(
                ++search_reductions.candidates;search_reductions.shortened+=polished.size()<before_reductions;
                search_reductions.nonworse_created+=before_reductions>current.size()&&polished.size()<=current.size();
                search_reductions.best_created+=before_reductions>=best.size()&&polished.size()<best.size();
                auto probability=[&](size_t n){int d=int(n)-int(current.size());return d<=0?1.0:(d<=2&&n<=best.size()+4?exp(-d/temperature):0.0);};
                search_reductions.probability_changed+=probability(before_reductions)!=probability(polished.size());
            )
            if(same_moves(polished,current)){++duplicates;continue;}
            int delta=int(polished.size())-int(current.size());
            LOCAL_NOTE(if(dependency&&delta<0){
                ++local_dependency.current_improvements;local_dependency.current_saved-=delta;
            })
            LOCAL_NOTE(if(late_kind>=0&&delta<0){
                ++local_late_start.current_improvements[late_kind];
                local_late_start.current_saved[late_kind]-=delta;
            })
            bool accept=delta<=0||(delta<=2&&polished.size()<=best.size()+4&&rng.unit()<exp(-delta/temperature));
            if(accept) {
                LOCAL_NOTE(
                    search_reductions.accepted_shortened+=polished.size()<before_reductions;
                    for(int kind=0;kind<FINAL_KINDS;kind++)search_reductions.accepted_saved[kind]+=search_reductions.saved[kind]-savings_before[kind];
                )
                LOCAL_NOTE(if(repair_priority_broken>=0){
                    trace.count_by("lns_repair_priority_accepted",1);
                    trace.count_by("lns_repair_priority_penalized_accepted",repair_priority_broken>0);
                })
                ++accepted;uphill+=delta>0;block_accepted+=packet;flexible_accepted+=flexible;paired_accepted+=paired;
                LOCAL_NOTE(local_backward_accepted += backward;)
                LOCAL_NOTE(local_dependency.accepted+=dependency;)
                LOCAL_NOTE(if(two_order){
                    ++local_two_order.accepted;
                    local_two_order.alternate_accepted+=local_two_order_alternate;
                })
                LOCAL_NOTE(if(late_kind>=0)++local_late_start.accepted[late_kind];)
                if(polished.size()<best.size()) {
                    int saved=int(best.size()-polished.size());
                    LOCAL_NOTE(if(repair_priority_broken>=0){
                        trace.count_by("lns_repair_priority_improvements",1);
                        trace.count_by("lns_repair_priority_saved",saved);
                        trace.count_by("lns_repair_priority_penalized_saved",repair_priority_broken>0?saved:0);
                    })
                    LOCAL_NOTE(if(two_order){
                        ++local_two_order.improvements;local_two_order.saved+=saved;
                        if(local_two_order_alternate){
                            ++local_two_order.alternate_improvements;local_two_order.alternate_saved+=saved;
                        }
                    })
                    LOCAL_NOTE(if(dependency){++local_dependency.improvements;local_dependency.saved+=saved;})
                    LOCAL_NOTE(if(late_kind>=0){
                        ++local_late_start.improvements[late_kind];local_late_start.saved[late_kind]+=saved;
                    })
                    LOCAL_NOTE(
                        trace.count_by("lns_best_saved", saved);
                        trace.count_by("lns_regular_saved", !packet && !reorder && !paired ? saved : 0);
                        trace.count_by("lns_packet_saved", packet && !flexible ? saved : 0);
                    )
                    ++improvements;block_improvements+=packet;
                    flexible_improvements+=flexible;flexible_saved+=flexible?saved:0;
                    reorder_improvements+=reorder;reorder_saved+=reorder?saved:0;
                    paired_improvements+=paired;paired_saved+=paired?saved:0;
                    backward_improvements+=backward;backward_saved+=backward?saved:0;
                    best=polished;stagnant=0;
                }
                current=move(polished);trajectory_changed();
            }
        }
    }

};

#ifdef LOCAL
// 個体IDと可変長の塔で独立再生し、ビット表現と再挿入器から独立に検証する。
void validate_output(const vector<Move>& answer) {
    array<vector<int>, max_cells> tower;
    array<bool, max_cells> returned{};
    int E = board_info.M, long_jumps = 0, mixed_moves = 0, max_height = 1, max_packet = 0;
    for (int p = 0; p < board_info.cell_count; ++p) if (board_info.initial[p]) tower[p].push_back(p);
    assert(answer.size() <= 100000);
    for (const auto& op : answer) {
        assert(0 <= op.p && op.p < board_info.cell_count && op.d < 4);
        const int p = op.p, h = int(tower[p].size()), k = op.k, l = op.l;
        assert(0 <= k && k < h && 1 <= l && l <= k + 1);
        int q = -1;
        for (int step = 1; step <= l; ++step) {
            const int row = board_info.row[p] + board_info.di[op.d] * step;
            const int col = board_info.col[p] + board_info.dj[op.d] * step;
            assert(0 <= row && row < board_info.N && 0 <= col && col < board_info.N);
            q = board_info.cell_id[row][col];
            assert(q >= 0);
        }
        const int g = int(tower[q].size());
        assert(g + h - k <= 8);
        bool mixed = false;
        for (int j = k + 1; j < h; ++j)
            mixed |= board_info.initial[tower[p][j]] != board_info.initial[tower[p][k]];
        mixed_moves += mixed;
        long_jumps += l > 1;
        max_height = max(max_height, g + h - k);
        max_packet = max(max_packet, h - k);
        while (int(tower[p].size()) > k) {
            tower[q].push_back(tower[p].back());
            tower[p].pop_back();
        }
        for (int v : {p, q}) {
            while (!tower[v].empty() && board_info.initial[tower[v].back()] == TowerBits(board_info.nest_code[v])) {
                const int id = tower[v].back();
                assert(!returned[id]);
                returned[id] = true;
                tower[v].pop_back();
                --E;
            }
        }
    }
    for (int p = 0; p < board_info.cell_count; ++p) {
        assert(tower[p].empty());
        if (board_info.initial[p]) assert(returned[p]);
    }
    assert(E == 0);
    trace.count_by("T", answer.size());
    trace.count_by("E", E);
    trace.count_by("validated_moves", answer.size());
    trace.count_by("delivered_slimes", board_info.M);
    trace.count_by("output_long_jumps", long_jumps);
    trace.count_by("output_mixed_moves", mixed_moves);
    trace.count_by("output_max_height", max_height);
    trace.count_by("output_max_packet", max_packet);
}
#endif


// 構築直後には同手数でも、その後の短縮余地が異なり得るため別の操作列を残す。
struct NeuralPortfolioStats {
    int attempted=1,completed=1,incomplete=0,retained=1,winner=-1;
    int first_ops=0;uint64_t first_hash=0;
    double first_seconds=0,extra_seconds=0;
    array<int,5> origin{},raw{},pilot{},final{};
} neural_portfolio;

struct InitialSolutions {
    struct Entry { vector<Move> moves; int origin; };
    vector<Entry> entries;
    void offer(const vector<Move>& moves,int origin) {
        LOCAL_ONLY(trace.count_by("race_offered",1));
        for(const auto& entry:entries) {
            if(entry.moves.size()!=moves.size())continue;
            bool same=true;
            for(size_t i=0;i<moves.size();i++) {
                const auto a=entry.moves[i],b=moves[i];
                if(a.p!=b.p||a.k!=b.k||a.d!=b.d||a.l!=b.l){same=false;break;}
