            }
            if(same){LOCAL_ONLY(trace.count_by("race_duplicates",1));return;}
        }
        const auto at=upper_bound(entries.begin(),entries.end(),moves.size(),
            [](size_t size,const Entry& entry){return size<entry.moves.size();});
        if(at==entries.end()&&entries.size()==5)return;
        entries.insert(at,Entry{moves,origin});
        if(entries.size()>5)entries.pop_back();
        LOCAL_ONLY(trace.count_by("race_retained",1));
    }
    size_t construction_cap() const {
        // 最長候補と同じ手数まで完成させ、別の初期解を選抜へ渡せるようにする。
        return entries.back().moves.size()+1;
    }
};

void grow_initial_solutions(vector<Move>& best,InitialSolutions& pool,TemporalLNS& total) {
#ifdef LOCAL
    const double start=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec(),end=PROGRAM_TIME_LIMIT_SEC;
#else
    const double start=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec(),end=LOCAL_SECONDS(1.900);
#endif
    const double budget=max(0.0,end-start);
    const int count=int(pool.entries.size());
    neural_portfolio.retained=count;
    for(int i=0;i<count;++i){neural_portfolio.origin[i]=pool.entries[i].origin;neural_portfolio.raw[i]=int(pool.entries[i].moves.size());}
    if(count==0)throw logic_error("empty initial solution pool");
    vector<unique_ptr<TemporalLNS>> searches;
    vector<int> active(count);iota(active.begin(),active.end(),0);
    for(int id=0;id<count;id++) {
        searches.push_back(make_unique<TemporalLNS>());
        searches.back()->random_state=rng.next();
        LOCAL_ONLY(
            trace.count_by("race_seed_"+to_string(id)+"_initial_ops",pool.entries[id].moves.size());
            trace.count_by("race_seed_"+to_string(id)+"_origin",pool.entries[id].origin);
            trace.count_by("race_seed_"+to_string(id)+"_collection_plans",
                0);
        );
    }
    total.started_at=start;
    LOCAL_ONLY(trace.count_by("race_seed_count",count));
    auto grow=[&](int id,double until,[[maybe_unused]] int round) {
        LOCAL_NOTE(const double began=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec();const int previous_attempts=searches[id]->attempts;)
        auto record=[&]() {
            LOCAL_ONLY(
                const string prefix="race_round_"+to_string(round);
                trace.count_by(prefix+"_grown",1);
                trace.count_by(prefix+"_seed_"+to_string(id)+"_ops",pool.entries[id].moves.size());
                trace.count_by(prefix+"_seed_"+to_string(id)+"_attempts",searches[id]->attempts-previous_attempts);
                trace.add_time_ms(prefix,(time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()-began)*1000.0);
            );
        };
        try { searches[id]->optimize(pool.entries[id].moves,until,start,end); }
        catch(...) {
            if(pool.entries[id].moves.size()<best.size())best=pool.entries[id].moves;
            record();
            throw;
        }
        if(pool.entries[id].moves.size()<best.size())best=pool.entries[id].moves;
        record();
    };
    auto summarize=[&]() {
        for(int id=0;id<count;id++) {
            neural_portfolio.final[id]=int(pool.entries[id].moves.size());
            total.accumulate(*searches[id]);
            LOCAL_ONLY(trace.count_by("race_seed_"+to_string(id)+"_final_ops",pool.entries[id].moves.size()));
        }
    };
    try {
        // 最初の2段階に各15%を使い、最短候補の継続探索へ70%を残す。
        for(int round=0;round<2&&active.size()>1;round++) {
            const double round_end=start+budget*(0.15*(round+1));
            for(size_t pos=0;pos<active.size();pos++) {
                const double now=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec();
                const double until=now+max(0.0,round_end-now)/double(active.size()-pos);
                grow(active[pos],until,round);
            }
            sort(active.begin(),active.end(),[&](int a,int b) {
                const size_t left=pool.entries[a].moves.size(),right=pool.entries[b].moves.size();
                return left!=right?left<right:a<b;
            });
            active.resize(round==0?(active.size()+1)/2:1);
            LOCAL_ONLY(trace.count_by("race_round_"+to_string(round)+"_survivors",active.size()));
        }
        const int winner=active.front();
        neural_portfolio.winner=winner;
        for(int i=0;i<count;++i)neural_portfolio.pilot[i]=int(pool.entries[i].moves.size());
        LOCAL_ONLY(
            trace.count_by("race_winner_initial_rank",winner);
            trace.count_by("race_winner_changed",winner!=0);
            trace.count_by("race_selection_saved",int(pool.entries[0].moves.size())-int(pool.entries[winner].moves.size()));
        );
        grow(winner,end,2);
        // 終了後の処理にも、選ばれた探索の乱数列を引き継ぐ。
        rng.x=searches[winner]->random_state;
    } catch(...) {summarize();throw;}
    summarize();
}


// NNとLNSは同じ開始時刻を参照する。NNの準備と変換の費用も探索時間から引く。
struct NNSharedDeadline {
    double limit;
    bool step() const {return time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()<limit;}
};

// D4 transform: rotate clockwise by (sym&3)*90 degrees, then mirror columns.
// It changes the policy's view, never the problem's objective or color names.
static pair<int,int> neural_transform_cell(int i,int j,int N,int sym,bool inverse=false) {
    if(inverse) {
        if(sym&4)j=N-1-j;
        for(int n=0;n<(sym&3);++n){const int old=i;i=N-1-j;j=old;}
    } else {
        for(int n=0;n<(sym&3);++n){const int old=i;i=j;j=N-1-old;}
        if(sym&4)j=N-1-j;
    }
    return {i,j};
}
static nn::Input neural_transform_input(const nn::Input& input,int sym) {
    nn::Input transformed;transformed.N=input.N;transformed.K=input.K;
    transformed.C.assign(input.N,string(input.N,'#'));
    for(int i=0;i<input.N;++i)for(int j=0;j<input.N;++j) {
        const auto [r,c]=neural_transform_cell(i,j,input.N,sym);
        transformed.C[r][c]=input.C[i][j];
    }
    return transformed;
}
static vector<Move> neural_restore_path(const nn::Board& board,const nn::SearchResult& result,int sym) {
    vector<Move> moves;moves.reserve(result.path.size());State checked=board_info.initial;
    for(nn::Move m:result.path) {
        const auto [i,j]=board.coordinates[m.p()];
        const auto [r,c]=neural_transform_cell(i,j,board_info.N,sym,true);
        const auto [qi,qj]=board.coordinates[board.ray[m.p()][m.d()][m.l()-1]];
        const auto [qr,qc]=neural_transform_cell(qi,qj,board_info.N,sym,true);
        const int direction=qr<r?0:qr>r?1:qc<c?2:3;
        const Move converted{int16_t(board_info.cell_id[r][c]),uint8_t(m.k()),uint8_t(direction),uint8_t(m.l())};
        board_info.apply(checked,converted);moves.push_back(converted);
    }
    if(board_info.count(checked)!=result.E)throw logic_error("symmetry conversion mismatch");
    return moves;
}
static void neural_extra_starts(const nn::Input& input,InitialSolutions& pool,vector<Move>& best) {
#ifndef V311_SINGLE_SEED
    const double deadline=0.30*PROGRAM_TIME_LIMIT_SEC;
    double estimated=neural_portfolio.first_seconds;
    for(int sym:{4,1,5}) {
        const double now=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec();
        if(now+1.35*estimated>=deadline)break;
        ++neural_portfolio.attempted;
        const auto transformed=neural_transform_input(input,sym);
        const nn::Board board(transformed);const nn::BoardState initial(transformed,board);
        NNSharedDeadline timer{deadline};
        const auto result=nn::search(transformed,board,initial,timer);
        estimated=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()-now;
        neural_portfolio.extra_seconds+=estimated;
        auto moves=neural_restore_path(board,result,sym);
        // This is an anytime portfolio: incomplete proposals are recorded and
        // not confused with completed seeds. The completed incumbent is valid.
        if(result.E){++neural_portfolio.incomplete;continue;}
        ++neural_portfolio.completed;
        pool.offer(moves,-3110-sym);
        if(moves.size()<best.size())best=move(moves);
    }
#else
    (void)input;(void)pool;(void)best;
#endif
}

int main() {
#ifdef LOCAL
    time_keeper.start_=chrono::steady_clock::now();
    for(const string key:{"finish_singles_calls","baseline_recovery","final_recovery",
        "construction_errors","lns_errors","construction_deadlines","lns_deadlines",
        "lns_initial_shortcut_saved","lns_initial_reorder_saved","lns_best_saved",
        "lns_regular_saved","lns_packet_saved","lns_invalid_candidates"})trace.count_by(key,0);
#endif
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);cin.tie(nullptr);
    board_info.read();state_pool.prepare(board_info.cell_count);
    for(int p=0;p<board_info.cell_count;p++)
        rng.x=(rng.x^(board_info.initial[p]+17*board_info.nest_code[p]+p))*0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
    nn::load_nn();nn::Input input;input.N=board_info.N;input.K=board_info.K;input.C.resize(input.N);
    for(int i=0;i<input.N;i++)input.C[i]=board_info.C[i];
    const nn::Board board(input);const nn::BoardState initial(input,board);
#ifdef LOCAL
    NNSharedDeadline timer{PROGRAM_TIME_LIMIT_SEC};
#else
    NNSharedDeadline timer{LOCAL_SECONDS(1.900)};
#endif
    const double first_nn_start=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec();
    const auto result=nn::search(input,board,initial,timer);
    neural_portfolio.first_seconds=time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()-first_nn_start;
    vector<Move> best;best.reserve(result.path.size());
    uint64_t hash=1469598103934665603ULL;
    {
        State checked=board_info.initial;
        for(nn::Move m:result.path){
            const auto [i,j]=board.coordinates[m.p()];
            Move converted{int16_t(board_info.cell_id[i][j]),uint8_t(m.k()),uint8_t(m.d()),uint8_t(m.l())};
            board_info.apply(checked,converted);best.push_back(converted);
            const uint64_t code=(((uint64_t(i*20+j)*8+m.k())*4+m.d())*8+m.l()-1);
            hash=(hash^code)*1099511628211ULL;
        }
        if(board_info.count(checked)!=result.E)throw logic_error("NN operation conversion mismatch");
    }
    LOCAL_ONLY(
        trace.count_by("initial_E",initial.E);trace.count_by("nn_initial_ops",best.size());
        trace.count_by("nn_initial_hash",hash&0x7fffffffffffffffULL);
        trace.count_by("nn_complete",result.E==0);trace.count_by("inferences",result.inferences);
        trace.count_by("depth",result.depth);trace.count_by("deadline",result.deadline);
        trace.count_by("duplicates",result.duplicates);trace.count_by("exhausted",result.exhausted);
        trace.add_time_ms("nn_initial",time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()*1000.0);
    );
    // NNの未完了はそのまま記録する。完成解を前提とするLNSには渡さない。
    if(result.E!=0){
        for(auto m:best)cout<<board_info.row[m.p]<<' '<<board_info.col[m.p]<<' '<<int(m.k)<<' '
            <<board_info.dirs[m.d]<<' '<<int(m.l)<<'\n';
        LOCAL_ONLY(trace.count_by("E",result.E);trace.count_by("T",best.size());trace.count_by("cnn_calls",nn::incremental_stats.calls);
        trace.count_by("cnn_full",nn::incremental_stats.full);
        trace.count_by("cnn_home",nn::incremental_stats.home);
        trace.count_by("cnn_incremental",nn::incremental_stats.incremental);
        trace.count_by("cnn_full_cells_per_layer",nn::incremental_stats.full_cells);
        for(int b=0;b<=nn::NN_DEPTH;++b)trace.count_by("cnn_cells_"+to_string(b),nn::incremental_stats.cells[b]);
        trace.summary(););
        return 0;
    }
    const size_t baseline=best.size();
    neural_portfolio.first_ops=int(best.size());neural_portfolio.first_hash=hash;
    InitialSolutions initial_solutions;initial_solutions.offer(best,-105);
    neural_extra_starts(input,initial_solutions,best);
    const int completed=neural_portfolio.completed,attempts=neural_portfolio.attempted;
    size_t before_lns=best.size();
#ifdef LOCAL
    trace.add_time_ms("construction", time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec() * 1000.0);
    const auto local_lns_started = chrono::steady_clock::now();
#endif
    time_keeper.time_limit_sec=LOCAL_SECONDS(1.930);
    LOCAL_ONLY(trace.count_by("selector_nn_enabled",nn_selector_choice(nn_selector_features()));trace.count_by("selector_M",board_info.M));
    TemporalLNS lns;
    try {grow_initial_solutions(best,initial_solutions,lns);}
    catch(const Deadline&){LOCAL_NOTE(trace.count_by("lns_deadlines", 1);)}
    catch(const exception& e){LOCAL_NOTE(trace.count_by("lns_errors", 1);)cerr<<"LNS diagnostic: "<<e.what()<<'\n';}
#ifdef LOCAL
    trace.add_time_ms("temporal_lns", chrono::duration<double, milli>(chrono::steady_clock::now() - local_lns_started).count());
#endif
    // 線形走査の転送圧縮を行い、探索の短縮と分けて集計する。
    const size_t pre_pair_ops=best.size();
    best=LOCAL_TIME(trace,"pair_transfer_compression",[&]{return compress_pair_transfers(best);});
    // 残った終了処理の時間で共同短縮する。探索途中で期限に達した区間は置換しない。
    const size_t pre_joint_ops=best.size();
    JointWindowStats joint_stats;
    best=LOCAL_TIME(trace,"joint_window",[&]{return compress_joint_windows(best,time_keeper.time_limit_sec,joint_stats);});
    joint_stats.summary();
    LOCAL_NOTE(trace.count_by("pre_joint_ops",pre_joint_ops);)
    [[maybe_unused]] const size_t pre_final_reductions_ops=best.size();
    FinalReductionStats final_reduction_stats;
    LOCAL_TIME(trace,"final_reductions",[&]{polish_final_reductions(best,final_reduction_stats);});
    final_reduction_stats.summary();
    LOCAL_NOTE(trace.count_by("pre_final_reductions_ops",pre_final_reductions_ops);)
    {
        State check=board_info.initial;
        for(auto m:best)board_info.apply(check,m);
        if(board_info.count(check)||best.size()>100000)throw logic_error("final answer check");
    }

    cerr<<"v311_nn_first_ops="<<neural_portfolio.first_ops
        <<" v311_nn_first_hash="<<neural_portfolio.first_hash
        <<" v311_nn_first_seconds="<<neural_portfolio.first_seconds
        <<" v311_nn_extra_seconds="<<neural_portfolio.extra_seconds
        <<" v311_nn_attempted="<<neural_portfolio.attempted
        <<" v311_nn_completed="<<neural_portfolio.completed
        <<" v311_nn_incomplete="<<neural_portfolio.incomplete
        <<" v311_nn_retained="<<neural_portfolio.retained
        <<" v311_winner="<<neural_portfolio.winner;
    for(int i=0;i<neural_portfolio.retained;++i)
        cerr<<" v311_seed_"<<i<<"_origin="<<neural_portfolio.origin[i]
            <<" v311_seed_"<<i<<"_raw="<<neural_portfolio.raw[i]
            <<" v311_seed_"<<i<<"_pilot="<<neural_portfolio.pilot[i]
            <<" v311_seed_"<<i<<"_final="<<neural_portfolio.final[i];
    cerr<<'\n';
    cerr<<"baseline="<<baseline<<" best="<<best.size()<<" pre_lns="<<before_lns<<" pre_pair="<<pre_pair_ops<<" pre_joint="<<pre_joint_ops
        <<" completed="<<completed<<" attempts="<<attempts<<" dp_words="<<0
        <<" lns_attempts="<<lns.attempts<<" insertions="<<lns.insertions
        <<" accepted="<<lns.accepted<<" improvements="<<lns.improvements<<" shortcuts="<<lns.shortcuts
        <<" block_attempts="<<lns.block_attempts<<" block_accepted="<<lns.block_accepted
        <<" block_improvements="<<lns.block_improvements<<" uphill="<<lns.uphill<<" restarts="<<lns.restarts
        <<" flexible_attempts="<<lns.flexible_attempts<<" flexible_accepted="<<lns.flexible_accepted
        <<" flexible_improvements="<<lns.flexible_improvements<<" flexible_saved="<<lns.flexible_saved
        <<" reorder_attempts="<<lns.reorder_attempts<<" reorder_improvements="<<lns.reorder_improvements
        <<" reorder_saved="<<lns.reorder_saved<<" duplicates="<<lns.duplicates
        <<" build_seconds="<<lns.build_seconds<<" lns_start="<<lns.started_at
        <<" full_builds="<<lns.full_builds<<" history_only_builds="<<lns.history_only_builds<<" packet_only_builds="<<lns.packet_only_builds
        <<" score_refreshes="<<lns.score_refreshes<<" stale_bank_selections="<<lns.stale_bank_selections
        <<" full_build_seconds="<<lns.full_build_seconds<<" history_only_seconds="<<lns.history_only_seconds<<" packet_only_seconds="<<lns.packet_only_seconds
        <<" alias_closures="<<lns.alias_closures<<" alias_gap_relaxations="<<lns.alias_gap_relaxations
        <<" alias_top_relaxations="<<lns.alias_top_relaxations<<" alias_cost_relaxations="<<lns.alias_cost_relaxations
        <<" block_seconds="<<lns.block_seconds
        <<" paired_attempts="<<lns.paired_attempts<<" paired_accepted="<<lns.paired_accepted
        <<" paired_improvements="<<lns.paired_improvements<<" paired_saved="<<lns.paired_saved
        <<" paired_seconds="<<lns.paired_seconds
        <<" backward_attempts="<<lns.backward_attempts<<" backward_improvements="<<lns.backward_improvements
        <<" backward_saved="<<lns.backward_saved
        <<" elapsed="<<fixed<<setprecision(6)<<time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec()<<'\n';
#ifdef LOCAL
    LOCAL_ONLY(
        LOCAL_TIME(trace, "validate", [&] { validate_output(best); });
        trace.count_by("floor_cells", board_info.cell_count);
        trace.count_by("state_payload_bytes", state_pool.bytes);
        trace.count_by("state_handle_bytes", sizeof(State));
        trace.count_by("state_slots", StatePool::slots);
        trace.count_by("state_pool_bytes", state_pool.bytes * StatePool::slots);
        trace.count_by("state_pool_free_at_end", state_pool.free_count);
        trace.count_by("baseline_ops", baseline);
        trace.count_by("pre_lns_ops", before_lns);
        trace.count_by("pre_pair_ops", pre_pair_ops);
        trace.count_by("final_ops", best.size());
        trace.count_by("construction_completed", completed);
        trace.count_by("construction_attempts", attempts);
        trace.count_by("dp_words", 0);
        trace.count_by("lns_attempts", lns.attempts);
        trace.count_by("lns_insertions", lns.insertions);
        trace.count_by("lns_accepted", lns.accepted);
        trace.count_by("lns_improvements", lns.improvements);
        trace.count_by("lns_candidate_shortcuts", lns.shortcuts);
        trace.count_by("lns_saved", int(before_lns) - int(pre_pair_ops));
        trace.add_time_ms("search_limit", LOCAL_SECONDS(1.930) * 1000.0);
        trace.add_time_ms("total", time_keeper.exact_elapsed_sec() * 1000.0);
        trace.count_by("lns_block_attempts", lns.block_attempts);
        trace.count_by("lns_block_accepted", lns.block_accepted);
        trace.count_by("lns_block_improvements", lns.block_improvements);
        trace.count_by("lns_flexible_attempts", lns.flexible_attempts);
        trace.count_by("lns_flexible_accepted", lns.flexible_accepted);
        trace.count_by("lns_flexible_improvements", lns.flexible_improvements);
        trace.count_by("lns_flexible_saved", lns.flexible_saved);
        trace.count_by("lns_reorder_attempts", lns.reorder_attempts);
        trace.count_by("lns_reorder_improvements", lns.reorder_improvements);
        trace.count_by("lns_reorder_saved", lns.reorder_saved);
        trace.count_by("lns_uphill", lns.uphill);
        trace.count_by("lns_restarts", lns.restarts);
        trace.count_by("lns_duplicates", lns.duplicates);
        trace.count_by("event_layers", local_event_layers);
        trace.count_by("event_reopens", local_event_reopens);
        trace.count_by("single_insert_calls", local_single_insert_calls);
        trace.count_by("packet_insert_calls", local_packet_insert_calls);
        trace.count_by("local_run_flexible_calls",local_run_flexible_calls);
        trace.count_by("local_run_paired_calls",local_run_paired_calls);
        trace.count_by("local_run_reinsert_calls",local_run_reinsert_calls);
        trace.count_by("local_run_reinserted_slimes",local_run_reinserted_slimes);
        trace.count_by("local_run_multi_calls",local_run_multi_calls);
        trace.count_by("local_run_multi_completed",local_run_multi_completed);
        trace.count_by("local_run_completed",local_run_completed);
        trace.count_by("local_run_completed_slimes",local_run_completed_slimes);
        trace.add_time_ms("lns_candidate_build", lns.build_seconds * 1000.0);
        trace.add_time_ms("lns_block_search", lns.block_seconds * 1000.0);
        trace.add_time_ms("lns_start", lns.started_at * 1000.0);
        trace.count_by("lns_paired_attempts", lns.paired_attempts);
        trace.count_by("lns_paired_accepted", lns.paired_accepted);
        trace.count_by("lns_paired_improvements", lns.paired_improvements);
        trace.count_by("lns_paired_saved", lns.paired_saved);
        trace.count_by("lns_backward_attempts", lns.backward_attempts);
        trace.count_by("lns_backward_improvements", lns.backward_improvements);
        trace.count_by("lns_backward_saved", lns.backward_saved);
        trace.count_by("lns_backward_accepted", local_backward_accepted);
        trace.count_by("shortcut_support_states", local_shortcut_support_states);
        trace.add_time_ms("lns_paired_search", lns.paired_seconds * 1000.0);
        local_dependency.summary();local_late_start.summary();local_two_order.summary();search_reductions.summary();
        local_nn.summary();trace.count_by("E",0);trace.count_by("T",best.size());trace.count_by("cnn_calls",nn::incremental_stats.calls);
        trace.count_by("cnn_full",nn::incremental_stats.full);
        trace.count_by("cnn_home",nn::incremental_stats.home);
        trace.count_by("cnn_incremental",nn::incremental_stats.incremental);
        trace.count_by("cnn_full_cells_per_layer",nn::incremental_stats.full_cells);
        for(int b=0;b<=nn::NN_DEPTH;++b)trace.count_by("cnn_cells_"+to_string(b),nn::incremental_stats.cells[b]);
        trace.summary();
    );
#endif
    for(auto m:best)cout<<board_info.row[m.p]<<' '<<board_info.col[m.p]<<' '<<int(m.k)<<' '
                         <<board_info.dirs[m.d]<<' '<<int(m.l)<<'\n';
}
